# 六爻 · 事业 · 国朝

*Liu Yao (six-line coin divination) · Career and office · The court and the state: ruler, ministers, the realm*

66 条。原文逐字核对过 `functions/_lib/doctrine/books/` 下的文本,行号是那份整理本的行号,不是原刻本的页码。「规则(整理)」是本库的归纳,引用时引原文。

## LY.CAREER.EL.01 朝廷国事总纲：以世爻为主、应象为佐，六合子旺为吉、六冲杀动为凶；国泰民安全资文武匡襄；欲知宗社安危须审易爻而较量

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占朝廷国事，以世爻为主，应爻为辅；六合、子孙旺相的是吉，六冲、凶煞发动的是凶。国家太平、百姓安宁，靠文臣武将的匡正辅佐；想知道宗庙社稷的安危，须要审察易爻来衡量。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining the affairs of the court and state, take the Self as the chief and the Other image as the aid; a six-combine and a thriving Output are auspicious, a six-clash and a moving baleful spirit are ominous. A state at peace and a people secure depend on the correction and aid of civil and military ministers; to know the safety or danger of the ancestral temple and altars, one must examine the lines of the Yi and weigh them.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 280 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 280*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以世爻为主，应象为佐，六合子旺为吉，六冲杀动为凶。 | 以世爻为主，应象为佐，六合、子孙旺为吉，六冲、杀动为凶。 | Take the Self as the chief and the Other image as the aid; a six-combine and a thriving Output are auspicious, a six-clash and a moving baleful spirit are ominous. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 281 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 281*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 国泰民安，共际唐虞之化育， | 国家太平、百姓安宁，正逢唐尧虞舜那样的教化； | The state at peace and the people secure, meeting the transforming influence of Tang and Yu; |
| 河清海晏，全资文武之匡襄。 | 黄河水清、海面平静，全靠文臣武将的匡正辅佐。 | the Yellow River clear and the sea calm, all depend on the aid and correction of civil and military ministers. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 282 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 282*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 云国之宁靖，民之太平，幸生尧舜之世，沾其化育。 | 说的是国家安宁、百姓太平，有幸生在尧舜那样的时代，沾受他们的教化。 | It means that the state is calm and the people at peace, and that one is fortunate to be born in the age of Yao and Shun and to share in their transforming influence. |
| 而黄河澄清，海波宁息，皆藉文臣武将之力匡扶而致冶也。 | 而黄河水清、海浪平息，都是借文臣武将的力量匡正扶持，才达到治理。 | And that the Yellow River runs clear and the waves of the sea are still is all through the strength of civil and military ministers who correct and support, and so bring about good government. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 283 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 283*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲知宗社之安危，须审易爻而较量。 | 想知道宗庙社稷的安危，须要审察易爻来衡量。 | To know the safety or danger of the ancestral temple and altars, one must examine the lines of the Yi and weigh them. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 284 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 284*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 宗社乃宗庙社稷六朝延治则安，乱则危，审析易理爻辞，而可剖决吉凶也。 | 宗社就是宗庙社稷：国家（历朝）延续得治理好就安稳，混乱就危险；审察剖析易理爻辞，就能判断吉凶。 | 'Zongshe' is the ancestral temple and altars of grain and soil: if the dynasty continues well governed it is secure, and if in disorder it is in danger; by examining and analysing the principles and line texts of the Yi, one can decide good and ill. |

> **按**:小标是这一章的取法：世爻为主、应爻为辅。第二、三句的「冶」应是「治」（第二条解里写作「而致冶也」），译文按「治」译。「宗社乃宗庙社稷六朝延治则安」的「六朝」文意不顺，疑是「历朝」之误，译文按「历朝」译。「六合子旺」「六冲杀动」里的六合、六冲指卦的六合、六冲（见术语表）。
>
> **Note:** The subtitle gives the method of the chapter: the Self as chief, the Other as aid. In the explanation the 'ye' of 'zhi zhi ye' is presumably 'zhi', to govern, and the translation reads 'govern'. 'Liu chao yan zhi ze an' in the explanation of 'zongshe' does not run smoothly, and 'liu chao' is presumably a miswriting of 'li chao', successive dynasties, and the translation reads it so. 'Six-combine' and 'six-clash' in the subtitle are the hexagram patterns (see the glossary).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.02、LY.CAREER.YY.31、LY.CAREER.HJC.63、LY.CAREER.HJC.64

## LY.CAREER.EL.02 朝廷国事：亲宫为邦国大忌空亡、本卦作朝堂最宜旺相；本卦是所占之卦名、亲宫是本宫之卦，皆宜旺相兴隆，大忌空亡墓绝

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:亲宫（本宫）当作邦国，最忌空亡；本卦当作朝堂，最适宜旺相。本卦是所占得的那一卦的卦名，亲宫是本宫之卦，所以都当作邦国、朝堂，都适宜旺相兴隆，最忌空亡、墓、绝。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The home palace is taken as the state, and Void is most to be avoided; the original hexagram is taken as the court hall, and thriving is best. The original hexagram is the name of the hexagram cast, and the home palace is the hexagram of the palace to which it belongs; so both are taken as the state and the court, both should be thriving and flourishing, and Void, tomb and extinction are most to be avoided.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 285 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 285*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 亲宫为邦国，大忌空亡； | 亲宫（本宫）当作邦国，最忌空亡； | The home palace is taken as the state, and Void is most to be avoided; |
| 本卦作朝堂，最宜旺相。 | 本卦当作朝堂，最适宜旺相。 | the original hexagram is taken as the court hall, and thriving is best. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 286 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 286*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本卦者，乃所占之卦名，亲宫者，乃本宫之卦也，故为邦国朝堂，皆宜旺相兴隆； | 本卦是所占得的那一卦的卦名，亲宫是本宫之卦，所以（它们）当作邦国、朝堂，都适宜旺相兴隆； | The original hexagram is the name of the hexagram cast, and the home palace is the hexagram of the palace to which it belongs; so they are taken as the state and the court, and both should be thriving and flourishing; |
| 大忌空亡墓绝。 | 最忌空亡、墓、绝。 | Void, tomb and extinction are most to be avoided. |

> **按**:「亲宫」书里的解释是「本宫之卦」，即占得的卦所属的那一宫（八宫之一）；这一章里的「亲宫」「本宫」是同一个意思。「旺相」是这一宫（的五行）当令得气。与易隐朝廷占「世爻所居之卦为国」（YY.32）是两种取法：易隐取世爻所在的那一个单卦，这里取整个本宫和整个本卦。
>
> **Note:** 'Qingong' is explained in the book as 'the hexagram of the home palace', that is the palace of the eight to which the hexagram cast belongs; in this chapter 'qingong' and 'home palace' mean the same. 'Thriving' means that the element of this palace is in season and has qi. This is a different way of taking it from the 'trigram in which the Self sits is the state' of the Yi Yin court divination (YY.32): the Yi Yin takes the one trigram holding the Self, and this takes the whole home palace and the whole original hexagram.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.01、LY.CAREER.EL.03、LY.CAREER.YY.32

## LY.CAREER.EL.03 朝廷国事：初爻属黎元吉曜当权培国本，二爻为士子文昌得令焕文章

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:初爻是百姓，吉星当权就能培固国家的根本；二爻是士人，文昌得令，文章就焕发光彩。初爻的百姓是国家的根本，喜欢临吉星；二爻的士人属文明，喜欢值文昌。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The first line is the common people; if an auspicious star holds power, the root of the state is nurtured. The second line is the scholars; if Wenchang is in season, their writings shine. The people of the first line are the root of the state and like to sit on an auspicious star; the scholars of the second line belong to civilization and like to meet Wenchang.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 288 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 288*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 初属黎元，吉曜当权培国本； | 初爻属于百姓，吉星当权，就能培固国家的根本； | The first line belongs to the common people; if an auspicious star holds power, it nurtures the root of the state; |
| 二为士子，文昌得令焕文章。 | 二爻是士人，文昌得令，文章就焕发光彩。 | the second line is the scholars; if Wenchang is in season, their writings shine. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 289 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 289*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 初民为邦本，欣临吉曜，二士属文明，喜值文昌。 | 初爻的百姓是国家的根本，喜欢临吉星；二爻的士人属文明，喜欢值文昌。 | The common people of the first line are the root of the state and like to sit on an auspicious star; the scholars of the second line belong to civilization and like to meet Wenchang. |

> **按**:初爻为民、二爻为士，与易隐朝廷占的分配（初民、二士，YY.31）相同。文昌是一颗主文运的神煞。
>
> **Note:** The first line as the people and the second as the scholars is the same assignment as in the Yi Yin court divination (the first the people, the second the scholars, YY.31). Wenchang is a spirit-star of literary fortune.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.02、LY.CAREER.EL.04、LY.CAREER.YY.31、LY.CAREER.YY.43

## LY.CAREER.EL.04 朝廷国事：喜聚三垣——天喜值二爻则守令贤能如龚遂；贵临四位——贵人星临四爻则卿相公侯政绩过郭子仪

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:喜神聚在三垣，郡守县令的贤能可比渤海的龚遂：喜，就是天喜，如果值在二爻，郡守、县令的贤能就和汉代的龚遂一样，龚遂做渤海太守时，教化百姓卖掉刀剑去买耕牛。贵人临在四爻，公侯的功绩超过汾阳王：贵，就是贵人星，如果临在四爻，卿相公侯的政绩就超过唐代的郭子仪，他以文武全才辅佐肃宗收复西京，立下中兴的伟绩，受封汾阳王。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Joy gathers at the Three Enclosures, the worth of prefects and magistrates equals Gong Sui of Bohai: 'Joy' is the Heavenly Joy, and if it sits on the second line the ability of prefects and magistrates is like that of Gong Sui of Han, who as prefect of Bohai taught the people to sell their swords and buy oxen. If the Noble sits on the fourth line, the achievements of dukes and marquises surpass the Prince of Fenyang: 'the Noble' is the Noble star, and if it sits on the fourth line the governing record of ministers and marquises exceeds that of Guo Ziyi of the Tang, who with his gifts in both letters and arms helped Emperor Suzong recover the western capital, achieved the great work of the restoration, and was enfeoffed Prince of Fenyang.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 290 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 290*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 喜聚三垣，守令贤齐于渤海。 | 喜神聚在三垣，郡守县令的贤能可比渤海（的龚遂）。 | If Joy gathers at the Three Enclosures, the worth of prefects and magistrates equals that of Gong Sui of Bohai. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 291 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 291*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 喜者，天喜是也，若值二爻，则固守邑令贤能同于汉之龚遂也。 | 喜，就是天喜；如果值在二爻，郡守、县令的贤能就和汉代的龚遂一样。 | 'Joy' is the Heavenly Joy; if it sits on the second line, the ability of prefects and magistrates is like that of Gong Sui of Han. |
| 昔龚为渤海太守，化民卖刀买牛故云。 | 从前龚遂做渤海太守，教化百姓卖掉刀剑去买耕牛，所以这样说。 | Formerly Gong Sui as prefect of Bohai taught the people to sell their swords and buy oxen, and that is why it is said so. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 292 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 292*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 贵临四位，公侯绩迈于汾阳。 | 贵人临在四爻，公侯的功绩超过汾阳（郭子仪）。 | If the Noble sits on the fourth line, the achievements of dukes and marquises surpass the Prince of Fenyang (Guo Ziyi). |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 293 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 293*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 贵者，贵人星也，若临四爻，则卿相公侯之政绩过子唐之郭令公子仪，郭以文武全才辅佐肃宗克复西京，中兴伟绩，赐爵汾阳王故云。 | 贵，就是贵人星；如果临在四爻，卿相公侯的政绩就超过唐代的郭令公子仪；郭子仪以文武全才辅佐肃宗收复西京，立下中兴的伟绩，受封汾阳王，所以这样说。 | 'The Noble' is the Noble star; if it sits on the fourth line, the governing record of ministers and marquises exceeds that of the Tang Duke Guo Ziyi, who with his gifts in both letters and arms helped Emperor Suzong recover the western capital, achieved the great work of the restoration, and was enfeoffed Prince of Fenyang, and that is why it is said so. |

> **按**:「三垣」是紫微垣、太微垣、天市垣三个星区；这一联只说「喜聚三垣」，解里只解释了喜是天喜、值二爻，没有再解释三垣，译文照字面。「固守邑令」疑是「郡守邑令」之误，译文按「郡守、县令」译；「过子唐之郭令公子仪」疑是「过于唐之郭令公子仪」，译文按此译。龚遂是汉宣帝时的渤海太守，郭子仪是唐代名将、封汾阳王，这两个典故书里自己写了出来。
>
> **Note:** 'The Three Enclosures' are the three star regions Ziwei, Taiwei and Tianshi; the couplet says only 'Joy gathers at the Three Enclosures', and the explanation only explains that Joy is Heavenly Joy sitting on the second line, and does not explain the Three Enclosures further; the translation follows the letter. 'Gu shou yi ling' is presumably a miswriting of 'jun shou yi ling', and the translation reads 'prefects and magistrates'; 'guo zi Tang zhi Guo ling gong Ziyi' is presumably 'guo yu Tang zhi Guo ling gong Ziyi', surpassing the Tang Duke Guo Ziyi, and is translated so. Gong Sui was prefect of Bohai under Emperor Xuan of the Han, and Guo Ziyi was a famous Tang general enfeoffed Prince of Fenyang; the book writes out both allusions itself.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.03、LY.CAREER.EL.05、LY.CAREER.HJC.65

## LY.CAREER.EL.05 朝廷国事：福德司权则君子进小人退（福德即子孙，用爻值之正人在位邪佞潜踪）；青龙当道则干戈偃息文教丕兴

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:福德（子孙）当权，君子被进用、小人退下：福德就是子孙，用爻值在子孙上，正直的人在位，邪佞的人隐藏踪迹。青龙当道，战事止息，文教大兴。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Fortune Virtue holds power, gentlemen advance and petty men retire: Fortune Virtue is Output, and if the Subject line sits on it, upright men are in office and the crafty and flattering hide their traces. If the Azure Dragon holds the road, arms are laid down and culture and teaching flourish.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 294 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 294*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 福德司权，君子进而小人退。 | 福德当权，君子进用、小人退下。 | If Fortune Virtue, that is Output, holds power, gentlemen advance and petty men retire. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 295 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 295*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 福德者，即子孙，用爻值之则正人在位，邪佞潜踪。 | 福德就是子孙，用爻值在它上面，正直的人在位，邪佞的人隐藏踪迹。 | Fortune Virtue is Output; if the Subject line sits on it, upright men are in office and the crafty and flattering hide their traces. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 296 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 296*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙当道，干戈偃息文教丕兴。 | 青龙当道，战事止息，文教大兴。 | If the Azure Dragon holds the road, arms are laid down and culture and teaching flourish. |

> **按**:「干戈偃息」是兵器收起、战事停止；「丕兴」是大兴。这一联后面书里没有解说。
>
> **Note:** 'Gan ge yan xi' is weapons put away and war stopped; 'pi xing' is flourishing greatly. The book gives no explanation after this couplet.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.04、LY.CAREER.EL.06、LY.CAREER.YY.39

## LY.CAREER.EL.06 朝廷国事：圣主躬占以世爻安帝座，臣民间卜以五爻为天子

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:君主自己占，用世爻来安放帝座；臣民代占，还是用五爻来定君王。当今皇帝亲自占的，以世爻安帝座；臣民推占的，仍以五爻为天子；各有各的道理，断然不会出错。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the sovereign divines for himself, set the throne by the Self line; when ministers and commoners divine on his behalf, fix the ruler by the fifth line. When the present emperor divines in person, the throne is set by the Self; when ministers and commoners divine for him, the fifth line is still taken as the Son of Heaven; each has its principle, and there will surely be no error.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 297 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 297*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 圣主躬占，当以世爻安帝座； | 圣明的君主亲自占，当以世爻安放帝座； | When the wise sovereign divines in person, the throne is set by the Self line; |
| 臣民间卜，还将五位定君王。 | 臣民代占，还是用五爻来定君王。 | when ministers and commoners divine on his behalf, the ruler is still fixed by the fifth line. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 298 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 298*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 云当今躬卜者，当以世爻而安帝座。 | 说的是当今亲自占卜的，当以世爻来安放帝座。 | It means that one who in the present age divines in person should set the throne by the Self line. |
| 臣庶推占，仍以五爻而为天子，各得具理断无外误。 | 臣民推占的，仍以五爻为天子，各有各的道理，断然不会出错。 | Ministers and commoners who divine on his behalf still take the fifth line as the Son of Heaven; each has its principle, and there will surely be no error. |

> **按**:这一条与易隐朝廷占总述（YY.31）的「主上自占以世爻为用，臣庶代卜以五爻为用」完全一致。「间卜」的「间」字义不明，疑是「闻」或「代」，译文按「代占」译。
>
> **Note:** This entry agrees exactly with the Yi Yin court overview (YY.31): 'when the sovereign divines himself, take the Self as the Subject; when ministers and commoners divine on his behalf, take the fifth line as the Subject'. The character 'jian' in 'jian bu' is unclear and is presumably 'wen' or 'dai', and the translation reads 'divine on his behalf'.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.05、LY.CAREER.EL.07、LY.CAREER.YY.31、LY.CAREER.HJC.64

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.07 朝廷国事：不临克害刑冲则升平四海、值生扶拱合则玉帛万方（用爻不犯克害刑冲决主四海承平万民乐业，卦内相生相合若拱若扶则殊方绝域皆来朝贡）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻不遇克、害、刑、冲，天下升平；遇到生、扶、拱、合，四方都来献玉帛。克害刑冲各有另章的起例；用爻不犯这些的，必定是四海承平、万民乐业；卦内相生相合、有拱有扶的，连远方绝域也都来朝贡。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Subject line does not meet control, harm, punishment or clash, the realm is in peace; if it meets generation, support, flanking or combination, all quarters bring jade and silk. The rules for control, harm, punishment and clash are set out in other chapters; if the Subject line is not hit by them, the four seas are at peace and the people content in their work; if within the hexagram there is generation and combination, flanking and support, even distant lands and remote regions come to pay tribute.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 299 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 299*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 不临克害刑冲，升平四海。 | 用爻不遇克、害、刑、冲，天下升平； | If the Subject line does not meet control, harm, punishment or clash, the realm is in peace; |
| 如值生扶拱合，玉帛万方。 | 如果遇到生、扶、拱、合，四方都来献玉帛。 | if it meets generation, support, flanking or combination, all quarters bring jade and silk. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 300 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 300*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 克害刑冲，别章各有起例，若用爻不犯者，决主四海承平，万民乐业。 | 克、害、刑、冲，别的章里各有起例；如果用爻没有犯这些，必定是四海承平、万民乐业。 | Control, harm, punishment and clash each have their rules in other chapters; if the Subject line is not hit by them, the four seas are surely at peace and the people content in their work. |
| 卦内相生相合，若拱若扶者，则殊方绝域皆来朝贡也。 | 卦内相生相合，有拱有扶的，远方绝域都来朝贡。 | If within the hexagram there is generation and combination, flanking and support, distant lands and remote regions all come to pay tribute. |

> **按**:「拱」是两个爻夹着另一个爻（拱夹）。「玉帛」是诸侯朝会的礼物。「用爻」是这一联里没有写出、解里补出的主语。
>
> **Note:** 'Gong', flanking, is two lines holding another between them. 'Jade and silk' are the gifts of the feudal lords at a court assembly. The subject, the Subject line, is not written in the couplet and is supplied by the explanation.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.06、LY.CAREER.EL.08、LY.CAREER.HJC.63

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.08 朝廷国事：宗庙管上爻安静则邦家巩固（比和安静则皇图如磐石）；岁星（太岁）关世旺相则国祚绵长

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:宗庙由上爻掌管：上爻安静，国家就稳固。宗庙专管社稷，得到比和、安静，国家的版图就像磐石一样安稳。岁星（太岁）关系到世爻、光芒旺相的，国祚就绵长：岁星就是太岁，关系到治乱，光芒旺相；如果这样，国祚就久远无疆。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The ancestral temple is governed by the top line: if it is still, the state is firm. The ancestral temple governs the altars of the state alone, and if it is in harmony and still, the realm is as secure as a rock. If the Year Star (Tai Sui) is linked with the Self and shines thriving, the dynasty's span is long: the Year Star is Tai Sui, it bears on order and disorder and shines thriving; if so, the dynasty lasts long without limit.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 301 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 301*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 宗庙管上爻，安静，则邦家巩固。 | 宗庙由上爻掌管，安静的话，国家就稳固。 | The ancestral temple is governed by the top line; if it is still, the state is firm. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 302 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 302*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 宗庙者，专主社稷，若得比和安静，则皇图巩盘石之安。 | 宗庙专管社稷，如果得到比和、安静，国家的版图就像磐石一样安稳。 | The ancestral temple governs the altars of the state alone, and if it is in harmony and still, the realm is as secure as a rock. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 303 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 303*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 岁星关世连，光芒则国祚绵长。 | 岁星关系到世爻，光芒旺相的话，国祚就绵长。 | If the Year Star is linked with the Self and shines thriving, the dynasty's span is long. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 304 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 304*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 岁星，即太岁也，关乎治乱光芒旺相也，若然值此，则国祚久远无疆也。 | 岁星就是太岁，关系到治乱，光芒旺相；如果值在这里，国祚就久远无疆。 | The Year Star is Tai Sui; it bears on order and disorder and shines thriving; if it sits here, the dynasty lasts long without limit. |

> **按**:「岁星关世连」字面不太顺，按解里的「关乎治乱」译成「关系到世爻」；「安静」是上爻不发动。「比和安静」见 YY.39 一类的「比和」用法。太岁是当年的地支（见术语表）。
>
> **Note:** 'Sui xing guan shi lian' does not read smoothly by the letter, and following the explanation's 'bears on order and disorder' it is translated as 'is linked with the Self'; 'still' means that the top line does not move. Tai Sui is the branch of the current year (see the glossary).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.07、LY.CAREER.EL.09、LY.CAREER.YY.31

## LY.CAREER.EL.09 朝廷国事：木位遇长生则前星焕彩（木爻属东方为青宫太子之象）；金官逢制陷则逆节潜藏（金爻属西方主兵革，鬼杀为叛逆之人，制服或随空亡墓绝则不久潜消）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:木爻遇到长生，前星（太子星）焕发光彩：木爻属东方，是青宫太子之象，前星也是太子之星，「焕彩」是说他得位，如果遇到长生又有气，他的德泽就广被而长久。金爻的官鬼逢到克制、陷落，就知道叛逆之人潜藏着：金爻属西方，主兵革之象，官鬼和凶煞是叛逆的人，如果被制服，或者随着空亡、墓、绝，不久就潜消。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Wood line meets Birth, the Front Star (the heir's star) shines: Wood belongs to the east and is the image of the Azure Palace and the heir apparent; the Front Star is also the star of the heir; 'shines' says that he has his place, and if it meets Birth and has qi, his virtue spreads widely and lasts. If a Metal Pressure line meets restraint or sinks, then know that rebels lie hidden: Metal belongs to the west and is the image of war; Pressure and baleful spirits are the rebels, and if they are subdued, or follow Void, tomb or extinction, they soon vanish in secret.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 305 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 305*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木位遇长生，会购买关星焕彩。 | 木爻遇到长生，前星（太子星）焕发光彩。 | If a Wood line meets Birth, the Front Star shines. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 306 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 306*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木爻属东方，为青宫太子之象，前星者，亦太子之星，焕彩，言其得位，如遇长生有气，则德被长生。 | 木爻属东方，是青宫太子之象，前星也是太子之星；「焕彩」是说他得位，如果遇到长生又有气，他的德泽就广被而长久。 | Wood belongs to the east and is the image of the Azure Palace and the heir apparent; the Front Star is also the star of the heir; 'shines' says that he has his place, and if it meets Birth and has qi, his virtue spreads widely and lasts. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 307 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 307*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金官逢制陷，乃知逆节潜藏。 | 金爻的官鬼逢到克制、陷落，就知道叛逆之人潜藏着。 | If a Metal Pressure line meets restraint or sinks, then know that rebels lie hidden. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 308 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 308*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金爻属西方，主兵革之象，鬼杀为叛逆之人，如有制服或随空亡墓绝，则不久潜消耳。 | 金爻属西方，主兵革之象，官鬼和凶煞是叛逆的人，如果被制服，或者随着空亡、墓、绝，不久就潜消。 | Metal belongs to the west and is the image of war; Pressure and baleful spirits are the rebels, and if they are subdued, or follow Void, tomb or extinction, they soon vanish in secret. |

> **按**:第一联的原文是「木位遇长生，会购买关星焕彩」，「会购买关」是传抄的错字，按解里「前星者，亦太子之星，焕彩」应是「前星焕彩」，译文按「前星焕彩」译。「德被长生」字面不顺，译作「德泽广被而长久」。「青宫」是太子的东宫（见 YY.38）。「逆节」是叛逆的行为。
>
> **Note:** The text of the first couplet reads 'mu wei yu changsheng, hui gou mai guan xing huan cai'; 'hui gou mai guan' is a miscopy, and following the explanation, 'the Front Star is also the star of the heir; shines', it should be 'qian xing huan cai', and the translation reads 'the Front Star shines'. 'De bei changsheng' does not run smoothly by the letter and is rendered 'his virtue spreads widely and lasts'. The 'Azure Palace' is the heir apparent's Eastern Palace (see YY.38). 'Nijie' is rebellious conduct.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.08、LY.CAREER.EL.10、LY.CAREER.YY.38、LY.CAREER.HJC.75

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.10 朝廷国事：粮饷论妻财休临败绝（妻财为粮饷，兴隆则国本克盈，衰败则京储匮乏）；城池看父母莫变刑伤（父母为城池，旺相则金汤之固，刑伤则有残缺之虞）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:粮饷看妻财，不要临败、绝：妻财是粮饷，兴旺则国家的根本充盈，衰败则京城的储备匮乏。城池看父母，不要变出刑伤：父母是城池，旺相则城池固若金汤，遇刑伤就有残缺的忧患。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Provisions are judged by Wealth, which must not sit on spent or extinct: Wealth is the grain and pay, and if it flourishes the root of the state is full, and if it is spent the capital's stores run short. The city walls are judged by Resource, which must not change into punishment or injury: Resource is the walls and moats, and if it is thriving they are as firm as walls of metal and moats of boiling water, and if punished or injured there is the worry of breaches.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 309 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 309*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 粮饷论妻财，休临败绝。 | 粮饷看妻财，不要临在败、绝。 | Provisions are judged by Wealth, which must not sit on spent or extinct. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 310 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 310*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 妻财为粮饷，兴隆则国本克盈，衰败则京储匮乏。 | 妻财是粮饷，兴旺则国家的根本充盈，衰败则京城的储备匮乏。 | Wealth is the grain and pay; if it flourishes the root of the state is full, and if it is spent the capital's stores run short. |

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 311 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 311*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 城池看父母，莫变刑伤。 | 城池看父母，不要变出刑伤。 | The city walls are judged by Resource, which must not change into punishment or injury. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 312 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 312*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母为城池旺相则有金汤之固，刑伤宁无残缺之虞。 | 父母是城池，旺相则城池固若金汤，遇到刑伤，岂能没有残缺的忧患。 | Resource is the walls and moats; if it is thriving they are as firm as walls of metal and moats of boiling water, and if it meets punishment or injury, how can there not be the worry of breaches. |

> **按**:「金汤」是金属的城、滚水的护城河，比喻极其坚固。这两联和易隐朝廷占的城池（YY.40）、帑藏（YY.41）取法相同：父母为城池，妻财为国库粮饷。
>
> **Note:** 'Jintang' are walls of metal and a moat of boiling water, an image of extreme firmness. These two couplets take the same view as the walls (YY.40) and treasury (YY.41) in the Yi Yin court divination: Resource is the city walls, and Wealth the state treasury and provisions.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.09、LY.CAREER.EL.11、LY.CAREER.YY.40、LY.CAREER.YY.41

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.11 朝廷国事结语：祸福攸关于大象，吉凶不离五行衰旺；筮者当察其衰旺、变通活泼、相机而断

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:祸福关系在大象，要变通地洞察细微；吉凶离不开五行，要剖析精研衰旺。人能推究事物、获得知识，报应比影子、回声还快。祸福吉凶所关，易的大象离不开卦的五行，千姿百态哪里能都说尽？筮者应当审察五行的衰旺，自然要变通灵活，相机判断，没有不灵验的，就像回声应着声音。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Fortune and misfortune turn on the great image, and one must adapt and perceive the faintest signs; good and ill do not leave the five elements, and one must analyse and study their strength and weakness. If a man can investigate things and gain knowledge, retribution is faster than shadow or echo. What fortune and misfortune turn on is that the great image of the Yi does not leave the five elements of the hexagram; of the thousand shapes and ten thousand states, how can all be told? The diviner should examine their strength and weakness and naturally adapt and be flexible, judging as the occasion offers, and there will be none that fail to prove true, as an echo answers a sound.

**《易林补遗·朝廷国事章第四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 314 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 4: Affairs of the court and state", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 314*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 祸福攸关于大象变通洞烛机微。 | 祸福关系在大象，要变通地洞察细微。 | Fortune and misfortune turn on the great image, and one must adapt and perceive the faintest signs. |
| 吉凶不离于五行剖精研衰旺。 | 吉凶离不开五行，要剖析精研衰旺。 | Good and ill do not leave the five elements, and one must analyse and study their strength and weakness. |
| 人能格物致知，报应捷于影响。 | 人能推究事物、获得知识，报应比影子、回声还快。 | If a man can investigate things and gain knowledge, retribution is faster than shadow or echo. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 314–315 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 314–315*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 祸福吉凶关系，易之大象不离卦之五行，千态万状焉能悉战？ | 祸福吉凶所关，易的大象离不开卦的五行，千姿百态哪里能都说尽？ | What fortune and misfortune turn on is that the great image of the Yi does not leave the five elements of the hexagram; of the thousand shapes and ten thousand states, how can all be told? |
| 筮者当察其衰旺，自宜变通活泼，相机而断，无有不验，若响之应声也。 | 筮者应当审察五行的衰旺，自然要变通灵活，相机判断，没有不灵验的，就像回声应着声音。 | The diviner should examine their strength and weakness and naturally adapt and be flexible, judging as the occasion offers, and there will be none that fail to prove true, as an echo answers a sound. |

> **按**:结语三联在书里没有标点（「祸福攸关于大象变通洞烛机微」），按对仗读成「祸福攸关于大象，变通洞烛机微」「吉凶不离于五行，剖精研衰旺」。「格物致知」出自《大学》，是推究事物、获得知识；「悉战」疑是「悉载」，译文按「说尽」译。
>
> **Note:** The three closing couplets are unpunctuated in the book ('huofu youguan yu daxiang biantong dongzhu jiwei'), and by their parallelism are read as 'fortune and misfortune turn on the great image; adapt and perceive the faintest signs' and 'good and ill do not leave the five elements; analyse and study strength and weakness'. 'Gewu zhizhi' is from the Daxue, investigating things to gain knowledge; 'xi zhan' is presumably 'xi zai', to record fully, and the translation reads 'tell all'.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.10、LY.CAREER.EL.12

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.89 上书奏对：以太岁五爻为主、文书官鬼为凭；启奏君王看五爻，生身合命宠应叨，岁君克世龙颜怒，月将生官品爵高；五爻为天子、太岁为君王，一爻生合身世所奏如心，冲克身世恐犯天威休陈情因，官不空破又得日月生扶者必然破格超升

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:上书、启奏，以太岁和五爻为主，以文书爻、官鬼为凭据。启奏君王要看五爻：五爻是天子，太岁是君王，这两者只要有一个生合身爻、世爻，所奏的就如愿，得到宠遇；五爻和太岁如果来冲克身爻、世爻，恐怕触犯天威，就不要陈述情由；岁君克世爻，君王龙颜震怒；月将生官爻，官品爵位就高。另外，官爻多半是自己的本职：不落旬空、月破，又得日月生扶的，一定破格超升。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For submitting a memorial, take Tai Sui and the fifth line as the chief, and the document and Pressure as the ground. To memorialise the sovereign look at the fifth line: the fifth line is the Son of Heaven and Tai Sui is the sovereign, and if either of the two generates or combines with the Body or the Self, what is memorialised goes as one wishes and one receives favour; if the fifth line and Tai Sui come to clash or control the Body and the Self, one fears offending the majesty of Heaven and should not present the circumstances; if Tai Sui controls the Self the dragon countenance is angry, and if the Month General generates the star of office, rank and title are high. Also, the star of office mostly stands for one's own post: if it does not fall on the ten-day Void or Month-break and is further generated and supported by the Day and the Month, one is surely promoted beyond the regulations.

**《易林补遗·上书咎奏章第三十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 832 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 34: Submitting letters and memorials", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 832*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以太岁五爻为主，文书官鬼为凭。 | 以太岁、五爻为主要看的，以文书、官鬼为凭据。 | Take Tai Sui and the fifth line as the chief to be looked at, and the document and Pressure as the ground. |

**《易林补遗·上书咎奏章第三十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 833 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 34: Submitting letters and memorials", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 833*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 启奏君王看五爻，生身合命宠应叨， | 启奏君王要看五爻：五爻生身爻、合命（世）爻，宠遇就会到来； | To memorialise the sovereign look at the fifth line: if it generates the Body and combines with the Self, the 'fate' line, favour is sure to be received; |
| 岁君克世龙颜怒， | 岁君克制世爻，君王龙颜震怒； | if Tai Sui controls the Self, the dragon countenance is angry; |
| 月将生官品爵高。 | 月将生助官爻，官品爵位就高。 | if the Month General generates the star of office, rank and title are high. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 834 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 834*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 启奏者须看五爻为天子，太岁为君王，此二者如有一爻生合身世，所奏如心。 | 启奏的人，必须把五爻看作天子、太岁看作君王；这两者里如果有一个生合身爻、世爻，所奏的就如愿。 | One who memorialises must take the fifth line as the Son of Heaven and Tai Sui as the sovereign; if either of the two generates or combines with the Body or the Self, what is memorialised goes as one wishes. |
| 五爻与太岁如来冲克身世，恐犯天威，休陈情因。 | 五爻和太岁如果来冲克身爻、世爻，恐怕触犯天威，就不要陈述情由。 | If the fifth line and Tai Sui come to clash or control the Body and the Self, one fears offending the majesty of Heaven, and should not present the circumstances. |
| 又论官多为本职，不值旬空月破，更得日月生扶者，必然破格超陛。 | 又论官爻，多半是自己的本职；不落旬空、月破，更得日月生扶的，一定破格超升。 | Also, the star of office mostly stands for one's own post; if it does not fall on the ten-day Void or Month-break and is further generated and supported by the Day and the Month, one will surely be promoted beyond the regulations. |
| 五爻为天子之爻。 | 五爻就是代表天子的爻。 | The fifth line is the line of the Son of Heaven. |

> **按**:章名「上书咎奏」的「咎」疑有误，按本章内容（启奏、上本）译作「上书奏本」。「五爻为天子」「太岁为君王」与前面求恩宠一条同取（EL.80，黄金策国朝 HJC.64、HJC.65）。「龙颜」是君王的面容，「天威」是君王的威严。「月将」书里没有解释，按文意是月令（月建）的力量；六壬里的月将是另一回事，这里不用。「破格超升」是不按常规、越级升官。「生身合命」的「命」按解里「生合身世」读作世爻。末句「五爻为天子之爻」是解里对前面的重申。
>
> **Note:** The 'jiu' of the chapter title 'shang shu jiu zou' looks wrong, and by the content of the chapter (memorialising and submitting a petition) it is translated 'submitting letters and memorials'. 'The fifth line is the Son of Heaven' and 'Tai Sui is the sovereign' are taken as in the earlier entry on seeking favour (EL.80, and HJC.64 and HJC.65 in the Huangjin Ce on the state). 'Longyan', the dragon countenance, is the sovereign's face, and 'tianwei' is the majesty of Heaven. 'Yuejiang' is not explained in the book, and by the sense it is the force of the month (the Month); the month general of the Da Liu Ren is another matter and is not used here. 'Po ge chao sheng' is being promoted out of the usual order and over rank. The 'ming' of 'sheng shen he ming' is read as the Self by the explanation's 'generates or combines with the Body or the Self'. The last sentence, 'the fifth line is the line of the Son of Heaven', is the explanation restating what went before.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.90、LY.CAREER.EL.80、LY.CAREER.HJC.26、LY.CAREER.HJC.64、LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.ZS.28、LY.CAREER.ZS.35

## LY.CAREER.EL.91 朝天面旨：以太岁五爻为主、官鬼世象为凭；面君官旺世无伤折槛廷诤也不妨，太岁五爻冲克世休趋殿陛惹天殃；面君者先推世象次察官爻，二爻旺相不空又不受君爻冲克则并无玷剥，世爻若被天子之爻冲克朝必遭殃守静为上

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:朝见君王，以太岁和五爻为主，以官鬼、世爻为凭据。面君时官爻旺相、世爻没有受伤，就是折断栏杆、在朝廷上直言力谏也无妨；太岁、五爻如果冲克世爻，就不要趋步到殿前去招来天灾。面君的人，先推世爻，再察官爻：这两爻如果旺相、不落空亡，又不受君王之爻冲克，去见天子就没有污损伤害；世爻如果被天子之爻冲克，上朝一定遭殃，以安静守着为上。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For an audience with the sovereign, take Tai Sui and the fifth line as the chief, and Pressure and the Self image as the ground. If the star of office is thriving and the Self is unhurt, even breaking the balustrade in a remonstrance at court is no matter; if Tai Sui or the fifth line clashes with or controls the Self, do not hasten to the palace steps to bring on a heavenly calamity. One who is to see the sovereign should first reckon the Self image and then examine the Pressure line: if these two lines are thriving, not on Void, and not clashed or controlled by the sovereign's line, then going before the Son of Heaven there will be no blemish or harm; if the Self line is clashed or controlled by the Son of Heaven's line, going to court will surely bring calamity, and keeping quiet is best.

**《易林补遗·朝天面旨章第三十五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 840 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 35: An audience with the sovereign", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 840*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以太岁五爻为主，官鬼世象为凭。 | 以太岁、五爻为主要看的，以官鬼、世爻为凭据。 | Take Tai Sui and the fifth line as the chief to be looked at, and Pressure and the Self image as the ground. |

**《易林补遗·朝天面旨章第三十五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 841 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 35: An audience with the sovereign", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 841*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 面君官旺世无伤，折槛廷诤也不妨， | 面见君王，官爻旺相、世爻没有受伤，就是折断栏杆在朝廷上直言力谏也无妨； | In an audience with the sovereign, if the star of office is thriving and the Self is unhurt, even breaking the balustrade in a remonstrance at court is no matter; |
| 太岁五爻冲克世，休趋殿陛惹天殃。 | 太岁、五爻冲克世爻，就不要趋步到殿前去招来天灾。 | if Tai Sui or the fifth line clashes with or controls the Self, do not hasten to the palace steps to bring on a heavenly calamity. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 842 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 842*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 面君者，先推世象，次察官爻，此二爻如临旺相，不值空亡，又不受君爻冲克，在去天天并无玷剥。 | 面君的人，先推世爻，再察官爻；这两爻如果旺相、不落空亡，又不受君王之爻冲克，去见天子就没有污损、伤害。 | One who is to see the sovereign should first reckon the Self image and then examine the Pressure line; if these two lines are thriving, not on Void, and not clashed or controlled by the sovereign's line, then going before the Son of Heaven there will be no blemish or harm. |
| 世爻若被天子之爻冲克者，朝必遭殃，守静为上。 | 世爻如果被天子之爻冲克，上朝一定遭殃，以安静守着为上。 | If the Self line is clashed or controlled by the Son of Heaven's line, going to court will surely bring calamity, and keeping quiet is best. |

> **按**:「折槛」用的是汉朝朱云在殿上直谏、攀折殿槛（栏杆）的典故；「廷诤」是在朝廷上直言劝谏；「殿陛」是宫殿的台阶。「在去天天并无玷剥」文字有误，疑是「此去天颜并无玷剥」，译作「去见天子就没有污损、伤害」。「君爻」是五爻（天子之爻）。「守静」是不去、不动。这一条与增删卜易上书「子动临身，力能折槛」（ZS.33）同用「折槛」一典，但那一条看的是子孙，这里看官爻和世爻。
>
> **Note:** 'Zhe jian' alludes to Zhu Yun of the Han, who remonstrated in the hall and clung to the balustrade until it broke; 'tingzheng' is remonstrating plainly at court; 'dianbi' are the steps of the palace. 'Zai qu tian tian bing wu dian bo' is corrupt in its characters and is presumably 'ci qu tianyan bing wu dian bo', translated 'going before the Son of Heaven there will be no blemish or harm'. 'Junyao', the sovereign's line, is the fifth line (the line of the Son of Heaven). 'Shoujing' is not going and not acting. This entry uses the same allusion of 'breaking the balustrade' as 'if Output moves on the Body, one has the strength to break the balustrade' in the Zengshan Buyi on submitting letters (ZS.33), but that entry looks at Output and this one looks at the Pressure line and the Self.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.92、LY.CAREER.EL.89、LY.CAREER.HJC.26、LY.CAREER.ZS.33

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.92 朝天面旨：日辰月建生身吉、克世伤官尽不祥，卦值晋升蒙上宠、爻成屯蹇岂为强；世爻与官爻纵值休囚得日月或动爻生助或变出生扶皆为佳兆，朝王卜得火地晋、地风升皆吉，水雷屯、水山蹇皆为难遇者必凶

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:日辰、月建生助身爻是吉，克世爻、伤官爻就都不祥；卦遇晋、升，蒙受君上的恩宠；爻成屯、蹇，哪里谈得上强？世爻和官爻即使逢休囚，只要得到日月或动爻生助，或者变出生扶它们的爻，都是好兆；如果动爻和日月伤官爻、伤世爻，都按凶来推。朝见君王卜得火地晋，晋是臣子遇见君王的象；又取地风升，升是往前、往上走，所以这两卦都吉；如果卜得水雷屯、水山蹇两卦，都是艰难，遇到的一定凶。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Day and the Month generate the Body it is auspicious; if they control the Self or hurt the star of office, all is inauspicious. If the hexagram is Jin or Sheng, one receives the sovereign's favour; if the lines make Zhun or Jian, how could it be strong? Even if the Self line and the Pressure line are resting or imprisoned, if they get generation and aid from the Day and the Month or a moving line, or change out a line that generates and supports them, it is all a good omen; if a moving line and the Day and Month hurt the star of office or the Self, all is judged ominous. If in going to see the king one casts Jin, Fire over Earth, Jin is the image of a minister meeting his sovereign; Sheng, Earth over Wind, is also taken, for Sheng is advancing and going up, so these two hexagrams are both auspicious. If one casts Zhun, Water over Thunder, or Jian, Water over Mountain, both mean difficulty, and whoever meets them will surely have ill.

**《易林补遗·朝天面旨章第三十五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 843 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 35: An audience with the sovereign", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 843*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日辰月建生身吉，克世伤官尽不祥， | 日辰、月建生助身爻是吉，克世爻、伤官爻就都不祥； | If the Day and the Month generate the Body it is auspicious; if they control the Self or hurt the star of office, all is inauspicious; |
| 卦值晋升蒙上宠，爻成屯蹇岂为强。 | 卦遇晋、升，蒙受君上的恩宠；爻成屯、蹇，哪里谈得上强？ | if the hexagram is Jin or Sheng, one receives the sovereign's favour; if the lines make Zhun or Jian, how could it be strong? |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 845–847 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 845–847*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世爻与官爻纵值休囚，得日月或动爻生助、或变出生扶，皆为佳兆，若动爻与日月伤官伤世，俱作凶推，朝王卜得火地晋，晋者以臣遇君之象。 | 世爻和官爻即使逢休囚，得到日月或动爻生助，或者变出生扶它们的爻，都是好兆；如果动爻和日月伤官爻、伤世爻，都按凶来推。朝见君王卜得火地晋，晋是臣子遇见君王的象。 | Even if the Self line and the Pressure line are resting or imprisoned, if they get generation and aid from the Day and the Month or a moving line, or change out a line that generates and supports them, it is all a good omen; if a moving line and the Day and Month hurt the star of office or the Self, all is judged ominous. If in going to see the king one casts Jin, Fire over Earth, Jin is the image of a minister meeting his sovereign. |
| 又取地风升，升者，进而上也，故此二卦皆吉。 | 又取地风升，升是往前、往上走，所以这两卦都吉。 | Sheng, Earth over Wind, is also taken, for 'sheng' is advancing and going up, so these two hexagrams are both auspicious. |
| 若卜水雷屯、水山蹇二卦，皆为难也，遇者必凶。 | 如果卜得水雷屯、水山蹇两卦，都是艰难，遇到的一定凶。 | If one casts Zhun, Water over Thunder, or Jian, Water over Mountain, both mean difficulty, and whoever meets them will surely have ill. |

> **打架**:这里说卜得屯、蹇两卦「皆为难也，遇者必凶」；增删卜易引了古人「晋、升为吉，屯、蹇为凶」的说法后，野鹤（ZS.07）说晋、升也要世爻得地，屯、蹇两卦只要世爻旺相就没有妨碍，他有一次世爻旺相占到屯卦，照样越级升迁。也就是说：这里把卦名当作断语，增删卜易把卦名放在世爻旺衰的后面。
>
> **Where they disagree:** This entry says that casting Zhun or Jian 'both mean difficulty, and whoever meets them will surely have ill'; the Zengshan Buyi quotes the ancients' 'Jin and Sheng are auspicious, Zhun and Jian ominous' and then the Old Man of Yehe (ZS.07) says that Jin and Sheng also need the Self to be well placed, that for Zhun and Jian there is no harm so long as the Self is thriving, and that once he got Zhun with a thriving Self and was promoted beyond rank all the same. That is, this entry takes the hexagram name as a verdict, while the Zengshan Buyi puts the hexagram name behind the strength of the Self.

> **按**:「火地晋」「地风升」「水雷屯」「水山蹇」是《周易》的卦名，这里按卦名取义：晋是前进，升是上升，屯、蹇是艰难。解里先说世爻、官爻的旺衰生克，最后才说卦名。「休囚」见术语表。「动爻与日月伤官伤世」书里连写，这里按「动爻和日月来伤官爻、伤世爻」读。
>
> **Note:** 'Huo di Jin', 'Di feng Sheng', 'Shui lei Zhun' and 'Shui shan Jian' are hexagram names of the Book of Changes, taken here by the meaning of the name: Jin is advancing, Sheng is rising, Zhun and Jian are difficulty. The explanation first speaks of the strength, generation and control of the Self and Pressure and only afterwards of the hexagram names. 'Resting or imprisoned' is in the glossary. 'Dong yao yu ri yue shang guan shang shi' is run together in the book and is read here as 'a moving line and the Day and Month come to hurt the star of office and the Self'.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.91、LY.CAREER.EL.93、LY.CAREER.ZS.07

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.26 仕宦·奏陈谏诤，那堪太岁刑冲（旧注：奏对陈疏上章谏诤赴召面君皆忌动爻冲克太岁亦忌刑克世爻；太岁月建生合世爻必见详允，冲克须防不测之祸；岁君衰静不带刑害虎蛇不见取用非大害；动空化空虚惊；有制伏冲散合住必得大臣申救；应动冲克世更防人奏劾）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:上奏陈情、进呈奏章、上书、进谏，以及奉召面君这类事，都忌讳动爻冲克太岁，也忌讳世爻被刑克；如果太岁、月建生合世爻，必定得到详细审议并获准；一旦遇到冲克，须提防意外的祸患；太岁衰弱安静又不带刑、害、白虎、螣蛇，只是不被采用，没有大害；动爻空亡或化空，也只是虚惊；有制伏、冲散、合住的，必有大臣出来申救；应爻发动冲克世爻，更要提防有人上奏弹劾。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For anything such as a memorial in audience, presenting a petition, submitting a written report, remonstrating, or going to an audience with the sovereign at his summons, it is feared that a moving line clashes or controls the Tai Sui, and also that the Self is punished or controlled; if the Tai Sui or the Month generates or combines with the Self, it will surely be examined in detail and approved; once a clash or control comes, beware of an unforeseen misfortune; if the Tai Sui is weak and still and carries no punishment, harm, White Tiger or Coiling Snake, one is only not adopted and there is no great harm; if the moving line is Void or turns into Void, it is only a false alarm; if there is something that subdues, clashes away or combines it, a high minister will surely come forward to plead and rescue; and if the Other moves and clashes or controls the Self, beware all the more of someone submitting an impeachment.

**《黄金策·仕宦》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7745 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7745*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 奏陈谏诤，那堪太岁刑冲； | 上奏陈情、进谏诤言，哪里经得起太岁的刑冲； | To submit a memorial or remonstrate, how can one withstand the Tai Sui's punishment or clash; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7746 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7746*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡欲奏对、陈疏，上章、谏诤，及赴召面君类，皆忌动爻冲克太岁，亦忌刑克世爻。 | 凡是要奏对、陈疏、上章、谏诤，以及奉召面君这类事，都忌讳动爻冲克太岁，也忌讳刑克世爻。 | For anything such as an audience with a memorial, presenting a petition, submitting a written report, remonstrating, or going to an audience with the sovereign at his summons, it is feared that a moving line clashes or controls the Tai Sui, and it is also feared that the Self is punished or controlled. |
| 若太岁月建生合世爻，必见详允。 | 如果太岁、月建生合世爻，必定得到详细审议并获准。 | If the Tai Sui or the Month generates or combines with the Self, it will surely be examined in detail and approved. |
| 一来冲克，须防不测之祸。 | 一旦遇到冲克，须提防意外的祸患。 | Once a clash or control comes, beware of an unforeseen misfortune. |
| 岁君衰静，不带刑害虎蛇，主则不见取用，非有大害。 | 太岁衰弱安静，又不带刑、害、白虎、螣蛇，就是不被采用，没有大害。 | If the Tai Sui is weak and still and carries no punishment, harm, White Tiger or Coiling Snake, it means one is not adopted, and there is no great harm. |
| 动空化空，亦是虚惊。 | 动爻空亡或者化空，也只是虚惊一场。 | If the moving line is Void or turns into Void, it is likewise only a false alarm. |
| 或有制伏冲散合住，必得大臣申救。 | 有制伏、冲散、合住的，必有大臣出来申救。 | If there is something that subdues, clashes away or combines it, a high minister will surely come forward to plead and rescue. |
| 应动冲克世爻，更防人奏劾。 | 应爻发动冲克世爻，更要提防有人上奏弹劾。 | If the Other moves and clashes or controls the Self, beware all the more of someone submitting an impeachment. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1978 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1978*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡遇奏对、陈疏、上章、谏诤及赴召面君类，皆忌动爻冲克，并忌太岁刑克世爻。 | 凡遇到奏对、陈疏、上章、谏诤和奉召面君这类事，都忌讳动爻冲克，也忌讳太岁刑克世爻。 | In anything such as an audience with a memorial, presenting a petition, submitting a written report, remonstrating, or going to an audience at the sovereign's summons, a moving line clashing or controlling is feared, and the Tai Sui punishing or controlling the Self is also feared. |
| 若太岁月建生合世爻，必见谕允； | 如果太岁、月建生合世爻，必定得到准许； | If the Tai Sui or the Month generates or combines with the Self, one will surely receive approval; |
| 如来冲克，须防不测之祸。 | 如果来冲克，须提防意外的祸患。 | if a clash or control comes, beware of an unforeseen misfortune. |

> **按**:「奏对」是面奏回答皇帝，「陈疏」「上章」是上书，「谏诤」是进言规劝，「赴召面君」是奉召去见皇帝，「申救」是出面陈说、营救，「奏劾」是上奏弹劾。旧注「亦忌刑克世爻」没有说谁刑克世爻，王洪绪补明是「太岁刑克世爻」。旧注比王洪绪多出的几句（太岁衰静不带刑害虎蛇只是不被采用、动空化空是虚惊、有制伏冲散合住有大臣申救、应动冲克世防人弹劾），是在「冲克」这个凶象上分轻重：太岁衰静无煞只是不被采纳，有煞才是祸；动爻空或化空是虚惊；有人制住冲克的爻，就有大臣出来救。这一联是「面君」类事的判据，所以放在「国朝」一题下。
>
> **Note:** 'Audience' is replying to the emperor in person, 'a petition' and 'a written report' are writing to the throne, 'remonstrating' is offering advice and admonition, 'going to an audience at his summons' is being summoned to see the emperor, 'plead and rescue' is coming forward to speak and save, and 'impeachment' is submitting a memorial of impeachment. The old commentary's 'it is also feared that the Self is punished or controlled' does not say by whom, and Wang Hongxu makes it clear that it is 'the Tai Sui punishing or controlling the Self'. The extra sentences of the old commentary (the Tai Sui weak and still with no punishment, harm, Tiger or Snake is only not being adopted; a moving line Void or turning into Void is a false alarm; with something that subdues, clashes away or combines it, a minister pleads and rescues; the Other moving and clashing the Self, beware impeachment) grade the ominous sign of clash or control by weight: the Tai Sui weak and still without killers is only not being adopted, and only with killers is there misfortune; the moving line Void or turned Void is a false alarm; and when something holds in check the line that clashes or controls, a minister comes out to rescue. This couplet is the criterion for matters of facing the sovereign, hence it is placed under 'the court and the state'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.8) 第 1978 行:奏陈谏诤，哪堪太岁冲
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1797 行:奏陈谏诤，那堪太岁刑冲

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.07、LY.CAREER.HJC.15

## LY.CAREER.HJC.63 国朝·君恕则臣忠，共济明良之会；国泰则民乐，当推祸福之原；国之治乱兴衰，卦理推详剖决（总论）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占国家的兴衰，是把整个卦当作国运来读。国家有始有终，必有兴有废；国君宽厚，臣子就忠心；国家太平，百姓就安乐；这些祸福的来由，都可以用卦理推到根上、剖开判决。哪一爻代表什么，见下一条。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A question about the fortunes of a state reads the whole hexagram as the fortune of the realm. A state has a beginning and an end and must rise and fall; when the ruler is forgiving the ministers are loyal, and when the state is at peace the people are happy; the source of such blessings and disasters can be traced and decided by the principles of the hexagram. Which line stands for what is in the next rule.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5814–5815 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5814–5815*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 君恕则臣忠，共济明良之会； | 国君宽恕体恤，臣子就忠心，君臣共同成就明君与良臣相遇的盛会； | When the ruler is forgiving the ministers are loyal, and together they bring about the meeting of a bright ruler and good ministers; |
| 国泰则民乐，当推祸福之原。 | 国家太平，百姓就安乐，要推究的就是祸福产生的根源。 | when the state is at peace the people are happy, and what is to be traced is the source of disaster and fortune. |
| 虽天地尚知其始终，况国家岂能无兴废？ | 连天地尚且知道有它的开始和终结，何况一个国家，怎么可能没有兴盛和衰亡？ | Even heaven and earth are known to have a beginning and an end, so how could a state be without rise and fall? |

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5888 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5888*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 国之治乱兴衰，卦理推详剖决。 | 国家是治是乱、是兴是衰，都可以用卦理详细推究、剖析判断。 | Whether a state is in order or in disorder, rising or declining, can be examined in detail and decided by the principles of the hexagram. |

> **按**:国朝章从这里开头、到这里收尾，中间二十多联都是拿具体的历史人物来讲卦象，用法见下面各条。
>
> **Note:** The chapter on the state opens and closes with these lines; the twenty-odd couplets between them use particular historical figures to teach the images, as the entries that follow show.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1462 行:君恕则臣忠，共济明良之会;同文 第 1462 行:国泰则民乐，当推祸福之原;同文 第 1462 行:虽天地尚知其始终，况国家岂能无兴废
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 369 行:君怒则臣忠，共济明良之会;同文 第 369 行:国泰则民乐，当推祸福之原;同文 第 369 行:虽天地尚知其始终，况国家岂能无兴废
- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1508 行:国之治乱兴衰，卦理推详剖决
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 373 行:国之治乱兴衰，卦理推详剖决

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.64

## LY.CAREER.HJC.64 国朝·本宫旺相，周文王创八百年之基；大象休囚，秦始皇遗二世主之祸（旧注：本宫为国爻，太岁为君、岁合为后、月建为臣、日建为东宫、子孙为黎庶、父爻为国；本宫旺相国祚长，休囚而大象又凶则速亡）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占国运，先分派各爻：本宫是国爻，太岁是君，和太岁相合的是后，月建是臣，日建是东宫（太子），子孙是百姓，父爻是国家。开国时占得本宫旺相，国运绵长，像周文王那样子孙享国八百年；本宫休囚、整个卦象又凶，国运短促，像秦始皇那样到二世就亡国。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To ask about the fortune of a state, first assign the lines: the home palace is the line of the state, Tai Sui is the ruler, the branch combining with Tai Sui is the empress, the Month is the minister, the Day is the Eastern Palace (the crown prince), Output is the common people and Resource is the realm. If a state is being founded and the home palace comes out thriving or strong, its fortune is long, as with King Wen of Zhou, whose descendants held the realm for eight hundred years; if the home palace is resting or trapped and the whole image is also bad, its fortune is short, as with the First Emperor of Qin, whose state was lost with the second reign.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5816 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5816*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本宫旺相，周文王创八百年之基； | 本宫旺相，就像周文王创下了八百年的基业； | If the home palace is thriving or strong, it is like King Wen of Zhou founding a base of eight hundred years; |
| 大象休囚，秦始皇遗二世主之祸。 | 整个卦象休囚，就像秦始皇留下了二世皇帝覆亡的祸患。 | if the whole image is resting or trapped, it is like the First Emperor of Qin leaving behind the disaster of the Second Emperor. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5817 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5817*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本宫为国爻。 | 本宫是代表国家的爻。 | The home palace is the line of the state. |
| 以太岁为君爻，岁合为后爻； | 拿太岁当君主的爻，和太岁相合的当皇后的爻； | Take Tai Sui as the ruler's line and the branch that combines with Tai Sui as the empress's line; |
| 月建为臣爻，日建为东宫； | 月建当臣子的爻，日建当东宫（太子）； | the Month as the ministers' line and the Day as the Eastern Palace (the crown prince); |
| 子孙为黎庶，父爻为国。 | 子孙当百姓，父爻当国家。 | Output as the common people and Resource as the realm. |
| 若建国时，卜得本宫旺相，如周文王，子孙享国八百年之久也。 | 如果是开国之时占卜，占得本宫旺相，就像周文王那样，子孙享有国运长达八百年。 | If the divination is made when a state is being founded and the home palace comes out thriving or strong, it is like King Wen of Zhou, whose descendants enjoyed the realm for as long as eight hundred years. |
| 史记周武王定鼎于洛，卜云：传位三十，历年七百。 | 《史记》记载周武王在洛邑定都，占卜的结果是：传位三十代，经历七百年。 | The Records of the Grand Historian say that when King Wu of Zhou fixed the tripods at Luo, the divination read: thirty successions, seven hundred years. |
| 后至赧王亡，共算八百余年。 | 后来到周赧王时灭亡，一共算下来有八百多年。 | Later the dynasty perished with King Nan, and in all it came to more than eight hundred years. |
| 故曰：周过其历也。 | 所以说：周朝超过了卜到的年数。 | Hence it is said: Zhou went beyond its reckoned span. |
| 若本宫休囚，大象又凶，则如秦始皇，二世亡国。 | 如果本宫休囚，整个卦象又凶，就像秦始皇那样，到二世就亡国。 | If the home palace is resting or trapped and the whole image is also bad, it is like the First Emperor of Qin, whose state was lost with the second reign. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1462 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1462*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如臣卜以本宫为国朝，以太岁为君爻，岁合为后爻，月建为臣爻，日建为东宫，子孙为黎庶，父爻为国。 | 臣子占卜时，以本宫为国朝，以太岁为君爻，岁合为后爻，月建为臣爻，日建为东宫，子孙为百姓，父爻为国家。 | When a minister casts, take the home palace as the court of the state, Tai Sui as the ruler's line, the branch combining with Tai Sui as the empress's line, the Month as the ministers' line, the Day as the Eastern Palace, Output as the common people and Resource as the realm. |
| 卜得本宫旺相，如周文王子孙享国八百年之久； | 占得本宫旺相，就像周文王的子孙享有国运八百年之久； | If the home palace comes out thriving or strong, it is like the descendants of King Wen of Zhou holding the realm for as long as eight hundred years; |
| 若本宫休囚，大象又凶，则如秦始皇二世亡国。 | 如果本宫休囚，整个卦象又凶，就像秦始皇到二世就亡国。 | if the home palace is resting or trapped and the whole image is also bad, it is like the First Emperor of Qin, whose state was lost with the second reign. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗把这段分派写成「如臣卜」（臣子占国朝），并在后面「年伤弱世」一联说「以下指言国君自卜」，把这一章分成臣子占国运和国君自占两种占法；卜筮大全的旧注没有这样标明。
>
> **Note:** The Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes this assignment as what applies 'when a minister casts', and at the couplet 'the year injures a weak Self' it says 'what follows speaks of the ruler casting for himself', so that it divides the chapter into a minister asking about the realm and the ruler asking about himself; the old commentary in the Bu Shi Da Quan does not mark this.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1462 行:本宫旺相，周文王创八百年之基;异文(相似 0.92) 第 1462 行:大象休囚，秦始皇遗二世之祸
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 369 行:本宫旺相，周文王创八百年之基;同文 第 370 行:大象休囚，秦始皇遗二世主之祸

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.63、LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.67

## LY.CAREER.HJC.65 国朝·九五逢阳，当遇仁明之主；四爻值福，必多忠义之臣（旧注：五爻为君位，逢阳象又遇青龙财福是仁明之君；四爻为臣位，子孙旺相克世是敢谏之臣，兄鬼生合世爻是阿谀佞臣）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:第五爻是君位：逢到阳象，又遇到青龙、妻财、子孙，是仁慈英明的君主；世爻临阳象、带吉神也一样。第四爻是臣位：子孙旺相、克世爻，是敢于直言进谏的臣子；兄弟、官鬼生合世爻，是阿谀奉承的佞臣。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The fifth line is the ruler's seat: if it meets a yang image and also meets the Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output, the ruler is benevolent and enlightened, and so it is if the Self sits in a yang image with auspicious spirits. The fourth line is the ministers' seat: if Output is thriving or strong and controls the Self, the minister dares to remonstrate plainly; if a Peer or Pressure line generates or combines with the Self, he is a flattering and treacherous minister.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5819 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5819*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 九五逢阳，当遇仁明之主； | 九五（第五爻）逢到阳，会遇到仁慈英明的君主； | If the fifth line meets yang, one will meet a benevolent and enlightened ruler; |
| 四爻值福，必多忠义之臣。 | 第四爻逢到福神，必定有很多忠义的臣子。 | if the fourth line meets the lucky star of Output, there will surely be many loyal and righteous ministers. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5820 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5820*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 五爻为君位。 | 第五爻是君主的位置。 | The fifth line is the ruler's seat. |
| 若逢阳象，更遇青龙财福，必是仁明之君。 | 如果逢到阳象，又遇到青龙、妻财、福神，必定是仁慈英明的君主。 | If it meets a yang image and further meets the Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output, the ruler is surely benevolent and enlightened. |
| 世临阳象，带吉神亦然。 | 世爻临在阳象上、带着吉神，也是这样。 | If the Self sits in a yang image and carries auspicious spirits, it is the same. |
| 四爻为臣位，若子孙旺相克世，乃敢谏直臣。 | 第四爻是臣子的位置，如果子孙旺相、克世爻，就是敢于直言进谏的臣子。 | The fourth line is the ministers' seat; if Output is thriving or strong and controls the Self, he is a minister who dares to remonstrate plainly. |
| 若兄鬼生合世爻，乃阿谀佞臣也。 | 如果兄弟、官鬼生合世爻，就是阿谀奉承的奸佞臣子。 | If a Peer or Pressure line generates or combines with the Self, he is a flattering and treacherous minister. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1464–1466 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, lines 1464–1466*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 五爻为君位，逢阳象遇青龙财福，是仁明之君。 | 第五爻是君主的位置，逢到阳象又遇到青龙、妻财、福神，是仁慈英明的君主。 | The fifth line is the ruler's seat; when it meets a yang image and meets the Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output, he is a benevolent and enlightened ruler. |
| 四爻为臣位，临旺福乃敢谏直臣，临兄鬼乃阿谀佞臣也。 | 第四爻是臣子的位置，逢到旺相的福神是敢于直言进谏的臣子，逢到兄弟、官鬼就是阿谀奉承的奸佞臣子。 | The fourth line is the ministers' seat; when it meets a thriving Output line he is a minister who dares to remonstrate plainly, and when it meets a Peer or Pressure line he is a flattering and treacherous minister. |

> **按**:条件有出入：旧注要子孙旺相并且克世爻、兄鬼要生合世爻；卜筮正宗只说第四爻「临旺福」「临兄鬼」，没有说克世或生合世爻。
>
> **Note:** The conditions differ: the old commentary requires Output to be thriving and to control the Self, and the Peer or Pressure line to generate or combine with the Self; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says only that the fourth line 'meets a thriving Output' or 'meets Peer or Pressure', and does not mention controlling or combining with the Self.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1464 行:九五逢阳，当遇仁明之主;同文 第 1464 行:四爻值福，必多忠义之臣
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:九五逢阳，当遇仁明之主;同文 第 370 行:四爻值福，必多忠义之臣

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.64、LY.CAREER.HJC.66、LY.CAREER.HJC.79、LY.CAREER.HJC.83
