## LY.CAREER.HJC.66 国朝·岁克衰宫，玉树后庭花欲谢（旧注：本宫衰死又遇太岁所克，其年国有乱亡之兆；陈后主宫中酣歌不已，后为隋所灭）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:本宫衰弱死绝，又被太岁克，这一年国家有动乱灭亡的征兆，就像陈后主在宫里日夜歌舞饮宴，后来被隋朝灭掉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the home palace is weak or dead and is also controlled by Tai Sui, the state shows signs of disorder and ruin that year, like the last ruler of Chen, who gave himself to songs and revels in the palace and was later destroyed by Sui.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5822 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5822*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 岁克衰宫，玉树后庭花欲谢； | 太岁克制衰弱的本宫，就像《玉树后庭花》将要凋谢； | If Tai Sui controls a weak home palace, it is as if 'Jade Trees and Flowers of the Rear Court' were about to fade; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5823 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5823*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本宫衰死，更遇太岁所克，其年国有乱亡之兆。 | 本宫衰弱死绝，又遇到太岁来克，这一年国家有动乱灭亡的征兆。 | If the home palace is weak or dead and meets control from Tai Sui, the state shows signs of disorder and ruin that year. |
| 昔陈将亡，后主使诸妃嫔，及女乐与侠客，共赋诗，互相赠答。 | 从前陈朝将要灭亡的时候，陈后主让众妃嫔，连同歌女乐伎和游侠宾客，一起作诗，互相赠答。 | Long ago, when Chen was about to fall, its last ruler had his consorts, together with the singing women and the wandering gallant guests, compose poems together and exchange them with one another. |
| 采其尤艳丽者，被以新声； | 挑出其中最艳丽的诗句，配上新曲； | They picked the most gorgeous of the verses and set them to new tunes; |
| 选宫女千余，习而歌之，分部迭奏。 | 选了一千多名宫女，学着歌唱，分成几部轮流演奏。 | they chose over a thousand palace women to learn and sing them, and the groups performed in turn. |
| 其曲有玉树后庭花，临春乐等，大略皆美诸嫔妃之容色。 | 那些曲子有《玉树后庭花》《临春乐》等，大致都是赞美各位妃嫔的容貌姿色。 | Among the pieces were 'Jade Trees and Flowers of the Rear Court' and 'Linchun Le', which in the main all praised the beauty of the consorts. |
| 君臣酣歌，自夕达旦，以此为常。 | 君臣尽兴唱歌饮酒，从晚上到天亮，一直这样成了常事。 | Ruler and ministers sang and drank to their heart's content from evening until dawn, and made it a habit. |
| 后为隋所灭。 | 后来陈被隋灭掉了。 | Later Chen was destroyed by Sui. |
| 故曰：玉树后庭花欲谢也。 | 所以说：《玉树后庭花》将要凋谢了。 | That is why it says: 'Jade Trees and Flowers of the Rear Court' is about to fade. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1468 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1468*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本宫衰弱遇太岁克，国有乱亡之兆。 | 本宫衰弱，遇到太岁来克，国家有动乱灭亡的征兆。 | If the home palace is weak and meets control from Tai Sui, the state shows signs of disorder and ruin. |
| 陈后主选宫女，曲有“玉树后庭花”，君臣酣歌，旦夕为常，后为隋所灭。 | 陈后主挑选宫女，曲子里有《玉树后庭花》，君臣尽兴歌唱，从早到晚成了常事，后来被隋灭掉。 | The last ruler of Chen picked palace women, and among their pieces was 'Jade Trees and Flowers of the Rear Court'; ruler and ministers sang to their heart's content from morning to night as a matter of course, and Chen was later destroyed by Sui. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1468 行:岁克衰宫，玉树后庭花欲谢
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:岁克衰宫，玉树后庭花欲谢

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.67

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.67 国朝·年伤弱世，鼎湖龙去不多时（旧注：太岁刑冲克害世爻主国君疾病或内难将作，世爻又衰弱必有君崩之忧）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:国君自己占卜：太岁来刑、冲、克、害世爻，主国君生病，或者宫廷内部将有祸乱；如果世爻同时又衰弱，就要担心国君驾崩。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the ruler casts for himself: if Tai Sui punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, the ruler will be ill or trouble will break out within the court; if the Self is also weak, there is cause to fear the ruler's death.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5825 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5825*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 年伤弱世，鼎湖龙去不多时。 | 太岁伤克衰弱的世爻，国君像黄帝在鼎湖乘龙升天一样，离世就在不久之后。 | If the year injures a weak Self, the dragon departs from Ding Lake before long. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5827–5828 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5827–5828*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 太岁刑冲克害世爻，主国君疾病，或内难将作。 | 太岁刑、冲、克、害世爻，主国君有疾病，或者内部的祸乱将要发作。 | If Tai Sui punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, it means illness of the ruler, or internal troubles about to break out. |
| 若克世爻，而世爻衰弱，必有君崩之忧。 | 如果克的是世爻，而世爻又衰弱，必定有国君驾崩的忧虑。 | If it controls the Self and the Self is weak, there is surely cause to fear the ruler's death. |
| 史记曰：黄帝乘龙上天。 | 《史记》说：黄帝乘龙升天。 | The Records of the Grand Historian says: the Yellow Emperor rose to heaven on a dragon. |
| 故凡帝崩曰升遐，讳言死也。 | 所以凡是皇帝去世都叫「升遐」，是避讳说死。 | So whenever an emperor dies it is called 'ascending far away', which avoids the word death. |
| 今日鼎湖龙去，言其不久而崩也。 | 现在说「鼎湖龙去」，是说他不久就要驾崩。 | Here 'the dragon leaves Ding Lake' says that he will die before long. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1470 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1470*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以下指言国君自卜。 | 以下是指国君自己占卜。 | What follows speaks of the ruler casting for himself. |
| 如太岁刑冲克害世爻，主疾病或内难将作。 | 例如太岁刑、冲、克、害世爻，主有疾病，或者内部的祸难将要发作。 | For example, if Tai Sui punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, it means illness or internal troubles about to break out. |

> **按**:「以下指言国君自卜」是卜筮正宗加的一句话，旧注里没有；按它的说法，从这一联起到「应带咸池」都是国君自占。
>
> **Note:** The sentence 'what follows speaks of the ruler casting for himself' is the Bu Shi Zheng Zong's own; the old commentary has no such line. On its reading, everything from this couplet through 'the Other carrying Xianchi' is the ruler's own cast.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1470 行:年伤弱世，鼎湖龙去不多时
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:年伤弱世，鼎湖龙去不多时

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.64、LY.CAREER.HJC.66、LY.CAREER.HJC.68

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.68 国朝·世临沐浴合妻财，夫差恋西施而亡国（旧注：世临沐浴，或财爻应爻带沐浴动克合世，必是国君好色；如夫差恋西施拒子胥之谏，后为越王勾践所灭）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻落在沐浴上，或者妻财爻、应爻带着沐浴发动、来克合世爻，多半是国君好色，就像夫差迷恋西施、拒不听伍子胥的劝谏，后来被越王勾践灭掉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self sits on Bathing, or the Wealth line or the Other line carries Bathing, moves, and controls or combines with the Self, the ruler is surely fond of women, like Fuchai who doted on Xishi, refused the remonstrance of Zixu, and was later destroyed by Goujian, King of Yue.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5830 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5830*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世临沐浴合妻财，夫差恋西施而亡国； | 世爻落在沐浴上、又合妻财，就像夫差迷恋西施而亡国； | If the Self sits on Bathing and combines with the Wealth line, it is like Fuchai who doted on Xishi and lost his state; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5831 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5831*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世临沐浴，或财爻、应爻带沐浴动，克合世爻，必是国君好色。 | 世爻落在沐浴上，或者财爻、应爻带着沐浴发动，来克、合世爻，必定是国君好色。 | If the Self sits on Bathing, or the Wealth line or the Other line carries Bathing and moves to control or combine with the Self, the ruler is surely fond of women. |
| 如夫差恋西施美，拒子胥谏，后为越王勾践所灭。 | 就像夫差迷恋西施的美貌，拒绝伍子胥的劝谏，后来被越王勾践灭掉。 | It is like Fuchai who doted on the beauty of Xishi, rejected the remonstrance of Zixu, and was later destroyed by Goujian, King of Yue. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1472 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1472*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 皆指言帝自卜也。 | （以上）都是指国君自己占卜。 | All of this speaks of the ruler casting for himself. |
| 如世临沐浴合财爻、应爻或沐浴动克合世，必是好色，如夫差恋西施之美，为越所灭。 | 例如世爻落在沐浴上，合财爻、应爻，或者沐浴发动来克合世爻，必定是好色，就像夫差迷恋西施的美貌，被越灭掉。 | For example, if the Self sits on Bathing and combines with the Wealth line or the Other line, or Bathing moves to control or combine with the Self, he is surely fond of women, like Fuchai who doted on the beauty of Xishi and was destroyed by Yue. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1472 行:世临沐浴合妻财，夫差恋西施而亡国
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:世临沐浴合妻财，夫差恋西施而亡国

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.67、LY.CAREER.HJC.69

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.69 国朝·应带咸池临九五，武后革唐命而为周（旧注：应爻为皇后，带咸池其后必淫，更居九五是后居尊位，如吕后临朝；再旺动刑冲克害世爻、世或空亡，则如武后废中宗革唐为周）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:应爻是皇后。应爻带咸池，皇后一定不检点；又落在第五爻，就是皇后居于至尊之位，像汉朝的吕后临朝称制；如果它再旺相发动、刑冲克害世爻，世爻又逢空亡，就像唐朝的武后废掉中宗、降为庐陵王，改唐为周。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Other line is the empress. If it carries Xianchi, the empress is sure to be licentious; if it also sits in the fifth place, the empress holds the seat of honour, like Empress Lü of Han presiding at court and issuing edicts; if it is also thriving and moving, and punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, while the Self may be Void, it is like Empress Wu of Tang, who deposed Emperor Zhongzong, made him Prince of Luling, and turned the Tang mandate into Zhou.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5833 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5833*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应带咸池临九五，武后革唐命而为周。 | 应爻带着咸池，又落在九五，就像武后改变了唐的国运、改唐为周。 | If the Other line carries Xianchi and sits in the fifth place, it is like Empress Wu who changed the mandate of Tang and made it Zhou. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5834 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5834*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应乃皇后之位。 | 应爻是皇后的位置。 | The Other line is the empress's place. |
| 若带咸池，其后必淫。 | 如果带着咸池，那位皇后必定淫乱。 | If it carries Xianchi, the empress is sure to be licentious. |
| 更居九五，乃是后居尊位。 | 再落在九五，就是皇后居于至尊之位。 | If it also sits in the fifth place, the empress holds the seat of honour. |
| 如汉吕后，临朝称制。 | 就像汉朝的吕后，临朝执政、代行皇帝的号令。 | It is like Empress Lü of Han, who presided at court and issued edicts in the ruler's place. |
| 更有旺动，刑冲克害世爻，世或空亡，必如唐武后废中宗为庐陵王，革唐命为周。 | 如果它再旺相发动，刑、冲、克、害世爻，世爻又落空亡，必定像唐朝的武后那样，废掉中宗、降为庐陵王，改唐的国运为周。 | If it is also thriving and moving, and punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, and the Self may be Void, it is surely like Empress Wu of Tang, who deposed Emperor Zhongzong, made him Prince of Luling, and changed the Tang mandate into Zhou. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1474 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1474*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 君自卜以应爻为皇后，若带咸池，其后必淫，更居九五尊位克世，如唐武后废中宗为庐陵王，革唐命为周。 | 国君自己占卜时，以应爻为皇后，如果带着咸池，那位皇后必定淫乱，再落在九五的尊位又来克世，就像唐朝的武后废掉中宗、降为庐陵王，改唐的国运为周。 | When the ruler casts for himself, take the Other line as the empress; if it carries Xianchi the empress is sure to be licentious, and if it also sits in the honoured seat of the fifth place and controls the Self, it is like Empress Wu of Tang, who deposed Zhongzong, made him Prince of Luling, and changed the Tang mandate into Zhou. |

> **按**:两处条件不同：旧注要应爻再旺相发动、刑冲克害世爻，并说「世或空亡」；卜筮正宗只说应爻居九五尊位、克世，没有提世爻落空。
>
> **Note:** The conditions differ: the old commentary adds that the Other line is thriving and moving and punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, and says 'the Self may be Void'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says only that the Other line sits in the honoured fifth place and controls the Self, and does not mention the Self being Void.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1474 行:应带咸池临九五，武后革唐命而为周
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:应带咸池临九五，武后革唐命而为周

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.68、LY.CAREER.HJC.74

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.70 国朝·游魂遇空，虞舜南巡不返（旧注：游魂卦主国君迁都游荡巡狩；再加凶杀克世，或世动入死墓空绝，则如虞舜南巡崩于苍梧之野）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:国君自占得游魂卦，是迁都、四处巡游的象，像周穆王巡游天下；如果再带凶煞克世，或世爻发动、变入死、墓、空、绝，就像虞舜南巡，死在苍梧的野外，一去不回。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the ruler casts for himself, a Wandering Soul hexagram is the image of moving the capital and touring the realm, like King Mu of Zhou who toured the whole land; if a baleful spirit is added that controls the Self, or the Self moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is like Shun of Yu, who went south on a tour and died in the wilds of Cangwu, never to return.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5836 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5836*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 游魂遇空，虞舜南巡不返； | 游魂卦遇到空，就像虞舜南巡一去不回； | If a Wandering Soul hexagram meets Void, it is like Shun of Yu who went south on a tour and never returned; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5837–5838 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5837–5838*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦遇游魂，主国君迁都游荡巡狩之象。 | 卦遇到游魂，主国君迁都、四处游荡巡视的象。 | If the hexagram is a Wandering Soul hexagram, it is the image of the ruler moving the capital and wandering about on tours of inspection. |
| 如周穆王巡狩游行天下。 | 就像周穆王巡视出游天下。 | It is like King Mu of Zhou who toured the whole land. |
| 若加凶杀克世，或世动入死墓空绝，则如虞舜南巡，崩于苍梧之野。 | 如果再加上凶煞来克世，或者世爻发动变入死、墓、空、绝，就像虞舜南巡，死在苍梧的原野上。 | If a baleful spirit is added that controls the Self, or the Self moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is like Shun of Yu who went south on a tour and died in the wilds of Cangwu. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1476 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1476*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦遇游魂不宜迁都巡狩，若加凶煞克世，或世爻动化墓绝，如虞舜南巡崩于苍梧之野。 | 卦遇到游魂，不适合迁都、巡视；如果再加凶煞来克世，或者世爻发动变入墓、绝，就像虞舜南巡，死在苍梧的原野上。 | If the hexagram is a Wandering Soul hexagram it is not fit to move the capital or go on a tour; if a baleful spirit is added that controls the Self, or the Self line moves and turns into tomb or extinction, it is like Shun of Yu who went south on a tour and died in the wilds of Cangwu. |

> **按**:旧注把游魂卦读作「迁都游荡巡狩之象」，只是一个象；卜筮正宗读作「不宜迁都巡狩」，是一条忌讳。两处说法的轻重不完全一样。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary reads a Wandering Soul hexagram as 'the image of moving the capital, wandering and touring', which is only an image; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong reads it as 'not fit for moving the capital or touring', which is a prohibition. The weight is not quite the same.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1476 行:游魂遇空，虞舜南巡不返
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:游魂遇空，虞舜南巡不返

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.71

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.71 国朝·归魂带煞，始皇返国亡身（旧注：归魂卦遇凶神恶杀动克世身，如始皇求仙海上，返国崩于沙丘之野）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:国君自占得归魂卦，又遇凶神恶煞发动来克世爻或卦身，就像秦始皇到海上求仙，回国后死在沙丘。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the ruler casts for himself, a Returning Soul hexagram that meets baleful spirits moving to control the Self or the Body is like the First Emperor of Qin, who sought immortals by the sea and died at Shaqiu after returning to his state.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5840 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5840*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 归魂带煞，始皇返国亡身。 | 归魂卦带着凶煞，就像秦始皇回到国都途中丧了命。 | If a Returning Soul hexagram carries a baleful spirit, it is like the First Emperor who returned to his state and lost his life. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5841 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5841*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若归魂卦，遇凶神恶杀，动克世身，如始皇求仙海上，返国崩于沙丘之野。 | 如果是归魂卦，遇到凶神恶煞发动来克世爻或卦身，就像秦始皇在海上求仙，回国后死在沙丘的原野上。 | If it is a Returning Soul hexagram and meets baleful spirits that move and control the Self or the Body, it is like the First Emperor who sought immortals by the sea and died in the wilds of Shaqiu after returning to his state. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1478 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1478*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若归魂卦遇动爻克世身，如始皇求仙海上，返国崩于沙丘。 | 如果是归魂卦，遇到动爻克世爻或卦身，就像秦始皇在海上求仙，回国后死在沙丘。 | If it is a Returning Soul hexagram and meets a moving line that controls the Self or the Body, it is like the First Emperor who sought immortals by the sea and died at Shaqiu after returning to his state. |

> **按**:旧注要「凶神恶杀」发动克世身；卜筮正宗只说「遇动爻克世身」，没有说凶神恶煞。「世身」按世爻与卦身两者读。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary requires baleful spirits moving to control the Self and Body; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says only 'meets a moving line that controls the Self and Body', without baleful spirits. 'Self and Body' is read as the Self line and the Body together.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1478 行:归魂带煞，始皇返国亡身
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:归魂带煞，始皇返国亡身

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.70

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.72 国朝·子发逢空，张子房起归山之计（旧注：子孙为臣，逢空动或四爻臣位空动，更被世爻克害，是君欲害臣、臣欲避君，如张良托辟谷弃职从赤松子游）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙是臣。子孙逢空发动，或者第四爻（臣位）落空而动、又被世爻克害，是国君想害臣子、臣子想躲开国君，就像张良借口辟谷、辞去官职，跟随赤松子出游。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output is the minister. If Output meets Void and moves, or the fourth line, the ministers' seat, is Void and moves and is also controlled and harmed by the Self, the ruler wants to harm the minister and the minister wants to avoid the ruler, like Zhang Liang, who pleaded fasting from grain, gave up office and went roaming with Chi Songzi.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5843 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5843*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子发逢空，张子房起归山之计； | 子孙发动逢到空，就像张子房起了退隐归山的打算； | If Output moves and meets Void, it is like Zhang Zifang forming the plan to retire to the hills; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5844 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5844*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙为臣。 | 子孙是臣子。 | Output is the minister. |
| 若逢空动，或四爻臣位空动，更被世爻克害，必是君欲害臣，臣欲避君。 | 如果逢空发动，或者第四爻臣位空动，又被世爻克害，必定是国君想害臣子，臣子想躲开国君。 | If it meets Void and moves, or the fourth line, the ministers' seat, is Void and moves, and is also controlled and harmed by the Self, the ruler surely wants to harm the minister and the minister wants to avoid the ruler. |
| 如汉张良，托为辟谷，弃职从赤松子游也。 | 就像汉朝的张良，假托辟谷不食，放弃官职跟随赤松子出游。 | It is like Zhang Liang of Han, who gave fasting from grain as his pretext, abandoned his post and went roaming with Chi Songzi. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1480 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1480*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 他宫子孙为臣，若逢空动被世克害，必是君欲害臣，如汉张良弃职从赤松子游也。 | 别的宫的子孙是臣子，如果逢空发动、被世克害，必定是国君想害臣子，就像汉朝张良弃官跟随赤松子出游。 | The Output of another palace is the minister; if it meets Void and moves and is controlled and harmed by the Self, the ruler surely wants to harm the minister, like Zhang Liang of Han who gave up office and went roaming with Chi Songzi. |

> **按**:子孙有两种：本宫的子孙是太子（见「日辰拱扶子位」一联），别的宫的子孙是臣。卜筮正宗在这一联明说「他宫子孙为臣」；卜筮大全旧注这里只写「子孙为臣」，到「日辰拱扶子位」才分他宫与本宫。
>
> **Note:** Output comes in two kinds: the Output of the home palace is the crown prince (see the couplet 'the Day supports Output's place'), and the Output of another palace is the minister. The Bu Shi Zheng Zong says so outright here, 'the Output of another palace is the minister'; the old commentary in the Bu Shi Da Quan writes only 'Output is the minister' here and draws the line between another palace and the home palace at 'the Day supports Output's place'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1480 行:子发逢空，张子房起归山之计
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:子发逢空，张子房起归山之计

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.75

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.73 国朝·将星被害，岳武穆抱吁天之冤（旧注：将星看日支，临财子必忠良智勇之将，值官鬼白虎强悍，临父是老将、遇兄庸常；将星持鬼克世如曹操桓温篡位，被动爻克害如岳飞遇秦桧）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:将星按占卦那天的地支来找，例如寅、午、戌日占，午爻就是将星。将星临妻财、子孙，必是忠良有智勇的将领；值官鬼、白虎，是强悍的将领；临父母爻，是年老的将领；遇兄弟，不过是平庸的将领。将星持官鬼来克害世爻，就像曹操、桓温挟天子以令诸侯，往往篡位自立；将星被动爻或第四爻动来克害，就像岳飞遇到秦桧这样的权奸而被害死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Find the General star from the day branch: on a Yin, Wu or Xu day, the Wu line is the General star, and so on for the others. A General star on Wealth or Output means a loyal, able, wise and brave general; meeting Pressure or the White Tiger, a fierce and stubborn one; on Resource, an old general; meeting Peer, no more than a commonplace one. If the General star holds Pressure and controls and harms the Self, it is like Cao Cao and Huan Wen, who held the emperor and gave orders to the lords and mostly ended by usurping the throne; if the General star is controlled and harmed by a moving line, or by a moving fourth line, it is like Yue Fei of Song, who was killed by the harm of the powerful and treacherous Qin Hui.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5846 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5846*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 将星被害，岳武穆抱吁天之冤。 | 将星被克害，就像岳武穆含着向天呼冤的冤屈。 | If the General star is harmed, it is like Yue Wumu who bore a grievance he could only cry out to heaven. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5847 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5847*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 将星，如寅午戌日卜，午爻为将星是。 | 将星，比如在寅、午、戌日占卜，午爻就是将星。 | As for the General star, if the cast is made on a Yin, Wu or Xu day, the Wu line is the General star. |
| 余类推。 | 其余的依此类推。 | The rest follow by analogy. |
| 若将星临财子，必得忠良智勇之将。 | 如果将星临在妻财、子孙上，必定得到忠良而有智有勇的将领。 | If the General star is on Wealth or Output, one will surely get a loyal, good, wise and brave general. |
| 将星值官鬼白虎，必强悍之将。 | 将星遇到官鬼、白虎，必定是强悍的将领。 | If the General star meets Pressure or the White Tiger, he is surely a fierce and stubborn general. |
| 若临父，必老将； | 如果临在父爻上，必定是年老的将领； | If it is on Resource, he is surely an old general; |
| 遇兄，不过庸常之将。 | 遇到兄弟，不过是平庸寻常的将领。 | if it meets Peer, he is no more than a commonplace general. |
| 若将星持鬼，克害世爻，必如曹操桓温，挟天子以令诸侯，多致篡位自立。 | 如果将星持着官鬼，克害世爻，必定像曹操、桓温那样，挟持天子来号令诸侯，多半导致篡位自立。 | If the General star holds Pressure and controls and harms the Self, it is surely like Cao Cao and Huan Wen, who held the emperor to command the lords and mostly ended by usurping the throne and setting themselves up. |
| 若将星被动爻克害，或被四爻动克，必如宋岳飞，遇秦桧权奸之害而死也。 | 如果将星被动爻克害，或者被第四爻发动来克，必定像宋朝的岳飞，遇到秦桧这样的权奸迫害而死。 | If the General star is controlled and harmed by a moving line, or controlled by a moving fourth line, it is surely like Yue Fei of Song, who was killed by the harm of the powerful and treacherous Qin Hui. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1482 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1482*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 将星，寅午戌日卜，午爻为将星，其余类推。 | 将星是这样找的：寅、午、戌日占卜，午爻就是将星，其余依此类推。 | As for the General star, on a Yin, Wu or Xu day the Wu line is the General star, and the rest follow by analogy. |
| 若将星临财子，必得忠职智勇，值官鬼白虎，必强悍之将。 | 如果将星临在妻财、子孙上，必定得到尽忠职守、有智有勇的将领；遇到官鬼、白虎，必定是强悍的将领。 | If the General star is on Wealth or Output, one will surely get a general who is loyal to his duty, wise and brave; if it meets Pressure or the White Tiger, he is surely a fierce and stubborn general. |
| 若将星持鬼克害世爻，恐有造谋之变； | 如果将星持着官鬼，克害世爻，恐怕会有图谋作乱的变故； | If the General star holds Pressure and controls and harms the Self, there may be an upheaval of plotting and scheming; |
| 若将星被动爻克害，如岳武穆遇秦桧之害也。 | 如果将星被动爻克害，就像岳武穆遇到秦桧的陷害。 | if the General star is controlled and harmed by a moving line, it is like Yue Wumu meeting the harm of Qin Hui. |

> **按**:将星持鬼克世的结果，旧注举曹操、桓温挟天子篡位；卜筮正宗收成「恐有造谋之变」，没有举人名。被克害那一句，旧注还多一个「被四爻动克」。
>
> **Note:** For a General star holding Pressure and controlling the Self, the old commentary gives Cao Cao and Huan Wen holding the emperor and usurping; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong boils it down to 'there may be an upheaval of plotting' with no names. For the harmed General star, the old commentary adds 'or controlled by a moving fourth line'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1482 行:将星被害，岳武穆抱吁天之冤
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 370 行:将星被害，岳武穆抱吁天之冤

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.72

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.74 国朝·应旺生合世爻，圣主得椒房之助（旧注：应爻为皇后，旺相生合世爻又临财福，皇后美貌智略、天性慈仁，能规君过、导君以善，如汉马后、宋宣仁）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:应爻是皇后。应爻旺相、生合世爻，又临妻财或子孙，皇后相貌美、有智谋、天性仁慈，能规劝国君的过失，引导国君向善，像汉朝的马皇后、宋朝的宣仁皇后。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Other line is the empress. If the Other line is thriving or strong, generates or combines with the Self, and sits on Wealth or Output, the empress is beautiful, wise and kind by nature, and can correct the ruler's faults and lead him to the good, like Empress Ma of Han and Empress Xuanren of Song.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5849 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5849*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应旺生合世爻，圣主得椒房之助； | 应爻旺相、生合世爻，圣明的君主就能得到皇后的帮助； | If the Other line is thriving and generates or combines with the Self, the sage ruler gains the help of the Pepper Chamber (the empress's quarters); |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5851–5852 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5851–5852*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应爻，皇后之位。 | 应爻是皇后的位置。 | The Other line is the empress's place. |
| 若应爻旺相，生合世爻，更临财福； | 如果应爻旺相，生合世爻，又临在妻财、福神上； | If the Other line is thriving or strong, generates or combines with the Self, and sits on Wealth or Output; |
| 主皇后美貌智略，天性慈仁； | 主皇后容貌美丽、有智谋才略，天性慈爱仁厚； | it means the empress is beautiful, wise and capable, and kind and benevolent by nature; |
| 能规君过，导君以善。 | 能规劝国君的过失，引导国君做善事。 | she can correct the ruler's faults and lead him to the good. |
| 如汉马后。 | 就像汉朝的马皇后。 | Such was Empress Ma of Han. |
| 宋宜仁，称女中尧舜也。 | 宋朝的宣仁（这里原文作「宜仁」）皇后，被称作女中的尧舜。 | Empress Xuanren of Song (written Yiren here) was called a Yao and Shun among women. |
| 后房用椒涂壁，以辟除邪气。 | 皇后的居室用花椒涂在墙壁上，用来驱除邪气。 | The empress's chambers had pepper plastered on the walls, to drive away evil influences. |
| 故曰椒房。 | 所以叫椒房。 | Hence the name Pepper Chamber. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1484 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1484*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应为皇后，若旺相合世爻，更临财福，主后智略仁慈，导君以善，如汉马后、宋宣仁，为女中尧舜是也。 | 应爻是皇后，如果旺相、合世爻，又临妻财、福神，主皇后有智谋才略、仁慈，引导国君向善，像汉朝的马皇后、宋朝的宣仁皇后，就是女中的尧舜。 | The Other line is the empress; if it is thriving or strong and combines with the Self, and further sits on Wealth or Output, the empress is wise, capable and kind and leads the ruler to the good, like Empress Ma of Han and Empress Xuanren of Song, who were the Yao and Shun among women. |

> **按**:宋朝那位皇后，卜筮大全的旧注写「宜仁」，卜筮正宗写「宣仁」；译文按「宣仁」读（宋朝被称作「女中尧舜」的宣仁太后）。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary in the Bu Shi Da Quan writes the Song empress as 'Yiren' and the Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes 'Xuanren'; the translation reads 'Xuanren' (the Song empress dowager Xuanren who was called 'the Yao and Shun among women').

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1484 行:应旺生合世爻，圣主得椒房之助
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:应旺生合世爻，圣主得椒房之助

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.69

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.75 国朝·日辰拱扶子位，东宫摄天子之权（旧注：他宫子孙旺相又得日辰生扶、世值胎养空绝，主幼主在位、大臣摄政，如周公辅成王；本宫子孙生旺得日辰扶助，国君将传位，太子当国）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:别的宫的子孙旺相，又遇日辰生扶拱合，而世爻落在胎、养、空、绝上，主国君年幼，有大臣摄政，像周公辅佐成王；本宫的子孙生旺，又得日辰扶助，是国君厌倦了大位、想要传位，太子主持国政、代行天子的事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Output of another palace is thriving or strong and is further generated, supported or combined by the Day, while the Self falls on Womb, Nurture, Void or extinction, the ruler is young and a great minister acts as regent, like the Duke of Zhou assisting King Cheng; if the Output of the home palace is thriving and gets support from the Day, the ruler is tired of the great seat and wants to hand it on, and the crown prince takes charge of the state and acts in the Son of Heaven's stead.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5854 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5854*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日辰拱扶子位，东宫摄天子之权。 | 日辰拱扶子孙之位，东宫（太子）就代行天子的权力。 | If the Day supports and holds up the place of Output, the Eastern Palace takes over the Son of Heaven's authority. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5855–5856 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5855–5856*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙乃臣位。 | 子孙是臣子的位置。 | Output is the ministers' place. |
| 若他宫子孙旺相，更值日辰，生扶拱合，世值胎养空绝，必主少国，疑有大臣摄政。 | 如果别的宫的子孙旺相，又逢日辰生、扶、拱、合，而世爻落在胎、养、空、绝上，必定是年幼的国君在位，大概有大臣摄政。 | If the Output of another palace is thriving or strong and further meets the Day generating, supporting, holding up or combining with it, while the Self falls on Womb, Nurture, Void or extinction, the state surely has a young ruler, and a great minister is probably acting as regent. |
| 如周公辅成王是。 | 就像周公辅佐成王。 | The Duke of Zhou assisting King Cheng is an example. |
| 若本宫子孙生旺，更得日辰扶助，必国君厌弃大宝，将欲传位； | 如果本宫的子孙生旺，又得到日辰扶助，必定是国君厌倦了帝位，想要传位； | If the Output of the home palace is thriving and gets support from the Day, the ruler is surely tired of the throne and wants to hand it on; |
| 太子当国，摄天子事也。 | 太子主持国政，代行天子的事务。 | the crown prince takes charge of the state and acts for the Son of Heaven. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1486 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1486*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若本宫子孙生旺，更得日辰扶助，欲传位太子，当国摄天子事也。 | 如果本宫的子孙生旺，又得到日辰扶助，是想要传位给太子，太子主持国政，代行天子的事务。 | If the Output of the home palace is thriving and gets support from the Day, the ruler wants to hand the throne to the crown prince, who takes charge of the state and acts for the Son of Heaven. |

> **按**:旧注把子孙分成别的宫的（臣子，幼主在位、大臣摄政）和本宫的（太子）两种；卜筮正宗只收了本宫子孙这一半。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary divides Output into that of another palace (the minister: a young ruler on the throne and a great minister as regent) and that of the home palace (the crown prince); the Bu Shi Zheng Zong keeps only the home palace half.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1486 行:日辰拱扶子位，东宫摄天子之权
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:日辰拱扶子位，东宫摄天子之权

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.72、LY.CAREER.HJC.76

## LY.CAREER.HJC.76 国朝·世克福爻，唐玄宗有杀儿之事（旧注：本宫子孙被四爻冲克刑害，必被谗臣谤讪；世克本宫子孙而子孙又入死墓空绝，太子遇谗被害，如唐玄宗信李林甫之谮废杀太子瑛等）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:本宫的子孙（太子）被第四爻冲、克、刑、害，必定被进谗的臣子诽谤；世爻克本宫的子孙，子孙又落入死、墓、空、绝，太子遭谗言陷害，就像唐玄宗听信李林甫的谗言，把太子瑛、鄂王瑶、光王琚都废为平民，后来又赐死在城东驿。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Output of the home palace (the crown prince) is clashed, controlled, punished or harmed by the fourth line, he is surely slandered by treacherous ministers; if the Self controls the Output of the home palace and that Output also falls into death, tomb, Void or extinction, the crown prince meets slander and is harmed, like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang, who trusted Li Linfu's slander, demoted Crown Prince Ying, Prince E (Yao) and Prince Guang (Ju) to commoners and later granted them death at the Chengdong post station.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5858 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5858*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世克福爻，唐玄宗有杀儿之事； | 世爻克福神，就像唐玄宗有杀儿子的事； | If the Self controls the Output line, it is like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang killing his sons; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5859 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5859*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若本宫子孙，被四爻冲克刑害，必被谗臣谤讪。 | 如果本宫的子孙被第四爻冲、克、刑、害，必定被进谗的臣子诽谤讥讽。 | If the Output of the home palace is clashed, controlled, punished or harmed by the fourth line, he is surely slandered and mocked by treacherous ministers. |
| 若世克本宫子孙，子孙又入死墓空绝，必太子遇谗被害。 | 如果世爻克本宫的子孙，子孙又落入死、墓、空、绝，必定是太子遭到谗言陷害。 | If the Self controls the Output of the home palace and that Output also falls into death, tomb, Void or extinction, the crown prince is surely harmed by slander. |
| 如唐玄宗信李林甫谮，将太子瑛，鄂王瑶，光王琚，皆废为庶人，后复赐死于城东驿也。 | 就像唐玄宗听信李林甫的谗言，把太子瑛、鄂王瑶、光王琚都废为平民，后来又赐他们死在城东驿。 | It is like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang who believed Li Linfu's slander, demoted Crown Prince Ying, Prince E (Yao) and Prince Guang (Ju) all to commoners, and later granted them death at the Chengdong post station. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1488 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1488*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若世克本宫之墓绝子孙，太子遇谗被害，如唐玄宗信李林甫谱，将太子、英鄂五瑶、光王琚皆废，复赐死。 | 如果世爻克本宫落在墓、绝上的子孙，太子遭到谗言陷害，就像唐玄宗听信李林甫的谗言，把太子、瑛、鄂王瑶、光王琚都废掉，又赐他们死。 | If the Self controls the Output of the home palace that is in tomb or extinction, the crown prince meets slander and is harmed, like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang who trusted Li Linfu's slander, deposed the crown prince, Ying, Prince E (Yao) and Prince Guang (Ju), and later granted them death. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗这一句有错字：「谮」写成了「谱」，「瑛、鄂王瑶」写成了「英鄂五瑶」；译文按旧注读。另外旧注有「四爻冲克刑害子孙」和「世克子孙」两种情形，卜筮正宗只收了后一种。
>
> **Note:** The Bu Shi Zheng Zong has miswritten characters in this sentence: 'slander' is written with a wrong character, and 'Ying and Prince E (Yao)' is written as a garbled string; the translation follows the old commentary. The old commentary also has two cases, the fourth line attacking Output and the Self controlling Output; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong keeps only the second.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1488 行:世克福爻，唐玄宗有杀儿之事
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:世克福爻，唐玄宗有杀儿之事

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.75、LY.CAREER.HJC.77

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.77 国朝·子伤君位，隋杨广有弑父之心（旧注：本宫子孙带杀旺动克害世爻，是太子有篡位之兆，如隋杨广弑父自立）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:本宫的子孙带着凶煞、旺相发动、克害世爻，是太子有篡位的征兆，就像隋朝的杨广弑杀父亲、自立为皇帝。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Output of the home palace carries a baleful spirit, is thriving and moves, and controls and harms the Self, the crown prince shows signs of usurping the throne, like Yang Guang of Sui who killed his father and set himself up as emperor.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5861 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5861*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子伤君位，隋杨广有弑父之心。 | 子孙伤克君位，就像隋朝的杨广有杀父的心思。 | If Output injures the ruler's place, it is like Yang Guang of Sui who had it in his heart to kill his father. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5862 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5862*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本宫子孙，带杀旺动，克害世爻，乃太子有篡位之兆。 | 本宫的子孙带着凶煞、旺相发动，克害世爻，是太子有篡位的征兆。 | If the Output of the home palace carries a baleful spirit, is thriving and moves, and controls and harms the Self, it is a sign that the crown prince will usurp the throne. |
| 如隋杨广弑父，自立为帝。 | 就像隋朝的杨广弑父，自立为皇帝。 | It is like Yang Guang of Sui who killed his father and set himself up as emperor. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1490 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1490*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本宫子孙旺动克害世爻，乃太子有篡位之兆，如隋杨广弑父自立为帝。 | 本宫的子孙旺相发动、克害世爻，是太子有篡位的征兆，就像隋朝的杨广弑父自立为皇帝。 | If the Output of the home palace is thriving and moves and controls and harms the Self, it is a sign that the crown prince will usurp the throne, like Yang Guang of Sui who killed his father and set himself up as emperor. |

> **按**:旧注比卜筮正宗多「带杀」一个条件。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary has one more condition than the Bu Shi Zheng Zong: 'carrying a baleful spirit'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1490 行:子伤君位，隋杨广有弑父之心
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:子伤君位，隋杨广有弑父之心

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.76、LY.CAREER.HJC.78

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.78 国朝·一卦无孙，宋仁宗有绝嗣之叹（旧注：卦中无子孙，或子孙休囚、动入墓绝空死，必是国无太子，如宋仁宗无子而叹）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里没有子孙，或者子孙休囚，又发动落入墓、绝、空、死，必定是国家没有太子，就像宋仁宗没有儿子而叹息。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If there is no Output in the hexagram, or Output is resting or trapped and moves into tomb, extinction, Void or death, the state has no crown prince, like Emperor Renzong of Song who sighed because he had no son.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5864 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5864*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 一卦无孙，宋仁宗有绝嗣之叹， | 整个卦里没有子孙，就像宋仁宗有绝了后嗣的叹息， | If there is no Output in the whole hexagram, it is like Emperor Renzong of Song sighing that his line would end, |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5865 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5865*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中无子孙，或子孙休囚，动入墓绝空死，必是国无太子。 | 卦中没有子孙，或者子孙休囚，发动后落入墓、绝、空、死，必定是国家没有太子。 | If there is no Output in the hexagram, or Output is resting or trapped and moves into tomb, extinction, Void or death, the state surely has no crown prince. |
| 如宋仁宗无子而叹。 | 就像宋仁宗没有儿子而叹息。 | It is like Emperor Renzong of Song who sighed because he had no son. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1492 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1492*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中无子孙或子孙休囚动入墓绝，必是国无太子，如宋仁宗无子而叹。 | 卦中没有子孙，或者子孙休囚、发动落入墓、绝，必定是国家没有太子，就像宋仁宗没有儿子而叹息。 | If there is no Output in the hexagram, or Output is resting or trapped and moves into tomb or extinction, the state surely has no crown prince, like Emperor Renzong of Song who sighed because he had no son. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1492 行:一卦无孙，宋仁宗有绝嗣之叹
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:一卦无孙，宋仁宗有绝嗣之叹

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.77、LY.CAREER.HJC.79

## LY.CAREER.HJC.79 国朝·四爻克子，秦扶苏中赵高之谋（旧注：四爻为臣位，旺动伤克本宫子孙，如秦太子扶苏被赵高矫诏赐死）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:第四爻是臣位。它旺相发动、伤克本宫的子孙（太子），就像秦朝的太子扶苏被赵高假传诏书赐死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The fourth line is the ministers' seat. If it is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Output of the home palace (the crown prince), it is like Fusu, the crown prince of Qin, who was ordered to die by Zhao Gao's forged edict.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5867 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5867*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四爻克子，秦扶苏中赵高之谋。 | 第四爻克子孙，就像秦朝的扶苏中了赵高的计谋。 | If the fourth line controls Output, it is like Fusu of Qin falling into Zhao Gao's scheme. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5868 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5868*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四爻，乃臣位。 | 第四爻是臣子的位置。 | The fourth line is the ministers' seat. |
| 若旺动，伤克本宫子孙，则如秦太子扶苏，被赵高矫诏赐死。 | 如果它旺相发动，伤克本宫的子孙，就像秦朝的太子扶苏，被赵高假传诏书赐死。 | If it is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Output of the home palace, it is like Fusu, the crown prince of Qin, who was ordered to die by Zhao Gao's forged edict. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1494 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1494*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四爻乃臣位，若旺动伤克本宫子孙，则如秦太子扶苏被赵高矫诏赐死。 | 第四爻是臣子的位置，如果旺相发动、伤克本宫的子孙，就像秦朝的太子扶苏被赵高假传诏书赐死。 | The fourth line is the ministers' seat; if it is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Output of the home palace, it is like Fusu, the crown prince of Qin, who was ordered to die by Zhao Gao's forged edict. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1494 行:四爻克子，秦扶苏中赵高之谋
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:四爻克子，秦扶苏中赵高之谋

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.76、LY.CAREER.HJC.78

## LY.CAREER.HJC.80 国朝·身值动官，唐太宗禁庭蹀血（旧注：世身持官带杀旺动，必至杀克兄弟、兄弟不相容，如唐太宗伏兵玄武门射死建成元吉）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻或卦身持着官鬼，带着凶煞、旺相发动，必定发展到杀害兄弟，是兄弟不能相容的征兆，就像唐太宗在玄武门埋伏士兵，射死建成、元吉，血流满了宫中。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self or the Body holds Pressure with a baleful spirit and is thriving and moving, it ends in killing brothers; it is a sign that brothers cannot tolerate each other, like Taizong of Tang, who set troops in ambush at the Xuanwu Gate and shot Jiancheng and Yuanji dead, so that blood flowed in the palace court.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5870 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5870*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身值动官，唐太宗禁庭蹀血； | 卦身遇到发动的官，就像唐太宗在禁宫的庭院里血流遍地； | If the Body meets a moving Pressure line, it is like Taizong of Tang with blood trampled in the palace court; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5871 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5871*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世身持官，带杀旺动，必至杀克兄弟，乃兄弟必不相容之兆。 | 世爻或卦身持着官，带着凶煞、旺相发动，必定发展到杀害兄弟，这是兄弟必定不能相容的征兆。 | If the Self or the Body holds Pressure, carries a baleful spirit and is thriving and moving, it surely comes to the killing of brothers, a sign that brothers cannot tolerate each other. |
| 如唐太宗，伏兵玄武门，将建成，元吉射死，血流禁庭，而被军马蹀践也。 | 就像唐太宗，在玄武门埋伏士兵，把建成、元吉射死，血流满了禁宫的庭院，被军马践踏。 | It is like Taizong of Tang, who set troops in ambush at the Xuanwu Gate, shot Jiancheng and Yuanji dead, and the blood flowed in the palace court and was trampled by the soldiers' horses. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1496 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1496*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身世持官带杀旺动，必至杀克兄弟，如唐太宗伏兵玄武门，射死建臣元吉，血流禁庭马蹀践也。 | 世爻、卦身持着官，带着凶煞、旺相发动，必定发展到杀害兄弟，就像唐太宗在玄武门埋伏士兵，射死建成、元吉，血流满了宫廷、被马践踏。 | If the Self and the Body hold Pressure, carry a baleful spirit and are thriving and moving, it surely comes to the killing of brothers, like Taizong of Tang who set troops in ambush at the Xuanwu Gate and shot Jiancheng and Yuanji dead, so that blood flowed in the palace court and was trampled by the horses. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗此处写「射死建臣元吉」，「建臣」应是旧注的「建成」，译文按旧注读。
>
> **Note:** The Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes 'shot dead Jianchen Yuanji' here; 'Jianchen' is read as the 'Jiancheng' of the old commentary, and the translation follows the old commentary.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1496 行:身值动官，唐太宗禁庭蹀血
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:身值动官，唐太宗禁庭蹀血

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.81

## LY.CAREER.HJC.81 国朝·世安空弟，周泰伯让国逃荆（旧注：世持兄弟空亡、与应相生合、更有吉神动克，是兄弟推让天位之象，如泰伯托为采药逃往荆蛮、让位季历）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻持着落空亡的兄弟，和应爻相生相合，又有吉神发动来克，是兄弟之间互相推让天子之位的象，就像泰伯假托采药逃到荆蛮，把位置让给季历。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self holds a Void Peer line, generating or combining with the Other, and an auspicious spirit also moves to control, it is the image of brothers yielding the throne to one another, like Taibo, who on the pretext of gathering herbs fled to the Jing barbarians and gave the succession to Jili.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5873 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5873*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世安空弟，周泰伯让国逃荆。 | 世爻安在落空的兄弟上，就像周朝的泰伯让国、逃到荆地。 | If the Self sits on a Void brother, it is like Taibo of Zhou who yielded the state and fled to Jing. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5874 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5874*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持兄弟空亡，与应相生合，更有吉神动克，必是兄弟推让天位之象。 | 世爻持着兄弟且落空亡，和应爻相生相合，又有吉神发动来克，必定是兄弟互相推让天子之位的象。 | If the Self holds the Peer line and it is Void, generating or combining with the Other, and an auspicious spirit also moves to control, it is surely the image of brothers yielding the throne to one another. |
| 如周泰伯托为采药，逃之荆蛮，让位季历也。 | 就像周朝的泰伯假托去采药，逃到荆蛮之地，把位置让给季历。 | It is like Taibo of Zhou, who on the pretext of gathering herbs fled to the Jing and Man peoples and yielded the throne to Jili. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1498 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1498*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持空弟，与应爻合，有吉神动克，是兄弟推让天位之象，如周泰伯托为采药逃之荆，蛮让位季历也。 | 世爻持着落空的兄弟，和应爻相合，有吉神发动来克，是兄弟互相推让天子之位的象，就像周朝的泰伯假托去采药，逃到荆蛮之地，把位置让给季历。 | If the Self holds a Void brother and combines with the Other, and an auspicious spirit moves to control, it is the image of brothers yielding the throne, like Taibo of Zhou who on the pretext of gathering herbs fled to the Jing and Man peoples and yielded the throne to Jili. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗原文「逃之荆，蛮让位季历」中间的逗号是标点之误，应连读为「荆蛮」，译文按此读。
>
> **Note:** In the Bu Shi Zheng Zong the comma in 'fled to Jing, Man yielded the place to Jili' is a punctuation slip; 'Jing and Man' is to be read together, and the translation reads it so.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1498 行:世安空弟，周泰伯让国逃荆
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 371 行:世安空弟，周泰伯让国逃荆

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.80、LY.CAREER.HJC.82

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.82 国朝·凶神生合世神，玄宗信林甫之佞（旧注：鬼杀发动生合世爻，必是佞臣阿谀、人君信任，如唐玄宗信任李林甫十九年，养成天下大乱）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼、凶煞发动来生合世爻，必定是奸佞的臣子阿谀奉承、国君信任他，就像唐玄宗信任李林甫十九年，养成了天下大乱。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Pressure line or a baleful spirit moves to generate or combine with the Self, a treacherous minister is surely flattering the ruler and the ruler trusts him, like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang who trusted Li Linfu for nineteen years and bred great disorder in the realm.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5876 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5876*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凶神生合世神，玄宗信林甫之佞； | 凶神生合世神，就像唐玄宗相信李林甫的奸佞； | If a baleful spirit generates or combines with the Self, it is like Xuanzong believing in the treachery of Li Linfu; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5877 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5877*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若鬼杀动，生合世爻，必是佞臣阿谀，人君信任。 | 如果官鬼、凶煞发动，生合世爻，必定是奸佞的臣子阿谀奉承，国君信任他们。 | If a Pressure line or a baleful spirit moves and generates or combines with the Self, a treacherous minister is surely flattering the ruler, and the ruler trusts him. |
| 如唐玄宗，信任李林甫一十九年，养成天下大乱。 | 就像唐玄宗，信任李林甫一十九年，养成了天下大乱。 | It is like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang, who trusted Li Linfu for nineteen years and bred great disorder in the realm. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1500 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1500*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若鬼煞动来生合世爻，必是佞臣阿谀，人君信任，如唐玄宗信任李林甫一十九年，养成天下大乱。 | 如果官鬼、凶煞发动来生合世爻，必定是奸佞的臣子阿谀奉承，国君信任他，就像唐玄宗信任李林甫一十九年，养成了天下大乱。 | If a Pressure line or a baleful spirit moves to generate or combine with the Self, a treacherous minister is surely flattering the ruler and the ruler trusts him, like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang who trusted Li Linfu for nineteen years and bred great disorder in the realm. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 1500 行:凶神生合世爻，玄宗信林甫之佞
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 372 行:凶神生合世神，玄宗信林甫之佞

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.81、LY.CAREER.HJC.83

## LY.CAREER.HJC.83 国朝·君位克伤四位，商纣害比干之忠（旧注：四爻为臣，持财子而被世爻动克，是犯颜直谏之臣遇剖心之主，如比干尽忠被纣王所杀）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:第四爻是臣。四爻持着妻财或子孙，却被世爻发动来克，是敢于冒犯君主、当面直谏的臣子，遇到了剖人心的君主，就像比干尽忠而被商纣王杀害。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The fourth line is the minister. If the fourth line holds Wealth or Output and is moved and controlled by the Self, it is a minister who dares to offend the ruler's face with plain advice meeting a ruler who cuts out hearts, like Bi Gan, who was loyal to the end and was put to death by King Zhou of Shang.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5879 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5879*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 君位克伤四位，商纣害比干之忠。 | 君位克伤第四爻，就像商纣王加害比干的忠心。 | If the ruler's place controls and injures the fourth place, it is like King Zhou of Shang harming the loyalty of Bi Gan. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5880 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5880*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四爻为臣。 | 第四爻是臣。 | The fourth line is the minister. |
| 持财子，而被世爻动克，必犯颜之臣，遭剖心之主。 | 它持着财、子孙，却被世爻发动来克，必定是敢于冒犯君主颜面直谏的臣子，遇上了剖人心的君主。 | If it holds Wealth or Output and is moved and controlled by the Self, he is surely a minister who dares to offend the ruler's face and meets a ruler who cuts out hearts. |
| 有如比干之尽忠，而被纣王之诛也。 | 就像比干尽忠，却被纣王杀害。 | It is like Bi Gan, who was loyal to the end and was put to death by King Zhou. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1502 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1502*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四爻为臣，持财子而被君爻克伤，如比干之尽忠，而被纣王之诛也。 | 第四爻是臣，持着财、子孙而被君爻克伤，就像比干尽忠，却被纣王杀害。 | The fourth line is the minister; if it holds Wealth or Output and is controlled and injured by the ruler's line, it is like Bi Gan, who was loyal to the end and was put to death by King Zhou. |

> **按**:旧注说四爻「被世爻动克」，卜筮正宗说「被君爻克伤」；前面分派各爻时，君爻是太岁，世爻不是，两处用的不是同一爻。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says the fourth line is 'moved and controlled by the Self', the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says it is 'controlled and injured by the ruler's line'; in the assignment of the lines the ruler's line is Tai Sui and not the Self, so the two do not use the same line.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1502 行:君位克伤四位，商纣害比干之忠
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.85) 第 372 行:君位伤克四位，商纣害比干之忠

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.79、LY.CAREER.HJC.82

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.84 国朝·离宫变入坎宫带凶杀，而徽钦亡身于漠北（旧注：卦中大象皆凶、世应克害刑冲，或世动入死墓空绝，是国君遭患死亡之兆；离南坎北，离化坎由南入北，如宋徽钦二宗被金所掳老死沙漠）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:整个卦象都凶，世爻、应爻互相克害刑冲，或者世爻发动入死、墓、空、绝，是国君遭灾死亡的征兆；离宫变入坎宫，离是南方、坎是北方，由南入北，就像宋朝的徽宗、钦宗被金人掳走，囚在五国城，老死在北方的沙漠地带。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the whole image is bad, the Self and the Other control, harm, punish and clash with each other, or the Self moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is a sign that the ruler will meet calamity and die; a Li-palace hexagram turning into a Kan-palace hexagram goes from south to north (Li is south, Kan is north), like the Song emperors Huizong and Qinzong, who were carried off by the Jin, held at Wuguo City, and grew old and died in the desert land.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5882 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5882*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 离宫变入坎宫带凶杀，而徽钦亡身于漠北； | 离宫变入坎宫，又带着凶煞，就像徽宗、钦宗身亡在北方的沙漠； | If a Li-palace hexagram turns into a Kan-palace hexagram and carries baleful spirits, it is like Huizong and Qinzong who lost their lives in the northern desert; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5883 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5883*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中大象皆凶，世应克害刑冲，或世动入死墓空绝，必是国君遭患，死亡之兆。 | 卦中整体的象都是凶的，世爻、应爻互相克害刑冲，或者世爻发动入死、墓、空、绝，必定是国君遭祸患、死亡的征兆。 | If the whole image in the hexagram is bad, and the Self and the Other control, harm, punish and clash with each other, or the Self moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is surely a sign that the ruler will meet calamity and death. |
| 离乃南方，坎乃北方。 | 离是南方，坎是北方。 | Li is the south and Kan is the north. |
| 离化坎，乃南入北。 | 离变化为坎，就是从南入北。 | Li turning into Kan is going from south into north. |
| 故如宋之徽钦二宗，被金兀术所掳，幽于五国城，卒老死于沙漠之地。 | 所以就像宋朝的徽宗、钦宗二帝，被金兀术掳走，囚禁在五国城，最后老死在沙漠地带。 | So it was with the two Song emperors Huizong and Qinzong, who were taken captive by the Jin general Wuzhu, held at Wuguo City, and in the end grew old and died in the desert land. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1504 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1504*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦象凶，世又遭克，或动入墓绝，乃死亡之兆。 | 卦象凶，世爻又被克，或者发动入墓、绝，是死亡的征兆。 | If the image is bad, the Self is also controlled, or it moves into tomb or extinction, it is a sign of death. |
| 离南坎北，离化坎由南入北，如宋徽钦被金所掳，死于漠北。 | 离在南、坎在北，离变化为坎是从南入北，就像宋朝徽宗、钦宗被金掳走，死在北方的沙漠。 | Li is south and Kan is north; Li turning into Kan goes from south into north, as when Huizong and Qinzong of Song were carried off by the Jin and died in the northern desert. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.94) 第 1504 行:离宫变入坎宫，带凶煞而徽钦亡身于漠北
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 372 行:离宫变入坎宫带凶杀，而徽钦亡身于漠北

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.85

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.85 国朝·乾象化为巽象有吉曜，而孙刘鼎足于东南（旧注：乾宫变巽宫，大象皆吉，当如刘备孙权建国东南，与魏三足鼎立）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:乾宫变巽宫，又有吉曜，就像刘备、孙权在东南建国，和魏国三足鼎立。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Qian-palace hexagram turning into a Xun-palace hexagram, with an auspicious star, is like Liu Bei and Sun Quan founding states in the south-east and standing with Wei like the three legs of a tripod.

**《黄金策·国朝》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5885 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The court and the state", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5885*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 乾象化为巽象有吉曜，而孙刘鼎足于东南。 | 乾的象变成巽的象，又有吉曜，孙权、刘备就在东南鼎足而立。 | If the Qian image turns into the Xun image and has an auspicious star, then Sun and Liu stand like the legs of a tripod in the south-east. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5886 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5886*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 乾宫变巽宫，大象皆吉。 | 乾宫变成巽宫，整个卦象都吉。 | If a Qian-palace hexagram turns into a Xun-palace hexagram, the whole image is auspicious. |
| 当如刘先主，吴孙权，建国东南，与魏三足鼎立也。 | 就像刘先主、吴国的孙权，在东南建国，和魏国三足鼎立。 | It is like the First Ruler Liu and Sun Quan of Wu, who founded states in the south-east and stood with Wei like the three legs of a tripod. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1506 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1506*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 乾变巽宫，大象皆凶，若有吉曜，如刘玄德与吴魏三国鼎足而立也。 | 乾变成巽宫，整个卦象都凶，如果有吉曜，就像刘玄德和吴、魏三国鼎足而立。 | If Qian turns into the Xun palace and the whole image is bad but there is an auspicious star, it is like Liu Xuande standing with Wu and Wei as three kingdoms like the legs of a tripod. |

> **打架**:旧注说「乾宫变巽宫，大象皆吉」，卜筮正宗写成「大象皆凶，若有吉曜」。一个说这个象本身吉，一个说象凶、要有吉曜才成。书里没有判哪一个对。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The old commentary says 'when a Qian-palace hexagram turns into a Xun-palace hexagram, the whole image is auspicious'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes 'the whole image is bad, but if there is an auspicious star'. One says the image itself is good, the other that the image is bad and only an auspicious star makes it work. The books do not decide which is right.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1506 行:乾象化为巽象，有吉曜而孙刘鼎足于东南
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 372 行:乾象化为巽象有吉曜，而孙刘鼎足于东南

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.84、LY.CAREER.HJC.63

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.01 国事总则：为国之要必以进忠良、故福爻不可失，必以退奸宄、故鬼象不可动；凡国占皆喜福临而恶鬼动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占国事，要义是进用忠良、为万民造福，所以福爻（子孙）不能失；要退去奸邪、除去四海的疾患，所以鬼象（官鬼）不能动。凡是占国事，都喜欢福神临到、厌恶官鬼发动，这是占国事的大象。本章细讲迁都、建庙、择后、立储、改制、享国各问。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining about the state, the essential is to advance the loyal and good and to bring blessing to the myriad people, so the fortune line, Output, must not be lost; one must also remove the treacherous and rid the four seas of their sickness, so the Pressure image must not move. In every divination about the state one is glad to see the fortune spirit arrive and dislikes Pressure moving, and this is the great image of such divinations. The chapter treats in detail the questions of moving the capital, building a temple, choosing an empress, naming an heir, changing the institutions, and how long the dynasty will last.

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 810 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 810*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内详迁都、建庙、择后、立储、改制、享国诸问 | 本章细讲迁都、建庙、择后、立储、改制、享国各问。 | This chapter treats in detail the questions of moving the capital, building a temple, choosing an empress, naming an heir, changing the institutions, and how long the dynasty will last. |

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 811 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 811*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 为国之要，必以进忠良，为万民之福，故福爻不可失也； | 治国的要义，必定是进用忠良，为万民造福，所以福爻不可失； | The essential of governing a state is surely to advance the loyal and good and to bring blessing to the myriad people, and so the fortune line, Output, must not be lost; |
| 必以退奸宄，去四海之疾，故鬼象不可动也。 | 必定要退去奸邪，除去四海的疾患，所以鬼象不可动。 | one must also drive out the treacherous and rid the four seas of their sickness, and so the Pressure image must not move. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 812 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 812*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡国占皆喜福临，而恶鬼动，此其大象也。 | 凡是占国事，都喜欢福神临到，厌恶官鬼发动，这是大象。 | In every divination about the state one is glad to see the fortune spirit arrive and dislikes Pressure moving; this is the great image. |

> **按**:「福爻」是子孙（福神），「鬼象」是官鬼之象（见术语表）。「奸宄」是奸邪作乱的人。这一章把国事的好坏归在两件事上：福神在不在、官鬼动不动，后面各条是它的展开。
>
> **Note:** 'Fuyao', the fortune line, is Output (the fortune spirit), and 'guixiang', the Pressure image, is the image of Pressure (see the glossary). 'Jiangui' are the treacherous who stir up disorder. The chapter puts the good or ill of the state on two things, whether the fortune spirit is present and whether Pressure moves, and the entries that follow unfold this.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.02、LY.CAREER.EL.01、LY.CAREER.HJC.63、LY.CAREER.YY.31

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.02 国事：内为邦畿、外为人民，卦宫安旺而国祚长、爻象合和而宗社久；冲莫见于正之、合莫被于破害，则天下和平国家奠安；次观旺衰动静合冲

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:内卦是国都和邦畿，外卦是人民；卦宫安稳旺相，国运就长；爻象和合，宗庙社稷就长久。冲不要出现在正卦和之卦（本卦和变卦）里，合不要被破、被害，这样天下就和平，国家就安定了。接着还要再看旺衰、动静、合冲。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The inner trigram is the capital and its domain and the outer is the people: if the palace of the hexagram is secure and thriving the dynasty is long, and if the lines combine harmoniously the ancestral temple and altars last long. If clash does not appear in the original or the changed hexagram, and a combination is not broken or harmed, the world is at peace and the state is secure. Next look at strength and weakness, movement and stillness, combination and clash.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 813–815 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, lines 813–815*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 是以内为邦畿，外为人民， | 所以内卦是国都邦畿，外卦是人民； | So the inner trigram is the capital and its domain and the outer trigram is the people; |
| 卦宫安旺而国祚长，爻象合和而宗社久； | 卦宫安稳旺相，国运就长；爻象和合，宗庙社稷就长久； | if the palace of the hexagram is secure and thriving the dynasty is long, and if the lines combine harmoniously the ancestral temple and altars last long; |
| 冲莫见于正之，合莫被于破害，则天下和平，而国家奠安矣。 | 冲不要出现在正卦、之卦里，合不要被破、被害，这样天下就和平，国家就安定了。 | if clash does not appear in the original or the changed hexagram, and a combination is not broken or harmed, then the world is at peace and the state is secure. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 816 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 816*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 次观旺衰动静合冲。 | 接着再看旺衰、动静、合冲。 | Next look at strength and weakness, movement and stillness, combination and clash. |

> **按**:「邦畿」是国都周围的王畿之地。「国祚」是国运，「宗社」是宗庙社稷。「正之」是正卦（本卦）和之卦（变卦）；「冲莫见于正之」是本卦、变卦里都不要出现冲（见术语表）。这一条与易林补遗朝廷国事「亲宫为邦国大忌空亡、本卦作朝堂最宜旺相」（EL.02）取同一个象：卦的整体代表国。
>
> **Note:** 'Bangji' is the royal domain around the capital. 'Guozuo' is the fortune of the dynasty and 'zongshe' is the ancestral temple and altars. 'Zheng zhi' is the original hexagram and the changed hexagram; 'clash must not appear in the original or the changed hexagram' means that neither is to show a clash (see the glossary). This entry takes the same image as 'the home palace is taken as the state, Void most to be avoided; the original hexagram is taken as the court hall, thriving best' in the Yilin Buyi chapter on court and state affairs (EL.02): the hexagram as a whole stands for the state.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.01、LY.CAREER.YM.03、LY.CAREER.EL.02、LY.CAREER.HJC.64、LY.CAREER.YY.32

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.03 国事：皇帝亲占以世爻为主象宜临日月生扶、臣下筮国以九五当尊位忌随墓空破；亲占应合世者后德著于樛木，臣占二持福者母仪冠于掖庭

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:皇帝亲自占卜，以世爻为主象，适宜得到日月的生扶；臣下为国占卜，以九五爻当作尊位，忌讳随墓、空、破。皇帝亲占而应爻合世的，皇后的德行像樛木那样昭著；臣下占卜而二爻持福神的，皇后的母仪在后宫居首。臣下占卜，以五爻为君、二爻为后，所以这样说。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the emperor divines in person, the Self line is the chief image and it is fitting for the Day and Month to generate and support it; when a minister divines for the state, the ninth-fifth line is the honoured position and following into the tomb, Void and break are to be avoided. If the emperor divines in person and the Other combines with the Self, the empress's virtue shines like that of the drooping tree; if a minister divines and the second line holds the fortune spirit, the empress's model of motherhood heads the harem. A minister divining takes the fifth line as the sovereign and the second as the empress, hence what is said.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 817 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 817*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 皇陛亲占，以世爻为主象，宜临日月生扶； | 皇帝亲自占卜，以世爻为主象，适宜得到日、月的生扶； | When the emperor divines in person, the Self line is the chief image, and it is fitting for it to meet generation and support from the Day and Month; |
| 臣下筮国，以九五当尊位，忌随墓空破。 | 臣下为国占卜，以九五爻当作尊位，忌讳随墓、空、破。 | when a minister divines for the state, the ninth-fifth line is the honoured position, and following into the tomb, Void and break are to be avoided. |
| 亲占而应合世者，后德著于樛木； | 皇帝亲占而应爻合世的，皇后的德行像樛木那样昭著； | If the emperor divines in person and the Other combines with the Self, the empress's virtue shines like that of the drooping tree; |
| 臣占而二持福者，母仪冠于掖庭。 | 臣下占卜而二爻持福神的，皇后的母仪在后宫居首。 | if a minister divines and the second line holds the fortune spirit, the empress's model of motherhood heads the harem. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 818 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 818*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 臣子占五为君，二为后，故云。 | 臣下占卜，以五爻为君、二爻为后，所以这样说。 | A minister divining takes the fifth line as the sovereign and the second as the empress, hence what is said. |

> **按**:「皇陛」疑是「皇帝」。「九五」是第五爻，代表君位。「樛木」是《诗经·周南》里称颂后妃德行（能惠及下人）的篇名；「掖庭」是后宫。「世爻为主象」是皇帝自己占，世就是皇帝本人。这一条与易林补遗「五爻为天子、太岁为朝廷」（EL.80）和黄金策国朝「九五逢阳，当遇仁明之主」（HJC.65）同取五爻为君。
>
> **Note:** 'Huangbi' is presumably 'huangdi', emperor. 'Jiuwu', the ninth-fifth, is the fifth line, which stands for the sovereign's position. 'Jiumu', the drooping tree, is the title of an ode in the Book of Odes that praises the virtue of a queen who is gracious to those beneath her; 'yeting' is the inner palace. 'The Self line is the chief image' means that when the emperor divines for himself the Self is the emperor in person. This entry takes the fifth line as the sovereign, as 'the fifth line is the Son of Heaven, Tai Sui is the court' in the Yilin Buyi (EL.80) and 'the ninth-fifth meeting yang, one meets a benevolent and enlightened sovereign' in the Huangjin Ce on the state (HJC.65) do.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.02、LY.CAREER.YM.04、LY.CAREER.EL.80、LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.YY.35、LY.CAREER.YY.37

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.04 国事：初为国本、二为士人、三乃万方之守牧、四为九州之屏藩、上为宗庙之爻，俱恶鬼而喜福、喜安旺而恶动摇；鬼为国之患又为巨寇，福为国之储又为良臣，财为国之赋又为阍寺，兄弟为国之聚敛又为宗支，父母为山川城郭又为诏令

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:推爻位：初爻是国本，二爻是士人，三爻是各地的守牧（地方长官），四爻是九州的屏藩，上爻是宗庙之爻；都厌恶官鬼、喜欢福神，喜欢安稳旺相、厌恶动摇。推六亲：官鬼是国家的祸患，又是大盗；福神是国家的储君，又是良臣；妻财是国家的赋税，又是宦官；兄弟是国家的聚敛，又是宗室；父母是山川城郭，又是诏令。看它们临在什么位置、属于哪一宫，比如官鬼发动在乾宫，西北有事；福神临在三爻，地方守牧得人，等等。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Reckoning by the line positions: the first line is the root of the state, the second the scholars, the third the governors of all the regions, the fourth the screens and bulwarks of the nine provinces, and the top line the line of the ancestral temple; all dislike Pressure and like the fortune spirit, like quiet and thriving and dislike moving and shaking. Reckoning by the six kin: Pressure is the plague of the state and also a great bandit; Output, the fortune spirit, is the heir of the state and also a good minister; Wealth is the revenue of the state and also the palace gatekeepers; Peer is the state's levying and hoarding and also the imperial clan; Resource is the mountains, rivers and city walls and also the edicts and orders. Look at where they sit and what palace they belong to: for example, if Pressure moves in the Qian palace there is trouble in the north-west, and if the fortune spirit sits on the third line the governors are well chosen, and the like.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 819 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 819*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 推之爻位，初为国本， | 推爻位：初爻是国本， | Reckoning by the line positions: the first line is the root of the state, |
| 二为士人， | 二爻是士人， | the second the scholars, |
| 三乃万方之守牧， | 三爻是各地的守牧（地方长官）， | the third the governors of all the regions, |
| 四为九州之屏藩， | 四爻是九州的屏藩， | the fourth the screens and bulwarks of the nine provinces, |
| 上为宗庙之爻， | 上爻是宗庙之爻， | and the top line the line of the ancestral temple; |
| 俱恶鬼而喜福，喜安旺而恶动摇。 | 都厌恶官鬼、喜欢福神，喜欢安稳旺相、厌恶动摇。 | all dislike Pressure and like the fortune spirit, like quiet and thriving and dislike moving and shaking. |
| 推之六亲， | 推究六亲： | Reckoning by the six kin: |
| 鬼者，国之患也，又为巨寇； | 官鬼是国家的祸患，又是大盗； | Pressure is the plague of the state and also a great bandit; |
| 福者，国之储也，又为良臣； | 福神是国家的储君，又是良臣； | Output, the fortune spirit, is the heir of the state and also a good minister; |
| 财者，国之赋也，又为阍寺； | 妻财是国家的赋税，又是宦官； | Wealth is the revenue of the state and also the palace gatekeepers; |
| 兄弟者，国之聚敛也，又为宗支； | 兄弟是国家的聚敛，又是宗室； | Peer is the state's levying and hoarding and also the imperial clan; |
| 父母者，山川城郭也，又为诏令。 | 父母是山川城郭，又是诏令。 | Resource is the mountains, rivers and city walls and also the edicts and orders. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 820 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 820*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 观其所临所属，如鬼动乾宫，西北有事，福临三位，守牧得人之类。 | 看它们所临的位置、所属的宫：比如官鬼发动在乾宫，西北有事；福神临在三爻，地方守牧得人，等等。 | Look at where they sit and what palace they belong to: for example, if Pressure moves in the Qian palace there is trouble in the north-west, and if the fortune spirit sits on the third line the governors are well chosen, and the like. |

> **按**:「守牧」是地方长官，「屏藩」是拱卫朝廷的藩镇，「阍寺」是守宫门的宦官，「宗支」是皇室宗亲的各支，「聚敛」是征收搜刮，「诏令」是皇帝的诏书命令。这一条的取象与易隐朝廷占的「分官」（YY.43、YY.44）、黄金策国朝的爻位（HJC.65）同一套：初民、二士、三守牧、四公卿、五君、上宗庙。
>
> **Note:** 'Shoumu' are the local governors, 'pingfan' are the border governors who guard the court, 'hunshi' are the eunuchs who keep the palace gate, 'zongzhi' are the branches of the imperial clan, 'juilian' is levying and extorting, and 'zhaoling' are the emperor's edicts and orders. The images here are the same set as the 'division of officials' in the Yi Yin divination on the court (YY.43, YY.44) and the line positions in the Huangjin Ce on the state (HJC.65): the first the common people, the second scholars, the third governors, the fourth ministers, the fifth the sovereign, the top the ancestral temple.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.03、LY.CAREER.YM.05、LY.CAREER.YY.43、LY.CAREER.YY.44、LY.CAREER.HJC.65

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.05 国事：鬼空内而无内衅、鬼发外而有外攘，近五防佞臣、附三戒酷吏、患在二而士横、患在初而民困；虎官不发于金乡则无干戈、武鬼不扬于水地则无泛潦，火鬼螣蛇岁忧火患、土官朱雀年虑蝗灾；凡鬼发内外何宫何位何属皆宜慎之

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼在内卦落空，就没有内乱；官鬼在外卦发动，就有外患要抵御；官鬼靠近五爻，要防佞臣；依附三爻，要戒酷吏；祸患在二爻，是士人横行；祸患在初爻，是百姓困苦。白虎官如果不在金乡发动，就没有战事；玄武官如果不在水地张扬，就没有水涝；火属官鬼带螣蛇，当年忧火灾；土官带朱雀，当年虑蝗灾。凡是官鬼发动，在内还是在外、哪一宫、哪一位、属于哪一类，都应当谨慎；整饬弊端要防奸邪，朝廷的谋划应当预先。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure is Void in the inner trigram there is no disorder within; if Pressure moves in the outer trigram there are outside enemies to repel; if Pressure is near the fifth line guard against treacherous ministers; if it attaches to the third line beware of cruel officials; if the trouble is on the second line the scholars run riot, and if on the first line the people are in distress. If a White Tiger Pressure does not move in a Metal palace there is no war; if a Dark Tortoise Pressure does not show itself on watery ground there is no flooding; a Fire Pressure with the Coiling Snake means anxiety about fire in the year, and an Earth star of office with the Vermilion Bird means worry about a plague of locusts. Wherever Pressure moves, inside or outside, in which palace, in which position and of which element, one should be careful; in setting right abuses guard against treachery, and the court's planning should be done in advance.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 821 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 821*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 是以鬼空内而无内衅， | 官鬼在内卦落空，就没有内乱； | If Pressure is Void in the inner trigram there is no disorder within; |
| 鬼发外而有外攘， | 官鬼在外卦发动，就有外患要抵御； | if Pressure moves in the outer trigram there are outside enemies to repel; |
| 近五而防佞（？）臣， | 官鬼靠近五爻，要防佞臣； | if Pressure is near the fifth line guard against treacherous ministers; |
| 附三而戒酷吏， | 依附三爻，要戒酷吏； | if it attaches to the third line beware of cruel officials; |
| 患在二而士横， | 祸患在二爻，是士人横行； | if the trouble is on the second line the scholars run riot; |
| 患在初而民困。 | 祸患在初爻，是百姓困苦。 | if on the first line the people are in distress. |
| 虎官不发于金乡，则无干戈， | 白虎官如果不在金乡发动，就没有战事； | If a White Tiger Pressure does not move in a Metal palace there is no war; |
| 武鬼不扬于水地，则无泛潦； | 玄武官如果不在水地张扬，就没有水涝； | if a Dark Tortoise Pressure does not show itself on watery ground there is no flooding; |
| 如火鬼螣蛇，岁忧火患， | 火属官鬼带螣蛇，当年忧火灾； | a Fire Pressure with the Coiling Snake means anxiety about fire in the year, |
| 土官朱雀，年虑蝗灾。 | 土官带朱雀，当年虑蝗灾。 | and an Earth star of office with the Vermilion Bird means worry about a plague of locusts. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 822 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 822*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡鬼发内外、何宫、何位、何属，皆宜慎之，干蛊防奸，庙算当豫也。 | 凡是官鬼发动，在内还是在外、哪一宫、哪一位、属于哪一类，都应当谨慎；整饬弊端要防奸邪，朝廷的谋划应当预先。 | Wherever Pressure moves, inside or outside, in which palace, in which position and of which element, one should be careful; in setting right abuses guard against treachery, and the court's planning should be done in advance. |

> **按**:「近五而防佞（？）臣」的「佞」字书里自己标了问号，是传抄者对这个字没有把握；文意是官鬼靠近君位（五爻）要防奸臣，译作「佞臣」。「内衅」是内部的变乱，「外攘」是对外抵御，「干戈」是战事，「泛潦」是水涝，「干蛊」是整饬前人留下的弊端（《易》蛊卦「干父之蛊」）。「金乡」「水地」是金、水所属的宫位。「虎官」「武鬼」是白虎、玄武上的官鬼。「庙算」是朝廷的谋划。
>
> **Note:** The book itself puts a question mark after the character 'ning' in 'jin wu er fang ning(?) chen', showing that the copyist was not sure of it; by the sense, Pressure near the sovereign's position (the fifth line) means guarding against treacherous ministers, and it is translated so. 'Neixin' is disorder within, 'waixiang' is repelling enemies from outside, 'gange' is war, 'fanlao' is flooding, and 'gan gu' is setting right the abuses left by one's predecessors (from the Gu hexagram of the Book of Changes, 'setting right the father's decay'). 'Jinxiang' and 'shuidi' are the palaces that Metal and Water belong to. 'Huguan' and 'wugui' are Pressure on the White Tiger and the Dark Tortoise. 'Miaosuan' is the court's planning.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.04、LY.CAREER.YM.06、LY.CAREER.EL.03、LY.CAREER.YY.33、LY.CAREER.YY.42、LY.CAREER.HJC.64

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.06 国事：子孙遇木而旺相岁稔桑麻、妻财遇土而兴隆年丰田野、父母值巳午而见当启文明；子在青龙文士彬彬、子临白虎武功烈烈，动朱雀善弹劾之章、摇元武懿宫闱之德，勾陈得田畯之喜、螣蛇籍使臣之力

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙遇木而旺相，当年桑麻丰收；妻财遇土而兴隆，当年田野丰收；父母爻值巳、午而出现，当开启文明。子孙在青龙，文士彬彬有礼；子孙临白虎，武功烈烈；动在朱雀，善于弹劾的奏章；摇在玄武，美在宫闱的德行；勾陈得田官的喜庆，螣蛇靠使臣的力量。凡是福神临在五行、六亲、六神上，都是吉祥的应验，所以福神摇在玄武，也是后宫的美事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output meets Wood and thrives, the year's mulberry and hemp are plentiful; if Wealth meets Earth and flourishes, the fields yield well in the year; if Resource falls on Si or Wu and appears, civilisation is opened up. If Output is on the Azure Dragon, the scholars are refined; if Output is on the White Tiger, military exploits blaze; moving on the Vermilion Bird, good memorials of impeachment; swaying on the Dark Tortoise, fine virtue in the inner palace; on Hook Earth it enjoys the delight of the field officers, and on the Coiling Snake it relies on the strength of envoys. Wherever the fortune spirit rests, on the five elements, the six kin or the six spirits, it is an auspicious response, so the fortune spirit swaying on the Dark Tortoise is also a fine thing for the inner palace.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 823 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 823*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙遇木而旺相，岁稔桑麻， | 子孙遇到木而旺相，当年桑麻丰收； | If Output meets Wood and thrives, the year's mulberry and hemp are plentiful; |
| 妻财遇土而兴隆，年丰田野， | 妻财遇到土而兴隆，当年田野丰收； | if Wealth meets Earth and flourishes, the fields yield well in the year; |
| 父母值巳午而见，当启文明； | 父母爻值巳、午而出现，当开启文明； | if Resource falls on Si or Wu and appears, civilisation is opened up; |
| 子在青龙，则文士彬彬， | 子孙在青龙，就文士彬彬有礼； | if Output is on the Azure Dragon, the scholars are refined; |
| 子临白虎，则武功烈烈； | 子孙临白虎，就武功烈烈； | if Output is on the White Tiger, military exploits blaze; |
| 动朱雀，善弹劾之章， | （子孙）动在朱雀，善于弹劾的奏章； | moving on the Vermilion Bird, good memorials of impeachment; |
| 摇元武，懿宫闱之德； | 摇在玄武，美在宫闱的德行； | swaying on the Dark Tortoise, fine virtue in the inner palace; |
| 勾陈得田畯之喜， | （福神）在勾陈，得田官的喜庆， | on Hook Earth it enjoys the delight of the field officers, |
| 螣蛇籍使臣之力。 | 在螣蛇，靠使臣的力量。 | and on the Coiling Snake it relies on the strength of envoys. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 824 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 824*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡福临五行六亲六神，俱是祯应，故福摇元武，亦为后宫之美。 | 凡是福神临在五行、六亲、六神上，都是吉祥的应验，所以福神摇在玄武，也是后宫的美事。 | Wherever the fortune spirit rests, on the five elements, the six kin or the six spirits, it is an auspicious response, so the fortune spirit swaying on the Dark Tortoise is also a fine thing for the inner palace. |

> **按**:「稔」是丰熟。「田畯」是管田事的官（《诗经》里的田官）。「弹劾」是监察官的检举。「宫闱」是后宫。「元武」就是玄武。这一条是福神（子孙）落在五行、六神上的象：木主桑麻，土主田野，青龙文、白虎武、朱雀言官、玄武后宫、勾陈农事、螣蛇使臣。「巳午」是火，「父母值巳午」取的是火为文明。
>
> **Note:** 'Ren' is ripening. 'Tianjun' is the official in charge of the fields (the field officer of the Book of Odes). 'Tanhe' is impeachment by the censors. 'Gongwei' is the inner palace. 'Yuanwu' is the Dark Tortoise. This entry gives the images of the fortune spirit (Output) falling on the five elements and the six spirits: Wood governs mulberry and hemp, Earth the fields, the Azure Dragon the civil, the White Tiger the military, the Vermilion Bird the censors, the Dark Tortoise the inner palace, Hook Earth farming, the Coiling Snake envoys. 'Si and Wu' are Fire, and Resource on Si or Wu takes Fire as civilisation.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.05、LY.CAREER.YM.07、LY.CAREER.YY.35、LY.CAREER.YY.38

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.07 国事：问迁都犹迁居、当以外旺为凭、鬼兴为忌；问建庙犹奉灵、当以三冲六坏鬼动鬼疾鬼克为忌；迁都建庙皆忌贞冲悔冲合处逢冲，太庙不可破散、太灵不可鬼动鬼空鬼散及鬼克世

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:问迁都，如同迁居：以外卦旺相为凭据，官鬼兴旺是忌。问建庙，如同供奉神灵：以三冲、六坏、鬼动、鬼疾、鬼克为忌。迁都、建庙，都忌贞冲、悔冲、合处逢冲这几种情形。六爻是太庙所属，所以不可破散；官鬼是太灵（祖先神灵），所以不可鬼动、鬼空、鬼散，以及鬼克世。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For moving the capital, which is like moving house, the outer being thriving is the ground and Pressure flourishing is the taboo; for building a temple, which is like enshrining the spirits, the taboos are the three clashes, the six breakages, and Pressure moving, being sick or controlling. In moving the capital and building a temple, the taboos are the clash of the original hexagram, the clash of the changed hexagram, and a combination meeting a clash. The six lines belong to the great temple, so it must not be broken or scattered; Pressure is the great spirit, so Pressure must not move, be Void or scatter, nor control the Self.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 825 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 825*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如问迁都，犹迁居也，当以外旺为凭，鬼兴为忌。 | 如果问迁都，如同迁居，应当以外卦旺相为凭据，官鬼兴旺是忌； | If one asks about moving the capital, it is like moving house, and the outer being thriving is the ground, with Pressure flourishing a taboo; |
| 如问建庙，犹奉灵也，当以三冲六坏鬼动鬼疾鬼克为忌。 | 如果问建庙，如同供奉神灵，应当以三冲、六坏、鬼动、鬼疾、鬼克为忌。 | if one asks about building a temple, it is like enshrining the spirits, and the taboos are the three clashes, the six breakages, and Pressure moving, being sick or controlling. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 826 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 826*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 迁都建庙，皆忌贞冲、悔冲、合处逢冲之例。 | 迁都、建庙，都忌贞冲、悔冲、合处逢冲这几种情形。 | In moving the capital and building a temple, the taboos are the cases of the clash of the original hexagram, the clash of the changed hexagram, and a combination meeting a clash. |
| 六爻太庙所属，故不可破散， | 六爻是太庙所属，所以不可破散； | The six lines belong to the great temple, and so it must not be broken or scattered; |
| 鬼为太灵，故不可鬼动、鬼空、鬼散，及鬼克世也。 | 官鬼是太灵（祖先神灵），所以不可鬼动、鬼空、鬼散，以及鬼克世。 | Pressure is the great spirit, and so Pressure must not move, be Void or scatter, nor control the Self. |

> **按**:「贞冲」「悔冲」是本卦（贞）、变卦（悔）是六冲卦；「合处逢冲」是本来相合的地方遇到冲；注里列的这三种就是正文的「三冲」（见术语表）。「六坏」书里这一处没有列出。「奉灵」是供奉神灵，「太庙」是帝王的宗庙，「太灵」是祖先的神灵。这一条与易林补遗朝廷国事里的忌六冲（EL.01）取同一类忌：国是一个整体，冲散就是国破。
>
> **Note:** 'Zhenchong' and 'huichong' are the original hexagram (zhen) and the changed hexagram (hui) being a six-clash hexagram; 'hechu fengchong' is a place that was a combination meeting a clash; these three listed in the note are the 'three clashes' of the main text (see the glossary). The 'six breakages' are not listed here in the book. 'Fengling' is enshrining the spirits, 'taimiao' is the emperor's ancestral temple, and 'tailing' is the spirits of the ancestors. This entry takes the same kind of taboo as the six-clash in the Yilin Buyi chapter on court and state affairs (EL.01): the state is a whole, and a clash that scatters it is the state broken.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.06、LY.CAREER.YM.08、LY.CAREER.EL.01

## LY.CAREER.YM.08 国事：择后犹婚姻、财在何宫当应何所；立储喜子孙跨于日月、改制忧官鬼发于强梁

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:选皇后如同婚姻：财爻在哪一宫，就应在哪个地方的女子。如果财在中间的爻，应得晋地的女子；财在内卦，应在本乡。立储君，喜欢子孙跨在日、月上；改制，忧虑官鬼在强横的位置上发动。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Choosing an empress is like a marriage: in whichever palace Wealth lies, the match corresponds to that place. If Wealth is in a middle line, the match should be a woman of Jin; if Wealth is in the inner trigram, it should correspond to the home district. In naming an heir one is glad for Output to straddle the Day and Month; in changing the institutions one worries about Pressure moving in the place of the violent and overbearing.

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 827 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 827*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 择后犹婚姻也，财在何宫，当应何所。 | 选皇后如同婚姻，财爻在哪一宫，就应在哪个地方的女子。 | Choosing an empress is like a marriage: in whichever palace Wealth lies, the match corresponds to that place. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 828 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 828*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如财在中爻，当得晋女，财在内卦，当应本乡。 | 如果财在中间的爻，应得晋地的女子；财在内卦，应在本乡。 | If Wealth is in a middle line, the match should be a woman of Jin; if Wealth is in the inner trigram, it should correspond to the home district. |

**《易冒·国事章第四十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 829 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 42: State affairs", base text; Yi Mao, line 829*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 立储喜子孙跨于日月，改制忧官鬼发于强梁。 | 立储君，喜欢子孙跨在日、月上；改制，忧虑官鬼在强横的位置上发动。 | In naming an heir one is glad for Output to straddle the Day and Month; in changing the institutions one worries about Pressure moving in the place of the violent and overbearing. |

> **按**:「择后」是选立皇后，「立储」是确立太子，「改制」是改革制度。「中爻」「晋女」书里没有说明，这里照字面译，「晋」疑指晋地。「跨于日月」「发于强梁」书里没有解释，按字面是子孙同时受日、月扶助、官鬼在强横的位置发动。择后看财与婚姻章同（见本书婚姻部分），立储看子孙与易隐朝廷占「青宫：以子孙为用」（YY.38）、黄金策国朝「日辰拱扶子位」（HJC.75）同。
>
> **Note:** 'Zehou' is choosing and installing an empress, 'lichu' is establishing the crown prince, and 'gaizhi' is reforming the institutions. The book does not explain 'zhong yao' and 'Jin nü', which are translated literally, and 'Jin' is presumably the land of Jin. 'Kua yu riyue' and 'fa yu qiangliang' are not explained in the book and by the letter are Output being supported by both the Day and Month and Pressure moving in a violent and overbearing place. Choosing an empress looks at Wealth as in the chapter on marriage (see the marriage part of this library), and naming an heir looks at Output as in 'the Crown Prince: take Output as the Subject' in the Yi Yin divination on the court (YY.38) and 'the Day generating and supporting Output's place' in the Huangjin Ce on the state (HJC.75).

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.07、LY.CAREER.YM.09、LY.CAREER.YY.37、LY.CAREER.YY.38、LY.CAREER.HJC.75
