## LY.CAREER.YM.57 面圣上书、叩阍献策与条陈于将相：不可遇遁；见六冲则上下睽隔、受五克（五爻克世）则披君逆鳞、五位伤（五爻空破绝散）则忠言逆耳；条陈于将相喜官兴而忌克世

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:面见皇上上书、叩阍陈情献策，不可遇到遁卦。见六冲，就上下隔阂；受五爻的克，就触犯君主的逆鳞；五位受伤，就忠言逆耳。如果是向将相逐条陈述意见，喜官旺而忌克世。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In presenting a memorial to the sovereign in person, or knocking at the palace gate to offer a plan, the hexagram Dun must not be met. Meeting a six-clash, high and low are estranged; suffering the control of the fifth line, one touches the reversed scale on the sovereign's throat; the fifth place being injured, loyal words grate on the ear. If one is presenting views item by item to a general or chief minister, thriving Pressure is welcome and controlling the Self is feared.

**《易冒·功名章第六十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 1433 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 62: Fame and office", base text; Yi Mao, line 1433*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 面圣上书，叩阍献策，遁不可遇也。 | 面见皇上上书、叩阍陈情献策，不可遇到遁卦。 | In presenting a memorial to the sovereign in person, or knocking at the palace gate to offer a plan, the hexagram Dun must not be met. |
| 见六冲而上下睽隔，受五克而披君逆鳞，五位伤而忠言逆耳。 | 见六冲，就上下隔阂；受五爻的克，就触犯君主的逆鳞；五位受伤，就忠言逆耳。 | Meeting a six-clash, high and low are estranged; suffering the control of the fifth line, one touches the reversed scale on the sovereign's throat; the fifth place being injured, loyal words grate on the ear. |
| 若条陈于将相，喜官兴而忌克世也。 | 如果是向将相逐条陈述意见，喜官旺而忌克世。 | If one is presenting views item by item to a general or chief minister, thriving Pressure is welcome and controlling the Self is feared. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1434 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1434*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 五克谓五爻克世， | 五克就是五爻克世， | 'Five controls' means the fifth line controlling the Self, |
| 五位，谓五爻空破绝散也。 | 五位受伤，就是五爻空、破、绝、散。 | and 'the fifth place injured' means the fifth line Void, broken, extinct or scattered. |

> **按**:「面圣上书」是当面向皇上呈递奏章，「叩阍」是到宫门叩击喊冤、陈情，「献策」是进献谋略，「条陈」是向将相逐条陈述意见。「遁不可遇」按文意是遁卦（天山遁，是退避的象）不可遇，与书里别处「得遁而不进」同类，是按卦名取义的忌。「睽隔」是隔阂。「逆鳞」是传说龙喉下倒生的鳞，触到它龙必怒，比喻触犯君主的忌讳。「忠言逆耳」是忠言不被听从。书里自注：五克是五爻克世，五位伤是五爻空、破、绝、散。和易林补遗朝天面旨（EL.91–93）、黄金策仕宦的奏陈谏诤（HJC.26）对着看。
>
> **Note:** 'Mian sheng shang shu' is presenting a memorial to the sovereign face to face, 'kouhun' is knocking at the palace gate to complain of a wrong or plead one's case, 'xiance' is offering a plan, and 'tiaochen' is presenting views item by item to a general or minister. 'Dun bu ke yu', by the sense, is that the hexagram Dun (Retreat, the image of withdrawing) must not be met, of a kind with 'getting Dun, no advance' elsewhere in the book, a taboo taken from the name of the hexagram. 'Kuige' is estrangement. 'Nilin' is the scale growing the wrong way beneath a dragon's throat, which makes the dragon furious when touched, a figure for offending the sovereign's taboos. 'Loyal words grate on the ear' is loyal words not being heeded. The book's own gloss: 'five controls' is the fifth line controlling the Self, and 'fifth place injured' is the fifth line Void, broken, extinct or scattered. Read against the Yilin Buyi on the audience at court (EL.91-93) and the Huangjin Ce on memorials and remonstrances (HJC.26).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.89、LY.CAREER.EL.90、LY.CAREER.EL.91、LY.CAREER.EL.93、LY.CAREER.HJC.26、LY.CAREER.YM.56、LY.CAREER.YM.58

## LY.CAREER.YM.63 衙门裁革、公议短长与国务行止：裁革止察官爻（官旺则不裁）、公议短长惟求鬼象（鬼破则中止）、国务行止专从五位（兴革损益得君命为行止、五爻克世则不行）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:衙门裁撤革除，只察看官爻；公议的短长，只求鬼象；国家政务行止，专从五位。官旺就不裁；公议将要实行的事，鬼破就中止；兴办、革除、减少、增加，以得到君命才行，五爻克世，就不能行。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whether a government office is abolished, one looks only at the Pressure line; whether public opinion carries or fails, one seeks only the Pressure image; whether a state affair goes ahead or stops, one follows only the fifth place. Thriving Pressure means it is not abolished; of a matter public opinion is about to carry out, broken Pressure stops it; to set up, abolish, reduce or increase anything, it goes ahead only if the sovereign's order is obtained, and if the fifth line controls the Self, it does not go.

**《易冒·功名章第六十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 1443 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 62: Fame and office", base text; Yi Mao, line 1443*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 衙门裁革，止察官爻， | 衙门裁撤革除，只察看官爻； | Whether a government office is abolished, one looks only at the Pressure line; |
| 公议短长，惟求鬼象， | 公议的短长，只求鬼象； | whether public opinion carries or fails, one seeks only the Pressure image; |
| 国务行止，专从五位也。 | 国家政务的行止，专从五位。 | whether a state affair goes ahead or stops, one follows only the fifth place. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1444 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1444*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官旺则不裁。 | 官旺就不裁。 | Thriving Pressure means it is not abolished. |
| 公议将行之事，鬼破则中止。 | 公议将要实行的事，鬼破就中止。 | Of a matter public opinion is about to carry out, broken Pressure stops it. |
| 兴革损益，得君命为行止，五爻克世，则不行也。 | 兴办、革除、减少、增加，以得到君命为行止，五爻克世，就不能行。 | To set up, abolish, reduce or increase anything, it goes ahead only if the sovereign's order is obtained, and if the fifth line controls the Self, it does not go. |

> **按**:「裁革」是衙门被裁撤革除，「公议」是公众（朝野）的议论，「国务」是国家政务。「五位」是五爻（君位）。书里自注：官旺则不裁；公议要实行的事，鬼破则中止；兴、革、损、益（兴办、革除、减少、增加）要得到君命才行得通，五爻克世就行不通。这一条是功名章的结尾，从个人的官职转到了衙门和国家的事；和本书国事章（YM.01 起）、易林补遗朝廷国事（EL.01 起）对着看。
>
> **Note:** 'Caige' is a government office being cut and abolished, 'gongyi' is the discussion of the public (court and country), and 'guowu' is the business of the state. 'Wuwei' is the fifth line (the sovereign's place). The book's own gloss: thriving Pressure means it is not cut; of a matter public opinion is about to carry out, broken Pressure stops it; to set up, abolish, reduce or increase anything one must obtain the sovereign's order for it to go through, and the fifth line controlling the Self means it does not. This entry is the end of the chapter on fame and office, turning from a person's own post to the affairs of the offices and the state; read against the chapter on affairs of state in this book (from YM.01) and the Yilin Buyi on the affairs of the court (from EL.01).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.01、LY.CAREER.YM.01、LY.CAREER.YM.62

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.31 朝廷占总述（游南子）：占朝廷与臣庶异，以世爻所居之卦为国；世爻五爻为君，应爻二爻为后；子孙国储，妻财妃嫔帑藏，父母城池，兄弟劫神，官鬼奸贼；初民二士三大夫四公卿五天子六宗庙；卜世卜年另有一法

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占朝廷的事和占臣子百姓自己的事不同：以世爻所在的那个单卦当作国家；世爻和五爻都是君主，君主自己占取世爻为用神，臣子百姓代他占取五爻；应爻和二爻是皇后；子孙是储君，妻财是妃嫔也是国库，父母是城池，兄弟是劫神，官鬼是奸贼；初爻是百姓，二爻是士人，三爻是大夫，四爻是公卿，五爻是天子，六爻是宗庙朝廷。察看各爻的生克旺衰，国家的利弊兴衰就看得清清楚楚；至于占国家传几世、延续几年，另有一种方法。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Divining for the court differs from divining for ministers and commoners: the trigram in which the Self line sits stands for the state; the Self and the fifth line are both the ruler, and when the ruler divines for himself the Self is taken as the Subject, while ministers and commoners divining on his behalf take the fifth line; the Other line and the second line are the empress; Output is the heir apparent, Wealth is the consorts and also the treasury, Resource is the city walls and moats, Peer is the robber spirit, and Pressure is the traitors and bandits; the first line is the common people, the second the scholars, the third the great officers, the fourth the ministers of state, the fifth the Son of Heaven, and the sixth the ancestral temple and court. Look at the generation, control, strength and weakness of each line and the gains and losses and rise and fall of the state are as clear as the palm of one's hand; as for divining how many reigns or years the dynasty will last, there is another method.

**游南子(《易隐》引)** — 《易隐》第 2337 行 · *Master Younan (quoted in the Yi Yin); Yi Yin, line 2337*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 游南子曰。 | 游南子说。 | Master Younan says. |
| 朝廷之占，与臣庶异。 | 占朝廷的事，和占臣子百姓自己的事不同。 | Divining for the court is different from divining for ministers and commoners. |
| 以世爻所居之卦为国也。 | 以世爻所在的那个单卦当作国家。 | The trigram in which the Self line sits is taken as the state. |
| 世爻为君，五爻亦为君也。 | 世爻是君主，五爻也是君主。 | The Self line is the ruler, and the fifth line is also the ruler. |
| 主上自占，以世爻为用，臣庶代卜，以五爻为用也。 | 君主自己占，以世爻为用神；臣子百姓代他占，以五爻为用神。 | When the sovereign divines for himself he takes the Self line as the Subject; when ministers and commoners divine on his behalf, they take the fifth line as the Subject. |
| 应为后，二爻亦为后也。 | 应爻是皇后，二爻也是皇后。 | The Other line is the empress, and the second line is also the empress. |
| 子孙为国储，妻财为妃嫔，亦为帑藏。 | 子孙是储君，妻财是妃嫔，也是国库。 | Output is the heir apparent; Wealth is the consorts, and also the treasury. |
| 父母为城池，兄弟为劫神，官鬼为奸贼也。 | 父母是城池，兄弟是劫神，官鬼是奸贼。 | Resource is the city walls and moats, Peer is the robber spirit, and Pressure is the traitors and bandits. |
| 又初爻民，二爻士，三爻大夫，四爻公卿，五爻天子，六爻宗庙朝廷也。 | 又，初爻是百姓，二爻是士人，三爻是大夫，四爻是公卿，五爻是天子，六爻是宗庙朝廷。 | Again, the first line is the common people, the second the scholars, the third the great officers, the fourth the ministers of state, the fifth the Son of Heaven, and the sixth the ancestral temple and court. |
| 察各爻之生克旺衰，而国家之利弊兴替已了若指掌也。 | 察看各爻的生克旺衰，国家的利弊兴衰就清楚得像看自己的手掌。 | Look at the generation, control, strength and weakness of each line, and the gains and losses and rise and fall of the state are as clear as the palm of one's hand. |
| 若乃卜世卜年，又自有一法焉。 | 至于占国家传几世、延续几年，又另有一种方法。 | As for divining how many reigns or years the dynasty will last, there is yet another method. |

> **按**:「单卦」是三爻卦，指内卦或外卦，世爻在哪个三爻里，哪个就是国（见 YY.32）。「劫神」是主夺取、劫掠的凶神。「用神」是占问时被拿来看这件事成败的那个爻。这一节是整个朝廷占的纲，后面各小节都是按这里的分配来断。
>
> **Note:** A 'single trigram' is a trigram of three lines, the inner or the outer trigram: whichever trigram holds the Self line is the state (see YY.32). The 'robber spirit' is the baleful spirit of seizing and plundering. The 'Subject' is the line taken as the matter asked about. This section is the outline of the whole divination for the court, and the sections after it judge by the assignments made here.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.32、LY.CAREER.YY.35、LY.CAREER.HJC.63、LY.CAREER.HJC.64

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.32 朝廷占·大象（一）：世爻所居之卦为国——旺相国治天下平，胎没次之，死囚休废国祚陵夷；空亡巡游无度；冲破疆围不固；刑克奸宄窃发；受害臣庶离心；岁生旺宫国运炎隆，岁克衰卦宗社将倾

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:大象，国运的总势：世爻所在的那个单卦就是国家。它旺相，国家治理得好，天下太平；胎、没次一等；死、囚、休、废，国运就衰落下去。它落空亡，君主巡游没有节制；被冲破，疆界不稳固；被刑克，奸邪作乱的人暗中起事；受害，臣子百姓离心。太岁生旺这一宫，国运炽盛；太岁克制衰弱的卦，国家的宗庙社稷将要倾覆。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The great image, the general trend of the state's fortune. The trigram in which the Self line sits is the state. If it is thriving, the state is well governed and the world at peace; the stages Tai and Mo come next; if it is dead, imprisoned, resting or abandoned, the dynasty declines. If it falls into Void, the sovereign tours without restraint; if it is clashed or broken, the borders are not secure; if it is punished or controlled, treacherous rebels rise in secret; if it is harmed, ministers and commoners lose heart. If Tai Sui generates a thriving palace, the state's fortune is blazing; if Tai Sui controls a weak trigram, the ancestral temple and altars of the state are about to fall.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2341 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2341*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世爻所居之卦为国。 | 世爻所在的那个单卦就是国家。 | The trigram in which the Self line sits is the state. |
| 旺相，则国治，而天下平也。 | 旺相，国家就治理得好，天下太平。 | If it is thriving, the state is well governed and the world is at peace. |
| 胎没次之，死囚休废，则国祚陵夷也。 | 胎、没次一等；死、囚、休、废，国运就衰落下去。 | The stages Tai and Mo come next; if it is dead, imprisoned, resting or abandoned, the dynasty declines. |
| 空亡，则巡游无度也。 | 落空亡，君主就巡游没有节制。 | If it falls into Void, the sovereign tours without restraint. |
| 冲破则疆围不固也。 | 被冲、被破，疆界就不稳固。 | If it is clashed or broken, the borders are not secure. |
| 刑克，则奸宄窃发也。 | 被刑、被克，奸邪作乱的人就暗中起事。 | If it is punished or controlled, treacherous rebels rise in secret. |
| 受害，则臣庶离心也。 | 受害，臣子百姓就离心。 | If it is harmed, ministers and commoners lose heart. |
| 岁生旺官者，国运炎隆也。 | 太岁生旺这一宫的，国运炽盛。 | If Tai Sui generates a thriving palace, the state's fortune is blazing. |
| 岁克衰卦者，宗社将倾也。 | 太岁克制衰弱的卦的，宗庙社稷将要倾覆。 | If Tai Sui controls a weak trigram, the ancestral temple and altars are about to fall. |

> **按**:「旺相胎没死囚休废」是单卦随季节的八种状态，书里没有逐一解释（征战占「方向」把八个卦在八个节气里的状态逐项列了出来，见 YY.51），译文把前四个当作由好到差的顺序，后四个当作衰败。第八句「岁生旺官」的「官」，和后面「旺宫」对照应是「宫」字抄错，译文按「宫」译；第九句「岁克衰卦」正是同一对仗。「宗社」是宗庙和社稷，代指国家。
>
> **Note:** The eight states, thriving, assisting, Tai, Mo, dead, imprisoned, resting and abandoned, are the states of a trigram through the seasons; the book does not explain each (the 'direction' section of the divination of war sets out the state of each of the eight trigrams in the eight solar terms, see YY.51), and the translation takes the first four as a descent from good to poor and the last four as decline. In the eighth sentence the character for 'official' in 'thriving official' is, compared with 'thriving palace' later on, presumably a miscopy for 'palace', and the translation follows 'palace'; the ninth sentence, 'Tai Sui controls a weak trigram', is the same pair. 'Zongshe' is the ancestral temple and the altars of soil and grain, standing for the state.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.31、LY.CAREER.YY.33、LY.CAREER.YY.51、LY.CAREER.HJC.64、LY.CAREER.HJC.66、LY.CAREER.HJC.67

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.33 朝廷占·大象（二）：鬼带亡劫在内卦动防奸细，暗动伤身世有阴谋；金鬼带大杀临旺宫动兵革兴，水洪浸，火旱魃，木稼穑痒，土疫疠；鬼空动虽凶无害

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:大象，灾变：官鬼带亡神、劫杀，在内卦发动的，要提防奸细；官鬼带亡神、劫杀，暗中发动而伤身爻、世爻的，有阴谋。金爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动，兵祸起；水爻的，有洪水浸泡之灾；火爻的，旱魃肆虐；木爻的，庄稼受害；土爻的，瘟疫流行。官鬼落空而发动的，虽然凶也没有大害。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The great image, disasters and upheavals. If Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves in the inner trigram, beware of spies; if Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves secretly to injure the Body and Self, there is a plot. If a Metal Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, war arises; if Water, flood disaster; if Fire, the drought demon rages; if Wood, the crops are blighted; if Earth, pestilence breaks out. If Pressure is Void and moves, though it is ominous it does no harm.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2341–2343 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, lines 2341–2343*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼带亡劫在内卦动者，防奸细也。 | 官鬼带亡神、劫杀，在内卦发动的，要提防奸细。 | If Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves in the inner trigram, beware of spies. |
| 鬼带亡劫，暗动伤身世者，有阴谋也。 | 官鬼带亡神、劫杀，暗中发动而伤身爻、世爻的，有阴谋。 | If Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves secretly to injure the Body and Self, there is a plot. |
| 金鬼带大杀，临旺官动者，兵革兴也。 | 金爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动的，兵祸兴起。 | If a Metal Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, war arises. |
| 水鬼带大杀，临旺宫动者，洪浸灾也。 | 水爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动的，有洪水浸泡之灾。 | If a Water Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, there is the disaster of flood. |
| 火鬼带大杀，临旺宫动者，旱魃虐也。 | 火爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动的，旱魃肆虐。 | If a Fire Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, the drought demon rages. |
| 木鬼带大杀，临旺宫动者，稼穑痒也。 | 木爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动的，庄稼受害。 | If a Wood Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, the crops are blighted. |
| 土鬼带大杀，临旺宫动者，疫疠作也。 | 土爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动的，瘟疫流行。 | If an Earth Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, pestilence breaks out. |
| 鬼空动者，虽凶无害也。 | 官鬼落空而发动的，虽然凶也没有大害。 | If Pressure is Void and moves, though it is ominous it does no harm. |

> **按**:第十一句「临旺官动」的「官」和后四句的「旺宫」对照，应是「宫」字抄错，译文按「宫」译。「旱魃」是传说中造成旱灾的鬼怪。「稼穑痒」的「痒」字面是发痒，疑是「恙」（病害）之误，译文按庄稼受害译。「亡劫」是亡神和劫杀（见 YY.20）。
>
> **Note:** In the eleventh sentence the character for 'official' in 'thriving official' is, compared with 'thriving palace' in the four sentences after it, presumably a miscopy for 'palace', and the translation follows 'palace'. 'Hanba' is the legendary demon that brings drought. In 'jiase yang', 'yang' literally means itchy and is presumably a miswriting of 'yang', sickness and harm, so the crops being blighted is what is translated. 'Wangjie' is the Lost Spirit and Robbery (see YY.20).

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.32、LY.CAREER.YY.34、LY.CAREER.HJC.64

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.34 朝廷占·大象（三）：欲知何方以八卦定；鬼杀值胎养长生乱方始，临官帝旺乱正炽，死墓绝乱将止；离动化坎北狩，兑动化震东迁，坎动化离南渡，震动化兑西征；逢六合安静太平，逢六冲乱动多变故

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:大象，方位、祸乱的阶段和迁徙：想知道事情在哪个方向，用八卦来定。官鬼和凶煞值胎、养、长生，祸乱刚开始；值临官、帝旺，祸乱正炽盛；值死、墓、绝，祸乱将要平息。离卦发动变坎卦，君主将要北狩；兑卦发动变震卦，想往东迁；坎卦发动变离卦，想往南渡；震卦发动变兑卦，必定西征。卦遇六合，安静的太平；遇六冲、乱动的，变故很多。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The great image, direction, the stage of the disorder, and moving the court. To know in which direction, fix it by the eight trigrams. If Pressure and a baleful spirit are in the stages of Tai, Yang or Birth, the disorder is just beginning; in Official Post or Emperor's Peak, the disorder is at its height; in death, tomb or extinction, the disorder is about to cease. Li moving and turning into Kan: the sovereign will go on a northern hunt; Dui moving and turning into Zhen: he thinks of moving east; Kan moving and turning into Li: he wishes to cross to the south; Zhen moving and turning into Dui: he will surely march west. If the hexagram is a six-combine and still, there is peace; if it meets the six-clash and moves wildly, there are many upheavals.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2343 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2343*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲知何方，以八卦定之也。 | 想知道事情在哪个方向，用八卦来定。 | To know in which direction, fix it by the eight trigrams. |
| 又鬼杀值胎养长生者，乱方始也。 | 又，官鬼和凶煞值胎、养、长生的，祸乱刚开始。 | Again, if Pressure and a baleful spirit are in the stages of Tai, Yang or Birth, the disorder is just beginning. |
| 值临官帝旺者，乱正炽也。 | 值临官、帝旺的，祸乱正炽盛。 | If they are in Official Post or Emperor's Peak, the disorder is at its height. |
| 值死墓绝者，乱将止也。 | 值死、墓、绝的，祸乱将要平息。 | If they are in death, tomb or extinction, the disorder is about to cease. |
| 离动化坎，将北狩也。 | 离卦发动变坎卦，君主将要北狩。 | Li moving and turning into Kan: the sovereign will go on a northern hunt. |
| 兑动化震，思东迁也。 | 兑卦发动变震卦，想往东迁。 | Dui moving and turning into Zhen: he thinks of moving east. |
| 坎动化离，欲南渡也。 | 坎卦发动变离卦，想往南渡。 | Kan moving and turning into Li: he wishes to cross to the south. |
| 震动化兑，必西征也。 | 震卦发动变兑卦，必定西征。 | Zhen moving and turning into Dui: he will surely march west. |
| 卦逢六合，安静者太平，逢六冲乱动者，则多变故也。 | 卦遇六合，安静的太平；遇六冲、乱动的，变故很多。 | If the hexagram is a six-combine and still, there is peace; if it meets the six-clash and moves wildly, there are many upheavals. |

> **按**:「北狩」是天子被迫离开京城、出走或被掳往北方的委婉说法（宋徽宗、钦宗和明英宗都被说成北狩）；「南渡」是朝廷迁往南方；「东迁」「西征」按字面。四句是按八卦方位配的：离在南、坎在北、兑在西、震在东，变成的那一卦所在的方位，就是去的方向。这个读法是书里没有明写的，译文只照字面译四句。「胎养长生」「临官帝旺」是十二长生的位置，「六合」「六冲」指卦的六合、六冲（见术语表）。
>
> **Note:** 'Beishou', the northern hunt, is the euphemism for the Son of Heaven being driven out of the capital, fleeing or being carried off to the north (the Huizong and Qinzong emperors of the Song and the Yingzong emperor of the Ming are all said to have gone on a northern hunt); 'nandu' is the court crossing to the south; 'eastward move' and 'westward march' are by the letter. The four sentences are matched with the directions of the eight trigrams: Li is south, Kan north, Dui west and Zhen east, and the direction of the trigram it changes into is the direction he goes. This reading is not written out in the book, and the translation follows only the letter of the four sentences. 'Tai, Yang, Birth' and 'Official Post, Emperor's Peak' are positions of the twelve stages, and 'six-combine' and 'six-clash' are the hexagram patterns (see the glossary).

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.33、LY.CAREER.YY.35、LY.CAREER.HJC.70、LY.CAREER.HJC.71、LY.CAREER.HJC.84、LY.CAREER.HJC.85

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.35 朝廷占·国君（一）：世与五爻旺相居阳宫阳爻者明君，旺静福主，带龙喜德合福贵动者仁主，加刑杀威震臣邻，带虎鬼大杀刑刃旺动暴虐，带玄武咸池动荒淫，带兄耗羊刃动多靡，带虎鬼死符病符动倦勤，休囚被太岁冲克随鬼入墓殂落不久

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:国君的品性和寿数：世爻与五爻旺相，又在阳宫的阳爻上的，是明君；旺相而安静的，是有福的君主；带青龙、喜神、德神、合神、福、贵人而发动的，是仁君；加上刑杀，威严震动臣下和邻国；带白虎、官鬼、大杀、刑、刃，旺相发动的，暴虐无道，肆意诛杀夷灭；带玄武、咸池发动的，荒淫无度；带兄爻、耗神、羊刃发动的，多奢靡而不节制；带白虎、官鬼、死符、病符发动的，身体常常倦怠，懒于政事；休囚，又被太岁冲克，以及随着官鬼入墓的，不久就驾崩。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The character and span of the sovereign. If the Self and the fifth line are thriving and sit on a yang line in a yang palace, he is a wise ruler; if thriving and still, a fortunate ruler; if they carry the Dragon, Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune and the Noble and move, a benevolent ruler; with punishing baleful spirits added, his majesty shakes ministers and neighbours; if they carry the Tiger, Pressure, a great baleful spirit, punishment and the blade, thriving and moving, he is cruel and lawless and gives free rein to executions and exterminations; if they carry the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi and move, he is dissolute without limit; if they carry a Peer line, the Waste spirit and the Goat Blade and move, he is extravagant and without restraint; if they carry the Tiger, Pressure, the Death Tally and the Illness Tally and move, he is often in poor health and tired of government; if they are resting or trapped and are clashed or controlled by Tai Sui, or follow Pressure into the tomb, he will not last long.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2347 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2347*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世与五爻旺相，居阳宫阳爻者，明君。 | 世爻与五爻旺相，又在阳宫的阳爻上的，是明君。 | If the Self and the fifth line are thriving and sit on a yang line in a yang palace, he is a wise ruler. |
| 旺静则福主也，带龙喜德合福贵动者，仁主。 | 旺相而安静的，是有福的君主；带青龙、喜神、德神、合神、福、贵人而发动的，是仁君。 | If they are thriving and still, he is a fortunate ruler; if they carry the Dragon, Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune and the Noble and move, he is a benevolent ruler. |
| 加刑杀则威震臣邻也，带虎鬼大杀刑刃旺动者暴虐不道肆情诛夷也。 | 加上刑杀，威严震动臣下和邻国；带白虎、官鬼、大杀、刑、刃，旺相发动的，暴虐无道，肆意诛杀夷灭。 | With punishing baleful spirits added, his majesty shakes ministers and neighbours; if they carry the Tiger, Pressure, a great baleful spirit, punishment and the blade, thriving and moving, he is cruel and lawless and gives free rein to executions and exterminations. |
| 带玄武咸池动者，荒淫无度也。 | 带玄武、咸池发动的，荒淫无度。 | If they carry the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi and move, he is dissolute without limit. |
| 带兄耗羊刃动者，多靡不节也。 | 带兄爻、耗神、羊刃发动的，多奢靡而不节制。 | If they carry a Peer line, the Waste spirit and the Goat Blade and move, he is extravagant and without restraint. |
| 带虎鬼死符病符动者，体常倦勤也。 | 带白虎、官鬼、死符、病符发动的，身体常常倦怠，懒于政事。 | If they carry the Tiger, Pressure, the Death Tally and the Illness Tally and move, he is often in poor health and tired of government. |
| 休囚而被太岁冲克，与随鬼入墓者，殂落不久也。 | 休囚，又被太岁冲克，以及随着官鬼入墓的，不久就驾崩。 | If they are resting or trapped and are clashed or controlled by Tai Sui, or follow Pressure into the tomb, he will not last long. |

> **按**:「福主」按字面是有福的君主；「倦勤」是厌倦、懒于政事；「殂落」是死亡，用于君主就是驾崩。喜、德、合、福、贵、耗、死符、病符、羊刃、大杀都是神煞，书里没有逐个解释。
>
> **Note:** 'Fuzhu' is by the letter a fortunate ruler; 'juanqin' is being tired of and slack in government; 'culuo' is death, which for a sovereign means demise. Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune, the Noble, the Waste spirit, the Death Tally, the Illness Tally, the Goat Blade and the great baleful spirits are all spirit-stars that the book does not explain one by one.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.34、LY.CAREER.YY.36、LY.CAREER.HJC.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.67、LY.CAREER.HJC.68

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.36 朝廷占·国君（二）：土鬼旺动伤世贼攻城；财带阴贵大杀动伤世五爻妇寺专权；父带禄贵大杀动伤世五爻勋戚尾大不掉；兄带阴贵大杀动伤世五爻贵戚骄恣；子带华盖大杀动伤世五爻妖僧方士蛊惑君心

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:国君身边的祸患：土爻的官鬼旺相发动、伤世爻的，贼寇攻城；财爻带阴贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，后宫妇人和宦官专权，蛀蚀国家；父爻带禄、贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，功臣外戚尾大不掉；兄爻带阴贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，皇亲国戚倚仗恩宠骄横放纵；子孙爻带华盖、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，妖僧和方士蛊惑君主的心。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Dangers about the sovereign. If an Earth Pressure line is thriving and moving and injures the Self, bandits attack the city; if the Wealth line carrying the Yin Noble and a great baleful spirit moves to injure the Self and the fifth line, women of the palace and eunuchs seize power and eat away at the state; if the Resource line carrying Emolument, the Noble and a great baleful spirit does so, the meritorious ministers and imperial in-laws become too big to control; if the Peer line carrying the Yin Noble and a great baleful spirit does so, the imperial kin presume on favour and are arrogant and unbridled; if the Output line carrying Canopy and a great baleful spirit does so, evil monks and alchemists bewitch the sovereign's mind.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2347 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2347*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土鬼旺动伤世者，贼攻城也。 | 土爻的官鬼旺相发动、伤世爻的，贼寇攻城。 | If an Earth Pressure line is thriving and moving and injures the Self, bandits attack the city. |
| 财带阴贵大杀动伤世爻五爻者，妇寺专权蠹国也。 | 财爻带阴贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，后宫妇人和宦官专权，蛀蚀国家。 | If the Wealth line carrying the Yin Noble and a great baleful spirit moves to injure the Self line and the fifth line, women of the palace and eunuchs seize power and eat away at the state. |
| 父带禄贵大杀，动伤世爻五爻者，勋戚尾大不悼也。 | 父爻带禄、贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，功臣外戚尾大不掉。 | If the Resource line carrying Emolument, the Noble and a great baleful spirit moves to injure the Self line and the fifth line, the meritorious ministers and imperial in-laws become too big to control. |
| 兄带阴贵大杀，动伤世爻五爻者，贵戚怙宠骄恣也。 | 兄爻带阴贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，皇亲国戚倚仗恩宠骄横放纵。 | If the Peer line carrying the Yin Noble and a great baleful spirit moves to injure the Self line and the fifth line, the imperial kin presume on favour and are arrogant and unbridled. |
| 子带华盖大杀，动伤世爻五爻者，妖僧方士蛊惑君心也。 | 子孙爻带华盖、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，妖僧和方士蛊惑君主的心。 | If the Output line carrying Canopy and a great baleful spirit moves to injure the Self line and the fifth line, evil monks and alchemists bewitch the sovereign's mind. |

> **按**:「妇寺」是后宫的妇人和寺人（宦官）；「勋戚」是功臣和外戚；「尾大不悼」的「悼」疑是「掉」（尾大不掉），译文按「掉」译；「方士」是炼丹求仙的术士；「阴贵」是阴贵人，「华盖」是一颗主孤高、宗教的神煞，书里没有解释。
>
> **Note:** 'Fusi' is the women of the palace and the 'si', the eunuchs; 'xunqi' is the meritorious ministers and the imperial in-laws; in 'weida budao' the character 'dao', grief, is presumably a miswriting of 'diao', to swing, in the saying 'the tail too big to wag', and the translation follows it; 'fangshi' are the magicians who make elixirs and seek immortality; 'Yin Noble' is the yin form of the Noble, and 'Canopy' is a spirit-star of aloofness and religion that the book does not explain.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.35、LY.CAREER.YY.37、LY.CAREER.HJC.82

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.37 朝廷占·后妃：应爻二爻与世爻五爻生合比和好逑；应克世二克五脱环进规；带龙喜德合福贵旺相女中尧舜；带玄武咸池艳妻煽处；带父母旺相攻书史；带子孙旺相繁螽斯；子动生财能逮下；应居五爻阳位加太岁紫微华盖贵马旺相聚一爻垂帘听政

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:后妃：应爻、二爻与世爻、五爻相生、相合、比和的，是好配偶；应爻克世爻，二爻克五爻的，皇后摘下环佩进言规劝；应爻、二爻带青龙、喜神、德神、合神、福、贵人，旺相的，是女中的尧舜；带玄武、咸池的，是艳妻煽动祸乱当权的格局；带父母爻旺相的，专攻书史；带子孙爻旺相的，像螽斯一样子孙众多；子孙爻发动生财爻的，能恩惠及下；应爻落在五爻的阳位上，又加太岁、紫微、华盖、贵人、驿马，旺相，都聚在一爻的，垂帘听政。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Consort and empress. If the Other and the second line generate, combine or are in harmony with the Self and the fifth line, they are a fine match; if the Other controls the Self and the second line controls the fifth, the empress takes off her ring and gives admonition; if the Other and the second line carry the Dragon, Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune and the Noble and are thriving, she is a Yao and Shun among women; if they carry the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi, it is the pattern of the beautiful wife who stirs up trouble and holds sway; if they carry Resource thriving, she devotes herself to books and histories; if they carry Output thriving, her descendants are as many as katydids; if Output moves to generate Wealth, she is bounteous to those below; if the Other sits on a yang position of the fifth line with Tai Sui, Ziwei, Canopy, the Noble and the Horse added, thriving and all gathered in one line, she rules from behind the curtain.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2351 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2351*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应爻二爻与世爻五爻生合比和者好逑也。 | 应爻、二爻与世爻、五爻相生、相合、比和的，是好配偶。 | If the Other and the second line generate, combine or are in harmony with the Self and the fifth line, they are a fine match. |
| 应爻克世，二爻克五者，脱环进规也。 | 应爻克世爻，二爻克五爻的，皇后摘下环佩、进言规劝。 | If the Other controls the Self and the second line controls the fifth, the empress takes off her ring and gives admonition. |
| 应爻二爻带龙喜德合福贵旺相者，女中尧舜也。 | 应爻、二爻带青龙、喜神、德神、合神、福、贵人，旺相的，是女中的尧舜。 | If the Other and the second line carry the Dragon, Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune and the Noble and are thriving, she is a Yao and Shun among women. |
| 带玄武咸池者，艳妻煽处也。 | 带玄武、咸池的，是艳妻煽动祸乱、当权的格局。 | If they carry the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi, it is the pattern of the beautiful wife who stirs up trouble and holds sway. |
| 带父母旺相者，攻书史也。 | 带父母爻旺相的，专攻书史。 | If they carry Resource and are thriving, she devotes herself to books and histories. |
| 带子孙旺相者，繁螽斯也。 | 带子孙爻旺相的，像螽斯一样子孙众多。 | If they carry Output and are thriving, her descendants are as many as katydids. |
| 子动生财者，能逮下也。 | 子孙爻发动生财爻的，能恩惠及下。 | If Output moves to generate Wealth, she is bounteous to those below. |
| 应居五爻阳位加太岁，紫微华盖贵马，旺相聚于一爻者，垂帘听政也。 | 应爻落在五爻的阳位上，又加太岁、紫微、华盖、贵人、驿马，旺相，都聚在一爻的，垂帘听政。 | If the Other sits on a yang position of the fifth line with Tai Sui, Ziwei, Canopy, the Noble and the Horse added, thriving and all gathered in one line, she rules from behind the curtain. |

> **按**:「脱环进规」按字面是摘下环佩、进言规劝，书里没有解释，译文照字面。「艳妻煽处」出自《诗经·十月之交》「艳妻煽方处」，说美貌的妻子煽动祸乱、当权。「螽斯」出自《诗经·螽斯》，比喻子孙众多；「逮下」出自《诗经·樛木》，指恩惠及于下人；「垂帘听政」是太后（皇后）隔着帘子代皇帝处理政事。「好逑」是好配偶。紫微是一颗神煞。
>
> **Note:** 'Tuo huan jin gui' is by the letter taking off the ring and giving counsel, which the book does not explain, and the translation follows the letter. 'Yan qi shan chu' comes from the Odes, 'Shi yue zhi jiao', 'the beautiful wife stirs up trouble and holds sway'. 'Zhongsi', the katydid, is from the Odes, 'Zhongsi', an image of many descendants; 'daixia' is from the Odes, 'Jiumu', and means favour reaching those below; 'chuilian tingzheng' is the empress dowager handling affairs behind a curtain in place of the emperor. 'Haoqiu' is a good match. Ziwei is a spirit-star.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.36、LY.CAREER.YY.38、LY.CAREER.HJC.69、LY.CAREER.HJC.74

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.38 朝廷占·青宫：以子孙为用；三传动爻生合子孙或子孙带龙德贵马福禄旺相者贤而有德；福临木爻生旺储德必厚；世生合子孙得主眷，世冲克刑害子孙忤主心；子临劫杀大杀动必更立；子被四爻刑害克破防篡逆；四爻带龙德生合子保傅贤，带武鬼咸池合福保傅邪佞；子带咸池偏宫出

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:太子（青宫）：以子孙为用神。三传（太岁、月建、日辰）、动爻生合子孙，或者子孙带青龙、德神、贵人、驿马、福、禄而旺相的，太子贤而有德；福神临在木爻上，生旺的，储君的德行必定深厚（青宫属木）；世爻生合子孙的，太子得到君主的眷顾；世爻冲、克、刑、害子孙的，太子违逆君主的心意；子孙爻临劫杀、大杀而发动的，太子必定被更换；子孙爻被四爻刑、害、克、破的，要提防篡逆；四爻带青龙、德神，生合子孙爻的，太子的师保贤明；四爻带玄武、官鬼、咸池，又合福神的，师保奸邪谄佞；子孙爻带咸池的，太子是偏宫所生。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Azure Palace, the heir apparent. Take Output as the Subject. If the three rulers or a moving line generate or combine with Output, or Output carries the Dragon, Virtue, the Noble, the Horse, Fortune and Emolument and is thriving, the heir is worthy and virtuous; if the fortune spirit sits on a Wood line and is born or thriving, the heir's stored virtue is surely deep (the Azure Palace belongs to Wood); if the Self generates or combines with Output, the heir wins the sovereign's regard; if the Self clashes with, controls, punishes or harms Output, the heir goes against the sovereign's mind; if Output sits on Robbery and a great baleful spirit and moves, the heir will surely be replaced; if Output is punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the fourth line, beware of usurpation; if the fourth line carrying the Dragon and Virtue generates or combines with Output, the heir's tutors are worthy; if the fourth line carrying the Dark Tortoise, Pressure and Xianchi combines with the fortune spirit, the tutors are wicked and flattering; if Output carries Xianchi, the heir is born of a secondary palace.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2355 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2355*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以子孙为用。 | 以子孙为用神。 | Take Output as the Subject. |
| 凡三传动爻生合子孙，或子孙带龙德贵马福禄旺相者，贤而有德也。 | 凡是三传（太岁、月建、日辰）、动爻生合子孙，或者子孙带青龙、德神、贵人、驿马、福、禄而旺相的，太子贤而有德。 | Whenever the three rulers or a moving line generate or combine with Output, or Output carries the Dragon, Virtue, the Noble, the Horse, Fortune and Emolument and is thriving, the heir is worthy and virtuous. |
| 福临木爻生旺者，储德必厚也。 | 福神临在木爻上，生旺的，储君的德行必定深厚。 | If the fortune spirit sits on a Wood line and is born or thriving, the heir's stored virtue is surely deep. |
| (青宫属木)。 | （青宫五行属木。） | (The Azure Palace belongs to Wood.) |
| 世生合子孙者，得主眷也。 | 世爻生合子孙的，太子得到君主的眷顾。 | If the Self generates or combines with Output, the heir wins the sovereign's regard. |
| 世冲克刑害子孙者，忤主心也。 | 世爻冲、克、刑、害子孙的，太子违逆君主的心意。 | If the Self clashes with, controls, punishes or harms Output, the heir goes against the sovereign's mind. |
| 子临劫杀大杀动者，必更立也。 | 子孙爻临劫杀、大杀而发动的，太子必定被更换。 | If Output sits on Robbery and a great baleful spirit and moves, the heir will surely be replaced. |
| 子被四爻刑害克破者，防篡逆也。 | 子孙爻被四爻刑、害、克、破的，要提防篡逆。 | If Output is punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the fourth line, beware of usurpation. |
| 四爻带龙德生合子者，保传贤也。 | 四爻带青龙、德神，生合子孙爻的，太子的师保贤明。 | If the fourth line carrying the Dragon and Virtue generates or combines with Output, the heir's tutors are worthy. |
| 四爻带武鬼咸池，合福者，保传邪侫也。 | 四爻带玄武、官鬼、咸池，又合福神的，师保奸邪谄佞。 | If the fourth line carrying the Dark Tortoise, Pressure and Xianchi combines with the fortune spirit, the tutors are wicked and flattering. |
| 子带咸池者，偏宫出也。 | 子孙爻带咸池的，太子是偏宫所生。 | If Output carries Xianchi, the heir is born of a secondary palace. |

> **按**:「青宫」是太子的东宫，东方属木，所以说「青宫属木」。「保傅」是辅导太子的师保。「偏宫」是正宫皇后以外的宫，即妃嫔所居，「偏宫出」就是妃嫔所生。「武鬼」是玄武和官鬼的省称，按这样译。
>
> **Note:** The 'Azure Palace' is the Eastern Palace of the heir apparent, and since the east belongs to Wood it is said that 'the Azure Palace belongs to Wood'. 'Baofu' are the tutors who guide the heir. 'Pian gong' is a palace other than that of the empress, where the consorts live, and 'born of the secondary palace' means born of a consort. 'Wugui' is an abbreviation of the Dark Tortoise and Pressure, and is translated so.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.37、LY.CAREER.YY.39、LY.CAREER.HJC.75、LY.CAREER.HJC.76、LY.CAREER.HJC.77、LY.CAREER.HJC.78、LY.CAREER.HJC.79

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.39 朝廷占·谏官：以子孙为用；子动克世直臣匡敕，刑冲世直臣撄鳞，合世谀臣将顺；子临岁月动克四爻生合五爻，子生旺日必有直臣纠劾权臣；子动克四五爻与日辰生合可其奏，与日辰刑害克冲疏不允反遭贬责

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:进谏的官员：以子孙为用神。子孙爻发动克世爻的，正直的臣子匡正、告诫君主；子孙爻发动刑、冲世爻的，正直的臣子冒死触犯君主的逆鳞；子孙爻发动合世爻的，谄谀的臣子顺从迎合。子孙爻临太岁、月建而发动，克四爻、生合五爻的，到子孙生旺的日子，必有正直的臣子弹劾权臣。子孙爻发动，克四爻、五爻，又得日辰生合的，君主准其奏章；与日辰刑、害、克、冲的，奏疏既不被允准，反而遭到贬官责罚。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The remonstrating officials. Take Output as the Subject. If Output moves and controls the Self, an upright minister corrects and warns the sovereign; if it moves to punish or clash with the Self, an upright minister touches the dragon's reversed scales; if it moves to combine with the Self, a flattering minister goes along. If Output sits on Tai Sui or the Month and moves, controlling the fourth line and generating or combining with the fifth, then on a day when Output is born or thriving there will surely be an upright minister impeaching a powerful one. If Output moves and controls the fourth and fifth lines and the Day generates or combines with it, his memorial is approved; if the Day punishes, harms, controls or clashes with it, the memorial is not allowed and he is instead demoted and rebuked.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2359 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2359*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以子孙为用。 | 以子孙为用神。 | Take Output as the Subject. |
| 子动克世者，直臣匡敕也。 | 子孙爻发动克世爻的，正直的臣子匡正、告诫君主。 | If Output moves and controls the Self, an upright minister corrects and warns the sovereign. |
| 子动刑冲世者，直臣撄鳞也。 | 子孙爻发动刑、冲世爻的，正直的臣子冒死触犯君主的逆鳞。 | If Output moves to punish or clash with the Self, an upright minister touches the dragon's reversed scales. |
| 子动合世者，谀臣将顺也。 | 子孙爻发动合世爻的，谄谀的臣子顺从迎合。 | If Output moves to combine with the Self, a flattering minister goes along. |
| 子临岁月动克四爻，生合五爻者，子生旺日，必有直臣纠劾权臣也。 | 子孙爻临太岁、月建而发动，克四爻、生合五爻的，到子孙生旺的日子，必有正直的臣子弹劾权臣。 | If Output sits on Tai Sui or the Month and moves, controlling the fourth line and generating or combining with the fifth, then on a day when Output is born or thriving there will surely be an upright minister impeaching a powerful one. |
| 子动，克四五爻，与日辰生合者，可其奏也。 | 子孙爻发动，克四爻、五爻，又得日辰生合的，君主准其奏章。 | If Output moves and controls the fourth and fifth lines and the Day generates or combines with it, his memorial is approved. |
| 与日辰刑害克冲者，疏既不允，反遭贬责也。 | 与日辰刑、害、克、冲的，奏疏既不被允准，反而遭到贬官责罚。 | If the Day punishes, harms, controls or clashes with it, the memorial is not allowed and he is instead demoted and rebuked. |

> **按**:「撄鳞」是触犯逆鳞：龙喉下有倒生的鳞，碰到它龙就会杀人，比喻触怒君主。「匡敕」是匡正、整饬。「将顺」是顺从。第六句里的「可其奏」按字面是认可他的奏章。
>
> **Note:** 'Yinglin' is touching the reversed scales: a dragon has a scale growing the wrong way under its throat, and whoever touches it is killed, an image of angering the sovereign. 'Kuangchi' is correcting and straightening out. 'Jiangshun' is going along. In the sixth sentence, 'approve his memorial' is by the letter to allow his memorial.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.38、LY.CAREER.YY.40、LY.CAREER.HJC.26、LY.CAREER.HJC.83

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.40 朝廷占·城池：以父母为用；旺相逢生合则坚固，休囚遇伤克则疏虞；父下伏鬼或父动化鬼须防奸细临城

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:城池：以父母为用神。父母爻旺相又遇生合，城池坚固；休囚又遇伤克，防守疏漏；父爻下面伏着官鬼，或父爻发动变官鬼的，须提防奸细到城下。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** City walls and moats. Take Resource as the Subject. If it is thriving and meets generation or combination, the walls are firm; if it is resting or trapped and meets injury or control, the defence is lax; if Pressure lies hidden under the Resource line, or Resource moves and turns into Pressure, beware of spies at the city.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2363 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2363*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以父母为用。 | 以父母为用神。 | Take Resource as the Subject. |
| 旺相逢生合，则坚固。 | 旺相又遇生合，就坚固。 | If it is thriving and meets generation or combination, the walls are firm. |
| 休囚遇伤克，则疏虞也。 | 休囚又遇伤克，就防守疏漏。 | If it is resting or trapped and meets injury or control, the defence is lax. |
| 父下伏鬼，或父动化鬼者，须防奸细临城也。 | 父爻下面伏着官鬼，或父爻发动变官鬼的，须提防奸细到城下。 | If Pressure lies hidden under the Resource line, or Resource moves and turns into Pressure, beware of spies at the city. |

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.39、LY.CAREER.YY.41、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

## LY.CAREER.YY.41 朝廷占·帑藏：以妻财为用；旺相逢生合丰盈缄固，败死绝空逢刑害克破则匮之漏卮；兄爻旺动多侵蚀，鬼爻旺动多耗费

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:国库：以妻财为用神。财爻旺相又遇生合，国库丰盈、封存牢固；衰败、死、绝、落空，又遇刑、害、克、破，国库就空虚，像漏了的酒器一样不断外泄；兄爻旺相发动，多被侵吞蚕食；官鬼爻旺相发动，多有耗费。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The treasury. Take Wealth as the Subject. If it is thriving and meets generation or combination, the treasury is full and securely sealed; if it is spent, dead, extinct or Void and meets punishment, harm, control or breaking, the treasury is empty, like a leaking vessel; if the Peer line is thriving and moving, there is much embezzlement; if the Pressure line is thriving and moving, there is much expenditure.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2367 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2367*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以妻财为用。 | 以妻财为用神。 | Take Wealth as the Subject. |
| 旺相逢生合，则丰盈缄固。 | 财爻旺相又遇生合，国库就丰盈、封存牢固。 | If it is thriving and meets generation or combination, the treasury is full and securely sealed. |
| 败死绝空，逢刑害克破，则匮之漏巵也。 | 衰败、死、绝、落空，又遇刑、害、克、破，国库就空虚，像漏了的酒器一样不断外泄。 | If it is spent, dead, extinct or Void and meets punishment, harm, control or breaking, the treasury is empty, like a leaking vessel. |
| 兄爻旺动，则多侵蚀。 | 兄爻旺相发动，多被侵吞蚕食。 | If the Peer line is thriving and moving, there is much embezzlement. |
| 鬼爻旺动，则多耗费也。 | 官鬼爻旺相发动，就多有耗费。 | If the Pressure line is thriving and moving, there is much expenditure. |

> **按**:「帑藏」是国库。「匮之漏卮」的「卮」是酒器，「漏卮」比喻财富不断外泄。「缄固」是封存牢固。
>
> **Note:** 'Tangcang' is the state treasury. In 'kui zhi lou zhi', 'zhi' is a wine vessel, and a 'leaking vessel' is an image of wealth running out without stop. 'Jiangu' is being sealed up firmly.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.40、LY.CAREER.YY.42、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

## LY.CAREER.YY.42 朝廷占·奸贼：以官鬼为用；鬼带杀刃动在乾坤卦祸起宫中，在坎离震兑祸起四方；木鬼东寇，金鬼西贼，火鬼南盗，水鬼北酋，土鬼四方散起

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:奸贼：以官鬼为用神。官鬼带凶煞和刃发动，在乾卦、坤卦的，祸乱起于宫中；在坎、离、震、兑的，祸乱起于四方。木爻的官鬼，是东方的寇盗；金爻的，是西方的贼；火爻的，是南方的盗；水爻的，是北方的酋首；土爻的，是四方散起的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Traitors and bandits. Take Pressure as the Subject. If Pressure carrying baleful spirits and the blade moves in Qian or Kun, the trouble arises within the palace; in Kan, Li, Zhen or Dui, it arises in the four quarters. A Wood Pressure line is brigands from the east; Metal, rebels from the west; Fire, robbers from the south; Water, a chieftain from the north; Earth, risings in scattered places on all sides.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2371 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2371*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以官鬼为用。 | 以官鬼为用神。 | Take Pressure as the Subject. |
| 鬼带杀刃动，在乾坤卦，则祸起官中。 | 官鬼带凶煞和刃发动，在乾卦、坤卦的，祸乱起于宫中。 | If Pressure carrying baleful spirits and the blade moves in Qian or Kun, the trouble arises within the palace. |
| 在坎离震兑，则祸起四方也。 | 在坎、离、震、兑的，祸乱起于四方。 | In Kan, Li, Zhen or Dui, it arises in the four quarters. |
| 又木鬼，东寇也。 | 又，木爻的官鬼，是东方的寇盗。 | Again, a Wood Pressure line is brigands from the east. |
| 金鬼，西贼也。 | 金爻的官鬼，是西方的贼。 | A Metal Pressure line is rebels from the west. |
| 火鬼，南盗也。 | 火爻的官鬼，是南方的盗。 | A Fire Pressure line is robbers from the south. |
| 水鬼，北酋也。 | 水爻的官鬼，是北方的酋首。 | A Water Pressure line is a chieftain from the north. |
| 土鬼，四方散起也。 | 土爻的官鬼，是四方散起的。 | An Earth Pressure line is risings in scattered places on all sides. |

> **按**:第二句「祸起官中」的「官」，按文意应是「宫」（宫中，即皇宫之内），因为乾坤是君与后，译文按「宫」译。
>
> **Note:** In the second sentence the character for 'official' in 'trouble arises in the official middle' is presumably a miscopy for 'palace' (within the palace), since Qian and Kun are the sovereign and the empress, and the translation follows 'palace'.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.41、LY.CAREER.YY.43、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.43 朝廷占·分官（一）：初爻临鬼黔首灾，二爻临鬼士子灾，三爻持兄鬼吏多贪酷——空则民离心/士不豫附/无良吏，生旺则民安物阜/文运盛/善政超迁，衰而受伤则民遭涂炭/士屈抑/贬秩褫职

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:分官（一），按爻位看百姓、士人和官吏：初爻临官鬼，百姓有灾；落空，百姓有离心，生旺，百姓安居物产丰盛，衰弱受伤，百姓遭涂炭。二爻临官鬼，读书人有灾；落空，读书人不乐意归附；金爻落空而发动，读书人多乱发议论；生旺又加文昌，文运昌盛；衰弱受伤，读书人多受压抑。三爻持兄爻、官鬼，官吏多贪婪酷虐；落空，没有好官吏；生旺，带青龙、喜神、贵人、驿马、进神而发动的，多有善政传到上面，官员晋级、越级升迁；衰弱受伤，再加白虎、螣蛇、刃、劫杀、退神而发动的，多有贪污失德，被降级革职。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Dividing the offices (1), reading the people, scholars and officials by line. When Pressure sits on the first line, the common people suffer; if it is Void the people lose heart, if thriving and born the people are at peace and goods abundant, if weak and injured the people fall into misery. When Pressure sits on the second line, the scholars suffer; if Void the scholars are unwilling to attach themselves; if a Metal line is Void and moves the scholars voice many wild opinions; if thriving and with Wenchang added, literary fortune flourishes; if weak and injured the scholars are often suppressed. When the third line holds a Peer line or Pressure, the officials are often greedy and cruel; if Void there are no good officials; if thriving and carrying the Dragon, Joy, the Noble, the Horse and an Advancing spirit and moving, there is much good government reported upward and officials are advanced and promoted out of turn; if weak and injured, with the Tiger, the Coiling Snake, the blade, Robbery and a Retreating spirit added and moving, there is much corruption and officials are demoted and dismissed.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2375 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 初爻临鬼，黔首灾。 | 初爻临官鬼，百姓有灾。 | When Pressure sits on the first line, the common people suffer. |
| 空则民有离心也，生旺则民安物阜也，衰而受伤，则民遭涂炭也。 | 落空，百姓有离心；生旺，百姓安居、物产丰盛；衰弱又受伤，百姓遭涂炭。 | If it is Void, the people lose heart; if thriving and born, the people are at peace and goods abundant; if weak and injured, the people fall into misery. |
| 二爻临鬼，士子灾也。 | 二爻临官鬼，读书人有灾。 | When Pressure sits on the second line, the scholars suffer. |
| 空则士不豫附也。 | 落空，读书人不乐意归附。 | If it is Void, the scholars are unwilling to attach themselves. |
| 金空动则士多横议也。 | 金爻落空而发动，读书人多乱发议论。 | If a Metal line is Void and moves, the scholars voice many wild opinions. |
| 生旺加文昌，则文运盛。 | 生旺又加文昌，文运昌盛。 | If it is thriving and born with Wenchang added, literary fortune flourishes. |
| 衰而受伤，则士多屈抑也。 | 衰弱又受伤，读书人多受压抑。 | If it is weak and injured, the scholars are often suppressed. |
| 三爻持兄鬼，吏多贪酷。 | 三爻持兄爻、官鬼，官吏多贪婪酷虐。 | When the third line holds a Peer line or Pressure, the officials are often greedy and cruel. |
| 空则无良吏也。 | 落空，没有好官吏。 | If it is Void, there are no good officials. |
| 生旺带龙喜贵马进神动者，多善政上闻，晋秩超迁也。 | 生旺，带青龙、喜神、贵人、驿马、进神而发动的，多有善政传到上面，官员晋级、越级升迁。 | If it is thriving and born, carrying the Dragon, Joy, the Noble, the Horse and an Advancing spirit and moving, there is much good government reported upward, and officials are advanced and promoted out of turn. |
| 衰而受伤，加虎蛇刃劫退神动者，多簠簋不饰，贬秩褫职也。 | 衰弱又受伤，再加白虎、螣蛇、刃、劫杀、退神而发动的，多有贪污失德，被降级革职。 | If it is weak and injured, with the Tiger, the Coiling Snake, the blade, Robbery and a Retreating spirit added and moving, there is much corruption, and officials are demoted and dismissed. |

> **按**:「黔首」是百姓；「涂炭」是陷在泥和炭火里，比喻极苦的境地；「豫附」的「豫」是乐意；「横议」是肆意议论；「簠簋不饰」是官员贪污失德（簠簋是祭祀盛黍稷的器具，不修饰指官箴败坏）；「褫职」是革职。文昌是一颗主文运的神煞。「金空动」书里没有说「金」是金爻还是金宫，译文按金爻译。
>
> **Note:** 'Qianshou' is the common people; 'tutan' is being in mud and live coals, an image of utter misery; the 'yu' of 'yufu' means willing; 'hengyi' is voicing opinions recklessly; 'fugui bu shi' is an official's corruption and loss of virtue (the fugui are the vessels holding grain at sacrifices, and not adorning them points to the ruin of official conduct); 'chizhi' is dismissal from office. Wenchang is a spirit-star of literary fortune. The book does not say whether the 'Metal' in 'Metal Void moves' is a Metal line or a Metal palace, and the translation takes it as a Metal line.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.42、LY.CAREER.YY.44、LY.CAREER.HJC.65

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.44 朝廷占·分官（二）：四爻持鬼公卿多奸伪——空则鳏旷不称任，临子多忠义（子空动忠义挂冠），生旺带贵马龙德遇四直生扶老成镇重，休囚遇蛇虎鬼凶亡劫被四直刑害克破模棱伴食；六爻旺逢生合宗庙奠安，动衰受伤鬼神震恫王纲顿驰，空则飨祀不举朝觐失废

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:分官（二），四爻公卿和六爻宗庙：四爻持官鬼，公卿多奸诈虚伪；落空，职位空缺，不称职；临在子孙爻上，多忠义之臣；子孙爻落空而发动，忠义之臣挂冠而去；生旺，带贵人、驿马、青龙、德神，又得四直（年月日时）生扶的，老成持重，内外四方都畏服；休囚，遇螣蛇、白虎、官鬼、凶煞、亡神、劫杀，又被四直刑、害、克、破的，模棱两可、尸位素餐，朝野失望。（五爻是天子，见前。）六爻旺相又逢生合，宗庙安定，朝廷肃穆安宁；发动、衰弱、受伤，鬼神震动惊恐，王法纲纪顿时松弛；落空，祭祀不举行，朝觐的礼废弛。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Dividing the offices (2), the ministers of the fourth line and the ancestral temple of the sixth. When the fourth line holds Pressure, the ministers are often crafty and false; if Void, posts stand vacant and the holders are unequal to them; if it sits on Output, there are many loyal and righteous ministers; if Output is Void and moves, the loyal and righteous hang up their caps and leave; if thriving and born, carrying the Noble, the Horse, the Dragon and Virtue and supported by the four rulers, the elders are steady and weighty and the Chinese and foreign are awed; if resting or trapped, meeting the Coiling Snake, the Tiger, Pressure, baleful spirits, the Lost Spirit and Robbery and punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the four rulers, they are noncommittal and fill their seats, and court and country are disappointed. (The fifth line is the Son of Heaven, see above.) When the sixth line is thriving and meets generation or combination, the ancestral temple is secure and the court solemn and calm; if it moves, is weak or injured, the spirits are shaken and afraid and the royal rules and bonds suddenly slacken; if Void, sacrifices are not held and the rites of court audience fall into disuse.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2375 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四爻持鬼，公卿多奸伪。 | 四爻持官鬼，公卿多奸诈虚伪。 | When the fourth line holds Pressure, the ministers are often crafty and false. |
| 空则鳏旷，不称任也。 | 落空，职位空缺，不称职。 | If it is Void, posts stand vacant and the holders are unequal to them. |
| 临子则多忠义。 | 临在子孙爻上，多忠义之臣。 | If it sits on Output, there are many loyal and righteous ministers. |
| 子空动，则忠义挂冠也。 | 子孙爻落空而发动，忠义之臣挂冠而去。 | If Output is Void and moves, the loyal and righteous hang up their caps and leave. |
| 生旺，带贵马龙德遇四直生扶者，老成镇重，夷夏慑服也。 | 生旺，带贵人、驿马、青龙、德神，又得四直（年月日时）生扶的，老成持重，内外四方都畏服。 | If it is thriving and born, carrying the Noble, the Horse, the Dragon and Virtue and supported by the four rulers, the elders are steady and weighty and the Chinese and foreign are awed. |
| 休囚，遇蛇虎鬼凶亡劫，被四直刑害克破者，模稜伴食，朝野失望也。 | 休囚，遇螣蛇、白虎、官鬼、凶煞、亡神、劫杀，又被四直刑、害、克、破的，模棱两可、尸位素餐，朝野失望。 | If it is resting or trapped, meeting the Coiling Snake, the Tiger, Pressure, baleful spirits, the Lost Spirit and Robbery, and punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the four rulers, they are noncommittal and fill their seats without doing anything, and court and country are disappointed. |
| (五爻天子，见前)。 | （五爻是天子，见前。） | (The fifth line is the Son of Heaven, see above.) |
| 六爻旺逢生合，则宗庙奠安，朝廷肃靖也。 | 六爻旺相又逢生合，宗庙安定，朝廷肃穆安宁。 | When the sixth line is thriving and meets generation or combination, the ancestral temple is secure and the court solemn and calm. |
| 动衰受伤，则鬼神震恫，王纲顿驰也。 | 发动、衰弱、受伤，鬼神震动惊恐，王法纲纪顿时松弛。 | If it moves, is weak or injured, the spirits are shaken and afraid and the royal rules and bonds suddenly slacken. |
| 空则饗祀不举，朝观失废也。 | 落空，祭祀不举行，朝觐的礼废弛。 | If it is Void, sacrifices are not held and the rites of court audience fall into disuse. |

> **按**:「鳏旷」是官位空缺；「挂冠」是辞官；「夷夏」是外族和中原；「伴食」是陪着吃饭，指官员不干事、只占位置（尸位素餐）；「饗祀」即飨祀，祭祀；「朝觐」原文作「朝观」。「子空动」按字面是子孙落空而发动。书里「临子」「子空动」的「子」都按子孙爻译。
>
> **Note:** 'Guankuang' is a vacancy in office; 'guaguan' is hanging up one's cap, resigning; 'yixia' is the foreign and the Chinese; 'banshi' is accompanying at the meal, an official who does nothing and only occupies a seat; 'xiangsi' is sacrifice; 'chaoguan' is the text's wording for the court audience. In 'on Zi' and 'Zi Void and moving', the 'Zi' is taken as the Output line.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.43、LY.CAREER.YY.45、LY.CAREER.HJC.65

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.45 朝廷占·万金赋穿断法（一）：五动生初君忧民，三动生初吏恤民，四动生初相动民，初爻生五民戴君，初合三四民服官，三四五带虎杀动克初民受害，初冲克三四五民胥谗

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《万金赋》穿断法，看君、官、民：五爻发动生初爻，君主忧虑百姓；三爻发动生初爻，官吏体恤百姓；四爻发动生初爻，宰相打动百姓；初爻生五爻，百姓拥戴君主；初爻合三爻、四爻，百姓服从官员；三爻、四爻、五爻带白虎、凶煞发动克初爻的，百姓受害；初爻冲克三爻、四爻、五爻的，百姓一同进谗毁谤。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Chuanduan method of the Wanjin Fu, reading ruler, officials and people. If the fifth line moves to generate the first, the sovereign worries for the people; if the third line moves to generate the first, the officials care for the people; if the fourth moves to generate the first, the minister moves the people; if the first generates the fifth, the people support the sovereign; if the first combines with the third and fourth, the people submit to the officials; if the third, fourth or fifth line carrying the Tiger and baleful spirits moves to control the first, the people suffer harm; if the first clashes with or controls the third, fourth or fifth, the people all slander.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2375 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 万金赋穿断法曰。 | 《万金赋》的穿断法说。 | The Chuanduan method of the Wanjin Fu says. |
| 五动生初，君忧民也。 | 五爻发动生初爻，君主忧虑百姓。 | If the fifth line moves to generate the first, the sovereign worries for the people. |
| 三动生初，吏恤民也。 | 三爻发动生初爻，官吏体恤百姓。 | If the third line moves to generate the first, the officials care for the people. |
| 四动生初，相动民也。 | 四爻发动生初爻，宰相打动百姓。 | If the fourth line moves to generate the first, the minister moves the people. |
| 初爻生五，民戴君也。 | 初爻生五爻，百姓拥戴君主。 | If the first line generates the fifth, the people support the sovereign. |
| 初合三四，民服官也。 | 初爻合三爻、四爻，百姓服从官员。 | If the first line combines with the third and fourth, the people submit to the officials. |
| 三四五带虎杀动克初者，民受害也。 | 三爻、四爻、五爻带白虎、凶煞发动克初爻的，百姓受害。 | If the third, fourth or fifth line carrying the Tiger and baleful spirits moves to control the first, the people suffer harm. |
| 初冲克三四五者，民胥谗也。 | 初爻冲克三爻、四爻、五爻的，百姓一同进谗毁谤。 | If the first line clashes with or controls the third, fourth or fifth, the people all slander. |

> **按**:这一段是《易隐》引的《万金赋》里的「穿断法」，书里没有说明「穿断」的意思，译文按音译，不解释。「相动民」按字面是宰相打动百姓，「民胥谗」按字面是百姓都在进谗毁谤，两处文意都不顺，译文照字面，不替它改。
>
> **Note:** This passage is the 'chuanduan' method of the Wanjin Fu as quoted in the Yi Yin, and the book does not say what 'chuanduan' means; the translation gives its sound and does not explain it. 'The minister moves the people' and 'the people all slander' are by the letter, and in both the sense does not run smoothly; the translation follows the letter and does not alter it.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.44、LY.CAREER.YY.46

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.46 朝廷占·万金赋穿断法（二）：五生合二君礼士，三四生合二臣下贤，三四合二生五臣荐贤于上，二生三四上景从，二临父克五书生进牍，四生克三大臣能任百官，三持财动生世下赂上，五生合三奖良吏，五生合四委心执政，四生合五摅诚报主

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《万金赋》穿断法，看君、士、臣：五爻生合二爻，君主礼敬士人；三爻、四爻生合二爻，臣下贤明；三爻、四爻合二爻又生五爻的，臣子向君主举荐贤才；二爻生三爻、四爻的，在上的人如影随形地追随；二爻临父母爻克五爻的，书生上书进奏；四爻生克三爻的，大臣能统率百官，提拔贤才、罢黜奸邪；三爻持财爻发动生世爻的，下面的人贿赂上面；五爻生合三爻，君主奖励、资助良吏；五爻生合四爻，君主把心思托付给执政大臣；四爻生合五爻，大臣竭诚报效君主。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Chuanduan method, reading sovereign, scholars and ministers. If the fifth line generates or combines with the second, the sovereign honours the scholars; if the third and fourth generate or combine with the second, the ministers are worthy; if the third and fourth combine with the second and generate the fifth, the ministers recommend the worthy to the sovereign; if the second generates the third and fourth, those above follow like a shadow; if the second sits on Resource and controls the fifth, a scholar presents a memorial; if the fourth generates or controls the third, the great minister can direct the hundred officials, promoting the worthy and dismissing the wicked; if the third holds Wealth and moves to generate the Self, those below bribe those above; if the fifth generates or combines with the third, the sovereign rewards and supports good officials; if the fifth generates or combines with the fourth, the sovereign entrusts his mind to the minister in charge; if the fourth generates or combines with the fifth, the minister gives his sincerity to repay the sovereign.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2375 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 五生合二者，君礼士也。 | 五爻生合二爻，君主礼敬士人。 | If the fifth line generates or combines with the second, the sovereign honours the scholars. |
| 三四生合二者，臣下贤也。 | 三爻、四爻生合二爻，臣下贤明。 | If the third and fourth lines generate or combine with the second, the ministers are worthy. |
| 三四合二生五者，臣荐贤于上也。 | 三爻、四爻合二爻又生五爻的，臣子向君主举荐贤才。 | If the third and fourth lines combine with the second and generate the fifth, the ministers recommend the worthy to the sovereign. |
| 二生三四者，上景从也。 | 二爻生三爻、四爻的，在上的人如影随形地追随。 | If the second line generates the third and fourth, those above follow like a shadow. |
| 二临父克五者，书生进牍也。 | 二爻临父母爻克五爻的，书生上书进奏。 | If the second line sits on Resource and controls the fifth, a scholar presents a memorial. |
| 四生克三者，大臣能任百官，陟贤黜奸也。 | 四爻生克三爻的，大臣能统率百官，提拔贤才、罢黜奸邪。 | If the fourth line generates or controls the third, the great minister can direct the hundred officials, promoting the worthy and dismissing the wicked. |
| 三持财动生世者，下赂上也。 | 三爻持财爻发动生世爻的，下面的人贿赂上面。 | If the third line holds Wealth and moves to generate the Self, those below bribe those above. |
| 五生合三者，人主奖资良吏也。 | 五爻生合三爻，君主奖励、资助良吏。 | If the fifth line generates or combines with the third, the sovereign rewards and supports good officials. |
| 五生合四者，委心执政也。 | 五爻生合四爻，君主把心思托付给执政大臣。 | If the fifth line generates or combines with the fourth, the sovereign entrusts his mind to the minister in charge. |
| 四生合五者，摅诚报主也。 | 四爻生合五爻，大臣竭诚报效君主。 | If the fourth line generates or combines with the fifth, the minister gives his sincerity to repay the sovereign. |

> **按**:「上景从」按字面是在上的人像影子一样跟从，书里没有说谁追随谁，译文照字面。「进牍」是呈上奏章。「摅诚」是尽情抒发诚意。「四生克三」可以读成四爻生或克三爻，书里没有分开，译文也不分开。
>
> **Note:** 'Shang jing cong' is by the letter those above following like a shadow; the book does not say who follows whom, and the translation follows the letter. 'Jindu' is presenting a memorial. 'Shucheng' is pouring out one's sincerity. 'The fourth generates or controls the third' can be read either as generating or as controlling; the book does not separate them and the translation does not either.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.45、LY.CAREER.YY.47、LY.CAREER.HJC.72

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.47 朝廷占·万金赋穿断法（三）：四持蛇虎兄鬼亡劫刑刃五动克之黜远奸佞，四持凶杀五动生合之信任谗邪，四持金虎鬼杀动克五大臣谋主（得日辰动爻合制庶无大咎）；四带玄咸生合五阿谀取容；五持父动克四忠言逆耳；四加福德将星被五刑害克破良将冤死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《万金赋》穿断法，看奸佞和忠良：四爻持螣蛇、白虎、兄弟、官鬼、亡神、劫杀、刑、刃，而五爻发动克它的，君主罢黜、远离奸佞；四爻持凶煞，而五爻发动生合它的，君主信任谗佞邪恶之人；四爻持金爻、白虎、官鬼、凶煞，发动克五爻的，大臣谋害君主，得日辰、动爻合住或制住它，大概没有大的祸害；四爻带玄武、咸池，生合五爻的，阿谀逢迎取悦君主；五爻持父母爻发动克四爻的，忠言逆耳；四爻加福德、将星，被五爻刑、害、克、破的，良将冤死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Chuanduan method, reading traitors and the loyal. If the fourth line holds the Coiling Snake, the Tiger, a Peer line, Pressure, the Lost Spirit, Robbery, punishment and the blade, and the fifth moves to control it, the sovereign dismisses and keeps away the flatterers; if the fourth holds baleful spirits and the fifth moves to generate or combine with it, the sovereign trusts slanderers and the wicked; if the fourth holds Metal, the Tiger, Pressure and baleful spirits and moves to control the fifth, the great minister plots against the sovereign, and if the Day or a moving line combines with or restrains it, perhaps there is no great harm; if the fourth carries the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi and generates or combines with the fifth, it is flattery to win favour; if the fifth holds Resource and moves to control the fourth, loyal words grate on the ear; if the fourth adds Output and the General star and is punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the fifth, a good general dies wronged.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2375 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 四持蛇虎兄鬼亡劫刑刃，而五动克之者，黜远奸佞也。 | 四爻持螣蛇、白虎、兄弟、官鬼、亡神、劫杀、刑、刃，而五爻发动克它的，君主罢黜、远离奸佞。 | If the fourth line holds the Coiling Snake, the Tiger, a Peer line, Pressure, the Lost Spirit, Robbery, punishment and the blade, and the fifth moves to control it, the sovereign dismisses and keeps away the flatterers. |
| 四持凶杀而五动生合之者，信任谗邪也。 | 四爻持凶煞，而五爻发动生合它的，君主信任谗佞邪恶之人。 | If the fourth line holds baleful spirits and the fifth moves to generate or combine with it, the sovereign trusts slanderers and the wicked. |
| 四持金虎鬼杀，动克五者，大臣谋主也。 | 四爻持金爻、白虎、官鬼、凶煞，发动克五爻的，大臣谋害君主。 | If the fourth line holds Metal, the Tiger, Pressure and baleful spirits and moves to control the fifth, the great minister plots against the sovereign. |
| 得日辰动爻合制之，庶无大咎。 | 得日辰、动爻合住或制住它，大概没有大的祸害。 | If the Day or a moving line combines with or restrains it, perhaps there is no great harm. |
| 四带玄咸生合五，阿谀取容也。 | 四爻带玄武、咸池，生合五爻的，阿谀逢迎取悦君主。 | If the fourth line carries the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi and generates or combines with the fifth, it is flattery to win favour. |
| 五持父动克四者，忠言逆耳也。 | 五爻持父母爻发动克四爻的，忠言逆耳。 | If the fifth line holds Resource and moves to control the fourth, loyal words grate on the ear. |
| 四加福德将星，被五爻刑害克破者，良将冤死也。 | 四爻加福德、将星，被五爻刑、害、克、破的，良将冤死。 | If the fourth line adds Output and the General star and is punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the fifth, a good general dies wronged. |

> **按**:「福德」是福德，即子孙（见术语表）；「将星」是主将帅的神煞。「玄咸」是玄武和咸池的省称。第三、四句「四持金虎鬼杀，动克五者，大臣谋主也。得日辰动爻合制之，庶无大咎」是连着的，所以放在一条里。
>
> **Note:** 'Fude' is the fortune virtue, that is Output (see the glossary); the 'General star' is the spirit-star of commanders. 'Xuan Xian' is an abbreviation for the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi. The third and fourth sentences, 'the great minister plots against the sovereign' and 'if the Day or a moving line combines with or restrains it, perhaps there is no great harm', run on from each other and are therefore placed in one entry.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.46、LY.CAREER.YY.48、LY.CAREER.HJC.72、LY.CAREER.HJC.73、LY.CAREER.HJC.82、LY.CAREER.HJC.83

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.48 朝廷占·爻象（鄱阳汪所性占例）：比屯豫利封建，涣萃利告庙，益随利郊天，升利封禅，晋宜受朝觐，屯豫利立君，豫既济师离谦利征伐，乾利即位，师解巽利田猎，比屯恒不利田猎，涣利发号施令，革利改朔，夬利去小人，师利赏战功，家人宜纳后，归妹宜嫁妹，蛊利求嗣，井利改邑，剥遁宜选宫嫔寺人，鼎利正位号，姤巽宜颁抚恤之诏，大有丰泰天下平，否国步艰，大过国祚危

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:什么卦利于做国家的什么事（鄱阳汪所性的占例）：比、屯、豫利于分封建国；涣、萃利于祭告宗庙；益、随利于郊祭天；升利于封禅；晋适宜接受朝觐；屯、豫利于立君；豫、既济、师、离、谦利于出兵征伐；乾利于即位；师、解、巽利于田猎，比、屯、恒不利于田猎；涣利于发号施令；革利于改定历法；夬利于除去小人；师利于赏战功；家人适宜纳后；归妹适宜嫁妹；蛊利于求嗣；井利于迁改城邑；剥、遁适宜选宫嫔、宦官；鼎利于确定名位称号；姤、巽适宜颁布抚恤的诏书；大有、丰、泰，天下太平；否，国运艰难；大过，国祚危险。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Which hexagram favours which act of state (the divination examples of Wang Suoxing of Poyang). Bi, Zhun and Yu favour enfeoffment; Huan and Cui favour reporting to the ancestral temple; Yi and Sui favour the suburban sacrifice to Heaven; Sheng favours the Feng and Shan sacrifices; Jin is fit for receiving court audiences; Zhun and Yu favour setting up a ruler; Yu, Jiji, Shi, Li and Qian (Modesty) favour military campaigns; Qian (Heaven) favours accession; Shi, Xie and Xun favour hunting, while Bi, Zhun and Heng are unfavourable for it; Huan favours issuing proclamations and orders; Ge favours changing the calendar; Guai favours removing petty men; Shi favours rewarding military merit; Jiaren is fit for taking an empress; Guimei is fit for marrying off a sister; Gu favours seeking an heir; Jing favours moving a city; Bo and Dun are fit for selecting palace consorts and eunuchs; Ding favours settling titles and ranks; Gou and Xun are fit for issuing edicts of relief; Dayou, Feng and Tai mean the world at peace; Pi means the state's course is hard; Daguo means the dynasty is in danger.

**《易隐·朝廷占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2379 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining for the court", base text; Yi Yin, line 2379*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鄱阳汪所性占例曰。 | 鄱阳汪所性的占例说： | Wang Suoxing of Poyang says in his divination examples: |
| 比屯豫利封建也， | 比、屯、豫三卦，利于分封建国。 | Bi, Zhun and Yu favour enfeoffment. |
| 涣萃利告庙也， | 涣、萃两卦，利于祭告宗庙。 | Huan and Cui favour reporting to the ancestral temple. |
| 益随利郊天也， | 益、随两卦，利于郊祭天。 | Yi and Sui favour the suburban sacrifice to Heaven. |
| 升利封禅也， | 升卦利于封禅。 | Sheng favours the Feng and Shan sacrifices. |
| 晋宜受朝观也， | 晋卦适宜接受朝觐。 | Jin is fit for receiving court audiences. |
| 屯豫利立君也， | 屯、豫两卦，利于立君。 | Zhun and Yu favour setting up a ruler. |
| 豫既济师离谦利征伐也， | 豫、既济、师、离、谦，利于出兵征伐。 | Yu, Jiji, Shi, Li and Qian (Modesty) favour military campaigns. |
| 乾利即位也， | 乾卦利于即位。 | Qian (Heaven) favours accession to the throne. |
| 师解巽利田猎也， | 师、解、巽，利于田猎。 | Shi, Xie and Xun favour hunting. |
| 比屯恒不利田猎也， | 比、屯、恒，不利于田猎。 | Bi, Zhun and Heng are unfavourable for hunting. |
| 涣利发号施令也， | 涣卦利于发号施令。 | Huan favours issuing proclamations and orders. |
| 革利改朔也， | 革卦利于改定历法。 | Ge favours changing the calendar. |
| 夬利去小人也， | 夬卦利于除去小人。 | Guai favours removing petty men. |
| 师利赏战功也， | 师卦利于赏战功。 | Shi favours rewarding military merit. |
| 家人宜纳后也， | 家人卦适宜纳后（立皇后）。 | Jiaren is fit for taking an empress. |
| 归妹宜嫁妹也， | 归妹卦适宜嫁妹。 | Guimei is fit for marrying off a sister. |
| 蛊利求嗣， | 蛊卦利于求嗣（求继承人）。 | Gu favours seeking an heir. |
| 井利改邑也， | 井卦利于迁改城邑。 | Jing favours moving a city. |
| 剥遁宜选宫嫔寺人也， | 剥、遁两卦，适宜选宫嫔和宦官。 | Bo and Dun are fit for selecting palace consorts and eunuchs. |
| 鼎利正位号也， | 鼎卦利于确定名位称号。 | Ding favours settling titles and ranks. |
| 姤巽宜颁抚恤之诏也， | 姤、巽两卦，适宜颁布抚恤的诏书。 | Gou and Xun are fit for issuing edicts of relief. |
| 大有丰泰天下平也， | 大有、丰、泰，天下太平。 | Dayou, Feng and Tai mean the world is at peace. |
| 否国步艰也， | 否卦，国运艰难。 | Pi means the state's course is hard. |
| 大过国祚危也。 | 大过卦，国祚危险。 | Daguo means the dynasty is in danger. |

> **按**:这一段是《易隐》引的鄱阳汪所性的「占例」，是一张「哪一卦适宜办哪一件国事」的清单，没有给理由。「谦」读 qian，与乾同音，译文写成 Qian （Modesty） 和 Qian （Heaven） 来分。「封禅」是帝王到泰山祭天地，「改朔」是改定历法（换正月），「嫁妹」按字面是出嫁妹妹，「抚恤之诏」是抚慰救济灾民的诏书，「国步」是国运，「国祚」是国运的年数。「朝观」原文作「朝观」，按文意是朝觐。
>
> **Note:** This passage is the 'divination examples' of Wang Suoxing of Poyang as quoted in the Yi Yin, a list of which hexagram is fit for which affair of state, giving no reasons. 'Qian', modesty, is pronounced as Qian, heaven, and the translation writes Qian (Modesty) and Qian (Heaven) to tell them apart. 'Fengshan' is the emperor's sacrifice to Heaven and Earth at Mount Tai, 'gai shuo' is fixing a new calendar (changing the first month), 'jia mei' is by the letter marrying off a younger sister, 'the edict of relief' is an edict comforting and relieving disaster victims, 'guobu' is the course of the state, and 'guozuo' is the span of the dynasty. The text writes 'chaoguan' where by sense it is the court audience.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.47、LY.CAREER.YY.49
